Ideas for my research: 借鉴价值极低。唯一可能间接参考的点是：Action-CoT中的"path-conditioned dual-query training scheme"这种结构化训练策略思路，如果未来在遥感在轨检测中需要引入时序推理或闭环校正机制，可参考其双向通信设计。但整体而言与本课题方向差异过大。

Abstract: Existing vision-language navigation methods often couple a VLM with waypoint decoders to produce multi-step action plans, but they typically lack an explicit closed-loop mechanism for tracking semantic progress, diagnosing execution failures, and recovering from error accumulation in long-horizon navigation. To address this gap, we propose ReflectVLN, an agentic VLN framework that organizes decision-making through bidirectionally interactive intention and execution agents. The intention agent performs subtask decomposition and reflection, generating executable subtask descriptions as corrective plans. Conditioned on these descriptions, the execution agent grounds them into short-horizon actions under current observations while monitoring sub-goal progress and detecting off-track behavior. Crucially, ReflectVLN enables closed-loop bidirectional communication: the execution agent emits progress and deviation signals to trigger reflection and subtask updates on demand, and the intention agent returns structured guidance that reconditions subsequent actions for recovery. To encourage temporally coherent decisions with interpretable intermediate rationales, we introduce Action Chain-of-Thought (Action-CoT), a path-conditioned dual-query training scheme for action generation. Experiments on standard VLN benchmarks show that ReflectVLN improves success rates and path efficiency under a constrained data budget, with favorable training cost and fewer high-level intention calls at inference time, while providing interpretable intermediate decisions for analysis and collaboration. Code is available at: https://github.com/AIprogrammer/ReflectVLN

</details>


### [66] [MambaPSA: A Mamba-based Replacement for C2PSA in YOLO26](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12681)
*Sheng-Wei Chan,Chia-Min Lin,Hsin-Jui Pan,Ching-Yu Tsai,Chih-Hsiang Yang,Yung-Che Wang,Jen-Shiun Chiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MambaPSA，用状态空间模型（Mamba）替换YOLO26中C2PSA注意力模块，实现轻量化且无NMS的目标检测，在PASCAL VOC上参数量减少2.9%、FLOPs降低12.1%、CPU吞吐提升17.6%。

Research relevance: 2分——弱相关。该论文涉及目标检测和轻量化（Mamba替换注意力模块），但**完全不涉及遥感/卫星/在轨场景**，数据集为PASCAL VOC（通用自然图像），无任何遥感相关实验。轻量化方法（SSM替换注意力）对遥感轻量化有间接借鉴意义，但非直接相关。

Reading priority: 2分——弱相关，阅读优先级较低。方法（SSM替换注意力）有轻量化借鉴意义，但论文完全聚焦通用检测且轻量化幅度有限（-2.9%参数量）。建议仅作为SSM在检测中应用的参考，不作为遥感轻量化的核心文献。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：通用目标检测（object detection）；应用场景：通用视觉检测，非遥感场景；传感器类型：自然图像（RGB）；目标类别：PASCAL VOC 20类（飞机、车等通用目标，但非遥感专用）。

Model architecture: 基模型：YOLO26（最新NMS-free检测框架）；改进点：(1) MambaPSA——用Mamba（SSM）替换backbone末端的C2PSA注意力模块；(2) BiViM（bidirectional Vision Mamba）——插入neck的P3/P4/P5层级。

Lightweight method: 轻量化方法：用状态空间模型（SSM/Mamba）替换注意力模块（C2PSA），利用SSM的线性计算复杂度实现轻量化。未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等传统轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明星上/边缘部署相关信息。仅报告了CPU推理吞吐（17→20 FPS，提升17.6%），未报告GPU延迟、显存、能耗、NPU/FPGA/TensorRT适配性。参数量减少2.9%，FLOPs降低12.1%，但未给出绝对数值。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：PASCAL VOC 2007+2012（通用自然图像，非遥感）；评价指标：mAP50:95（COCO-style平均精度）。

Experiments: 主要结果：(1) MambaPSA替换C2PSA：参数量-2.9%，FLOPs-12.1%，CPU FPS 17→20（+17.6%），mAP50:95 -0.1%（几乎无损）；(2) P4层插入BiViM：mAP50:95 +0.9%（精度增益最大）。消融实验：对比了不同层级（P3/P4/P5）插入BiViM的效果。

Limitations: (1) 仅在通用自然图像（PASCAL VOC）上验证，未在遥感数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR、NWPU VHR-10等）上测试；(2) 未报告GPU推理速度、参数量/FLOPs绝对值、模型大小（MB）；(3) 未涉及旋转框检测（遥感常见需求）；(4) 未讨论星上部署约束（功耗、存储、实时性）；(5) 仅替换一个模块，整体轻量化幅度有限（参数量仅-2.9%）。

Ideas for my research: (1) **SSM/Mamba用于遥感轻量化检测**：可尝试将MambaPSA或BiViM迁移到遥感检测模型（如YOLO遥感变体）中，替换注意力模块以降低FLOPs；(2) **NMS-free框架**：YOLO26无NMS的设计对遥感密集小目标场景（如舰船、车辆）有潜在优势，可减少后处理延迟；(3) **双向Mamba（BiViM）**：双向SSM可能更适合遥感图像中目标的上下文建模，值得在遥感骨干或neck中尝试；(4) 需在遥感数据集（DOTA、HRSC2016、ShipRSImageNet等）上重新验证，并补充旋转框检测实验。

Abstract: State space models (SSMs), notably Mamba, have recently emerged as efficient alternatives to self-attention with linear computational complexity. We investigate the integration of Mamba into YOLO26, the latest non-maximum suppression (NMS)-free object detection framework, by proposing MambaPSA, a lightweight Mamba-based replacement for the C2PSA block at the end of the backbone. To complement this study, we additionally insert a bidirectional Vision Mamba (BiViM) module at the P3, P4, and P5 levels of the neck. Experiments on PASCAL VOC 2007+2012 show that MambaPSA reduces parameters by 2.9%, FLOPs by 12.1%, and improves CPU inference throughput by 17.6% (from 17 to 20 FPS) with negligible accuracy change (-0.1 mAP50:95), while the P4 BiViM placement yields the best accuracy gain (+0.9 mAP50:95). These results suggest that SSMs offer a favorable efficiency-accuracy trade-off when replacing attention-based blocks in NMS-free lightweight detectors.

</details>


### [67] [Lesion Segmentation in Moderate to Severe Traumatic Brain Injury: An nnU-Net Based Approach with Adaptive Normalization in the AIMS-TBI 2025 Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12684)
*Inhwa Son,Gaeun Lee,Sohyeon Sim,Kwang-Hyun Uhm*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 基于nnU-Net框架结合脑实质自适应强度归一化策略，用于中重度创伤性脑损伤病灶分割的医学影像方法。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文处理的是医学MRI图像中的脑损伤病灶分割任务，完全不涉及遥感/卫星图像、目标检测或模型轻量化/部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：医学图像语义分割（病灶分割）；场景：中重度创伤性脑损伤（msTBI）临床诊断；传感器：T1-weighted MRI（磁共振成像）；目标类别：脑损伤病灶（lesion）与非病灶组织（non-lesion tissue）。

Model architecture: nnU-Net框架，自适应强度归一化策略（confined to brain parenchyma）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作面向医学影像分析，不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：AIMS-TBI 2025 Challenge提供的msTBI T1-weighted MRI数据集；评价指标：Overall Dice Coefficient（0.6305）、Lesion Dice（0.4805）、Non-lesion Dice（0.9324）。

Experiments: 在AIMS-TBI 2025 Challenge的held-out测试集上评估，方法在官方排行榜上表现具有竞争力。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关；2. 病灶分割Dice仅0.4805，说明高度异质性病灶检测仍具挑战；3. 未提供模型复杂度、推理速度等工程部署信息。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，可借鉴点有限。nnU-Net的自适应归一化策略思路（针对特定区域进行归一化以减少域间差异）在遥感图像中可能有类似应用（如针对地物区域做归一化），但整体方向差异过大，不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: The segmentation of lesions in Moderate to Severe Traumatic Brain Injury (msTBI) from T1-weighted MRI presents a significant clinical challenge due to the profound heterogeneity of lesion characteristics in terms of size, shape, and location. To address this, the AIMS-TBI 2025 Challenge was organized to promote the development of robust and accurate segmentation algorithms. In this paper, we present our deep learning-based solution. Our methodology employs the nnU-Net framework with an adaptive intensity normalization strategy confined to the brain parenchyma, effectively reducing inter-subject variability and mitigating artifacts from non-brain structures. Upon final evaluation on the held-out test set, our method demonstrated highly competitive performance on the official leaderboard, achieving an Overall Dice Coefficient of 0.6305. The model obtained a Dice score of 0.4805 for lesion segmentation and 0.9324 for non-lesion tissue. While the lesion Dice reflects the difficulty of detecting highly heterogeneous lesions, the high non-lesion Dice primarily indicates the model's strong ability to correctly identify non-lesion voxels, demonstrating good specificity in differentiating lesion from non-lesion regions. These results demonstrate that incorporating anatomically constrained normalization within the nnU-Net pipeline is a powerful and effective strategy for tackling the complexities of msTBI lesion segmentation.

</details>


### [68] [Label-Decoupled Style Augmentation for Domain Generalization in Multi-Label Remote Sensing Scene Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12704)
*Alaa Almouradi,Erchan Aptoula*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种标签解耦的风格增强框架，用于多标签遥感场景的域泛化，通过逐标签注意力机制将风格扰动限制在特定标签区域，避免全局统计增强中类别间污染问题。

Research relevance: 2分 — 弱相关。论文涉及遥感场景（aerial scene）和多标签分类，但**不涉及目标检测**（object detection），也不涉及轻量化/在轨部署。属于遥感场景理解中的域泛化问题，与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"方向仅存在遥感场景的弱关联。

Reading priority: 2分 — 与本研究方向弱相关。仅因遥感场景有弱关联，但任务类型（多标签分类）和核心关注点（域泛化）均与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"方向不匹配，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：多标签分类（multi-label classification）；场景：遥感航拍场景（aerial scene）；传感器类型：光学遥感图像（UCM, AID, DFC15数据集）；目标类别：6个共享标签（具体类别摘要未说明）。

Model architecture: 框架基于标签解耦的注意力机制：1）逐标签注意力（per-label attention）来自可学习模块或梯度类激活图（Grad-CAM）；2）逐标签特征统计量（per-label feature statistics）与跨域样本混合；3）通过注意力加权归一化（attention-weighted normalization）重组特征。与MixStyle、EFDMix等全局统计增强方法结合使用。

Lightweight method: 不涉及轻量化方法。论文提到"增加最多0.35%参数量，推理阶段不变"，但这只是说明其方法的低开销特性，并非模型压缩/加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到"增加最多0.35%参数量，推理阶段不变"，但未给出基线模型的参数量绝对值。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：UCM（UC Merced）、AID、DFC15三个多标签遥感数据集，构建leave-one-domain-out跨域基准；评价指标：mean Average Precision (mAP)；实验设置：3个数据划分×5个随机种子。

Experiments: 最佳变体平均mAP 71.5%，超过经验风险最小化（ERM）5.0个百分点，超过最强全局统计基线1.3个百分点，在最难迁移任务上提升达7.7个百分点。消融实验表明空间注意力和刷新定位图（refreshed localization maps）影响最大。

Limitations: 1）不涉及目标检测，仅针对多标签分类任务；2）不涉及任何轻量化技术（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）；3）不涉及在轨/星上部署场景；4）仅在光学遥感图像上验证，未涉及SAR、高光谱、多光谱等其他遥感模态；5）未报告模型速度、参数量绝对值、FLOPs等部署关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 1）标签解耦的域泛化思路可迁移到遥感目标检测的域适应问题中，例如不同卫星/传感器间的检测模型泛化；2）逐区域特征统计增强的思想可用于小目标检测中，避免背景噪声干扰目标区域的特征增强；3）低开销（0.35%参数增加）的模块设计思路值得借鉴，但需结合目标检测任务重新设计；4）整体借鉴价值有限，因为核心任务（分类vs检测）和核心关注点（域泛化vs轻量化）均不同。

Abstract: Multi-label classification assigns several co-occurring labels to each aerial scene, yet deployed models often encounter data distributions different from their training. Feature-statistics augmentation such as MixStyle, EFDMix, and correlated style uncertainty improves generalization at low cost but perturbs channel statistics globally, treating each image as a single style; one class can then contaminate the augmentation of another. Domain generalization is understudied for multi-label remote sensing; no prior method or multi-source benchmark targets it. A label-decoupled augmentation framework is therefore proposed, confining style perturbation to label-specific regions. Per-label attention, obtained from a learnable module or from gradient class-activation maps, yields per-label feature statistics; these statistics are mixed with cross-domain samples that share present labels, under independent per-label coefficients, and features are recomposed by attention-weighted normalization. Three operators combined with two attention sources produce six variants, evaluated on a leave-one-domain-out benchmark from multi-label UCM, AID, and DFC15 over six shared labels. Averaged over three splits and five seeds, the best variant attains 71.5% mean average precision, exceeding empirical risk minimization by 5.0 points and the strongest global-statistics baseline by 1.3 points, with the largest gain on the hardest transfer (up to 7.7 points). Ablations indicate that spatial attention and refreshed localization maps are most influential. The framework adds at most 0.35% parameters, leaves inference unchanged, and appears to offer a generic, inexpensive upgrade path for multi-label statistics-based domain generalization. Code is available upon acceptance at https://github.com/Alaa-Almouradi/Style-Augmentation-Upgrade.

</details>


### [69] [Color Pass-Through via Camera-Display Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12746)
*Ruikang Li,Molin Li,Jiarui Wu,Zhe Wei,Pengpeng Liu,Tianfan Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Color Pass-Through，一种端到端学习框架，将相机和显示器作为耦合系统进行联合校准，以改善手机拍摄到显示的色彩还原效果。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。论文研究的是智能手机拍摄-显示管线的色彩校准问题，属于计算摄影/图像处理领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测或任何模型压缩/加速技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：色彩校准与色彩还原（color calibration/pass-through）；场景：智能手机拍摄到屏幕显示管线；传感器类型：智能手机相机；目标类别：无目标检测任务。

Model architecture: 端到端学习框架（end-to-end learned framework），将相机和显示器作为耦合系统处理。摘要未说明具体网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。摘要未提及任何剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 使用数字观察者（digital observers）和人类观察者（human observers）进行验证；定量指标和5分制用户研究（5-point user study）。摘要未说明具体数据集名称。

Experiments: 相比代表性基线方法，在用户研究中平均提升+2.0分，定量指标提升超过2倍。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或目标检测技术信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于计算摄影/色彩科学领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题的研究范畴无交集。

Abstract: When a real-world scene is captured by a smartphone camera and viewed on its screen, the displayed image often differs noticeably from the original scene in color, brightness, and contrast. This gap persists despite substantial advances in both modern cameras and displays. A key reason is that most pipelines factor the high-dimensional capture-to-display process into two separately calibrated camera and display stages, and then connect them through low-dimensional color transforms, leading to information bottlenecks and inevitable error accumulation. To address this systemic challenge, we propose Color Pass-Through, an end-to-end learned framework that operates directly on captured images. Our key insight is to treat the camera and display as a coupled system rather than calibrating them in isolation. Coupling the camera and display yields two practical advantages: (1) it brings the entire real-world scenes to the display via end-to-end optimization, and (2) it allows efficient one-step calibration for each distinct observer via complete capture-to-display path. We validate Color Pass-Through using both digital and human observers. Compared with representative baselines, our method achieves an average gain of +2.0 points on a 5-point user study and more than 2x improvement on quantitative metrics, demonstrating improved reproduction of the perceived color of the original scene.

</details>


### [70] [Weakly Supervised Spatio-Temporal Candidate Discovery of Dairy Farm Sites from Seasonal Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12748)
*Usman Haider,Fatima Khalid,Karl Mason*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种弱监督管道，利用Barlow Twins编码器学习多季节Sentinel影像嵌入，结合OpenStreetMap先验和规则评分，从卫星图像中排序发现奶牛场候选集群。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。虽然使用了遥感卫星影像（Sentinel），但任务不是目标检测，而是农场候选点排序/发现；不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏等）；不涉及在轨部署或实时推理。属于遥感图像中的弱监督场景理解/检索任务，与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"方向无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不涉及目标检测任务，不涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法，不涉及在轨部署。仅作为遥感弱监督场景理解的参考，阅读优先级极低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：弱监督农场候选点排序与发现（spatiotemporal candidate ranking）；应用场景：农业遥感、奶牛场识别；传感器类型：Sentinel多光谱卫星影像（春季、夏季、秋季）；目标类别：奶牛场（dairy farm）；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测。

Model architecture: Barlow Twins编码器（自监督对比学习），用于学习多季节影像块嵌入；空间表示图（spatial representation graph）用于平滑规则评分；整体为弱监督管道，非端到端检测模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等信息。该管道设计用于地面后处理分析（human review），非星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：爱尔兰科克郡（County Cork, Ireland）的Sentinel多季节影像块（26,722个有效tile），结合OpenStreetMap农场先验；评价指标：Precision within 500m / 1000m（以held-out OSM农场特征为代理标签）；无mAP、F1、召回率等目标检测标准指标。

Experiments: 主运行从26,722个tile中选出535个高置信度tile，形成71个候选集群；Top 5集群在500m内精度0.60，1000m内精度0.80；Top 10集群在500m内精度0.40，1000m内精度0.80；无与SOTA检测方法的对比实验。

Limitations: 1. 任务非目标检测，无法直接迁移到遥感目标检测场景；2. 无轻量化设计，无法用于在轨部署；3. 依赖OpenStreetMap先验，泛化性受限；4. 评价指标为代理精度（proxy evaluation），非标准检测指标；5. 仅针对奶牛场单一场景，不适用于通用遥感目标检测。

Ideas for my research: 1. Barlow Twins自监督学习策略可用于遥感影像预训练，减少对标注数据的依赖，但需结合检测头；2. 多季节/多时相特征融合思路可借鉴到遥感目标检测中的数据增强或特征增强；3. 空间表示图（graph-based smoothing）可借鉴用于检测后处理中的空间一致性约束；4. 整体而言，该论文对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的借鉴价值较低，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Farm site discovery from satellite imagery is a spatiotemporal candidate ranking problem because farm evidence is distributed across pasture, field boundaries, roads, buildings, and seasonal vegetation patterns. Direct farm labels are often incomplete, which makes fully supervised detection difficult. This paper proposes a weakly supervised pipeline for ranking dairy farm candidate clusters from seasonal Sentinel imagery and open map priors. The method uses aligned spring, summer, and autumn image tiles from County Cork, Ireland, with spectral bands, vegetation indices, built area indices, and a pasture channel. A Barlow Twins encoder learns multi-season tile embeddings without farm labels. In parallel, weak OpenStreetMap farm priors are split into a prior and a held-out set. Prior features support a rule-based tile score that combines farm proximity, seasonal pasture evidence, and summer greenness, while held-out features are reserved only for proxy evaluation. The rule score is smoothed over a spatial representation graph using geographic proximity and embedding similarity, and high-scoring tiles are grouped into ranked candidate clusters. From 26,722 valid tiles, the main run selects 535 high-confidence tiles and forms 71 candidate clusters. The top 5 clusters achieve 0.60 precision within 500 m and 0.80 precision within 1000 m of held-out OpenStreetMap farm features. The top 10 clusters achieve 0.40 precision within 500 m and 0.80 precision within 1000 m. The results show that seasonal representation learning and weak geographic priors can reduce large satellite image collections into compact candidate sets for human review.

</details>


### [71] [CRC-HGD: A Histopathological Image Dataset for Grading Colorectal Cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12750)
*Elham Amjadi,Amin Bahreini,Sayed Mohammad Hasan Emami,Sayyed Mohammadreza Hakimian,Alireza Fahim,Hojjatollah Rahimi,Hamidreza Bolhasani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了一个结直肠癌组织病理学显微镜图像数据集CRC-HGD，用于癌症分级分类，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于医学组织病理学图像（H&E染色结直肠组织切片）的癌症分级数据集构建，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关领域，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：医学图像分类（结直肠癌组织学分级）；应用场景：病理诊断；传感器类型：光学显微镜（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：结直肠腺癌三级分化（Grade I/II/III）。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文仅介绍数据集，未提出任何模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：CRC-HGD，共1,914张图像，来自214名结直肠腺癌患者，H&E染色组织切片，4种放大倍数（4x/10x/20x/40x），三级分级标注。评价指标：摘要未说明。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。论文仅介绍数据集构建，未进行模型实验或对比。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法。该论文是纯医学图像数据集论文，不涉及任何检测或轻量化算法。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题在领域、任务、方法上均无交集。

Abstract: Colorectal cancer (CRC) is the third most common cancer worldwide and the second leading cause of cancer-related deaths globally, with approximately 1,926,425 new cases and 904,019 deaths reported in 2022. Accurate histologic grading plays a critical role in prognosis and treatment planning for colorectal adenocarcinoma. In recent years, artificial intelligence and its subcategories, including machine learning and deep learning, have been increasingly employed for automated cancer detection and classification. An appropriate and well-organized dataset is the essential first step to achieve this goal. This paper introduces CRC-HGD, a histopathological microscopy image dataset of 1,914 images obtained from 214 colorectal adenocarcinoma patients (Grade I: 106, Grade II: 75, Grade III: 33). The specimens are H&E-stained colorectal tissue sections acquired at the Poursina Hakim Research Center of Isfahan University of Medical Sciences, Iran, diagnosed between 2014 and 2019, and graded according to the World Health Organization (WHO) criteria into three grades: well-differentiated (Grade I), moderately differentiated (Grade II), and poorly differentiated (Grade III). For each specimen, four magnification levels are provided: 4x, 10x, 20x, and 40x. The dataset is accessible via Mendeley Data (https://doi.org/10.17632/yfp5sfj47m.4) and at http://databiox.com, where the latest version is also available. The distinctive feature of this dataset is the provision of labeled specimens across all three differentiation grades at multiple magnification levels, enabling comprehensive computational analysis of colorectal cancer grading.

</details>


### [72] [Hallo4D: Multi-Modal Hallucination Mitigation for Consistent Spatio-Temporal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12752)
*Hongbo Wang,Huaibo Huang,Jie Cao,Jin Liu,Haoyang Tong,Ran He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Hallo4D 是一个利用大多模态语言模型(LMM)检测和修正3D/4D内容生成中时空幻觉的统一框架，采用生成-检测-修正范式实现多视角和时间一致性优化。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于3D/4D内容生成中的时空一致性问题，属于计算机视觉中的生成式AI领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：3D/4D内容生成中的时空一致性优化；应用场景：视觉内容生成（非遥感）；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基于大多模态语言模型(LMM)的生成-检测-修正框架，包含LMM-based selector、多模型投票机制、运动感知关键帧采样、LMM引导初始化、外观对齐、曝光感知优化和可见性剪枝等模块。模型无关(model-agnostic)设计。

Lightweight method: 摘要中提到的"visibility pruning"（可见性剪枝）是用于增强鲁棒性的策略，并非模型轻量化意义上的剪枝（pruning）。不涉及量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该框架依赖大语言模型(LMM)，计算开销大，不适合星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。

Experiments: 在多种3D和4D生成设置下，Hallo4D持续优于强基线方法。摘要未提供定量对比数据。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 依赖大语言模型(LMM)，计算开销大，不适合资源受限的星上场景；3. 摘要未提供定量结果和具体数据集。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但以下思路可间接参考：1) "生成-检测-修正"范式可类比为遥感检测中的"检测-验证-修正"流程；2) 多模型投票(voting)思想可用于遥感目标检测中的模型集成或预测融合；3) 运动感知关键帧采样(motion-aware keyframe sampling)思路可迁移到遥感视频流中的关键帧选取。但这些均为间接启发，非直接相关。

Abstract: While recent advances in 3D generation have enabled impressive visual synthesis, existing methods often rely on 2D diffusion supervision without explicit mechanisms for geometric consistency, leading to spatial hallucinations such as duplicated structures and misaligned geometry. These issues become more severe in 4D generation, where maintaining consistency across viewpoints and temporal evolution introduces additional challenges, including jitter, identity flicker, and structural drift. We present \textbf{Hallo4D}, a unified and model-agnostic framework for mitigating spatiotemporal hallucinations in 3D and 4D content generation. Hallo4D introduces a generation-detection-correction paradigm that leverages large multimodal language models (LMMs) to identify and summarize spatial and temporal inconsistencies from multi-view and multi-frame renderings. These insights guide a consensus-driven image-space consistency optimization, where an LMM-based selector evaluates candidate corrections through multi-model voting, without requiring retraining or architectural modifications. To further improve temporal consistency and optimization efficiency, Hallo4D incorporates motion-aware keyframe sampling, LMM-guided initialization, and appearance alignment. We additionally introduce exposure-aware optimization and visibility pruning to enhance robustness under challenging viewpoints. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Hallo4D consistently outperforms strong baselines across diverse 3D and 4D generation settings, providing a scalable and generalizable solution for consistency-aware content generation.

</details>


### [73] [RFMSR: Residual Flow Matching for Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12753)
*Shuwei Huang,Tianyao Luo,Jicheng Liu,Daizong Liu,Pan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于残差流匹配的图像超分辨率方法RFMSR，通过将源分布中心化到低质量图像潜空间来缩短传输距离，并采用两阶段训练策略实现单步高质量生成。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于图像超分辨率（ISR）任务，虽然超分辨率可作为遥感图像预处理手段，但论文未涉及遥感场景、目标检测、在轨部署或模型轻量化核心问题。方法基于扩散模型/流匹配，模型规模大、训练成本高，与轻量化方向背道而驰。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非课题涉及遥感图像超分辨率作为检测前处理，且需要了解流匹配方法的最新进展，否则可忽略。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：图像超分辨率（Image Super-Resolution, ISR）；场景：通用图像超分，非遥感特定；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：无。

Model architecture: 基于流匹配（flow matching）的视觉框架RFMSR。核心创新：将源分布中心化到低质量（LQ）图像的潜空间（latent space），而非标准流匹配中的纯高斯先验，从而缩短传输距离并保留结构先验。采用两阶段训练策略：Phase I通过条件流匹配预训练速度场（velocity field），Phase II对单步预测施加端到端监督同时保留所有时间步的速度损失。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。论文虽提及"single-step acceleration"（单步加速），但这是针对扩散/流匹配推理步数的加速，并非模型轻量化。模型规模大（"massive model sizes"），与轻量化方向相悖。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文明确指出方法"massive model sizes and prohibitive training expenses"，不适合星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。仅提及与SOTA方法比较感知质量（perceptual quality）。

Experiments: 摘要未提供详细实验结果。仅声称在感知质量上达到或超越SOTA方法。代码已开源。

Limitations: 1. 模型规模大、训练成本高，不适合资源受限场景；2. 未涉及遥感图像超分的特定挑战（如多光谱/高光谱、大尺度变化、地物纹理保真度）；3. 未与遥感目标检测下游任务结合验证；4. 单步生成质量与多步精化的权衡缺乏定量分析。

Ideas for my research: 1. 残差流匹配的思想（将源分布中心化到输入潜空间）可借鉴到遥感图像超分任务中，作为遥感目标检测的前处理增强手段；2. 两阶段训练策略（先预训练速度场再端到端微调单步预测）对遥感场景下的快速推理有参考价值；3. 但整体而言，该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接关联度很低，不建议作为核心参考。

Abstract: Image super-resolution (ISR) has witnessed remarkable progress with diffusion models and flow matching. The dominant text-to-image (T2I) based approaches leverage large-scale foundation models as generative priors, achieving impressive perceptual quality but at the cost of massive model sizes and prohibitive training expenses. Recent flow-matching-based vision-only approaches have made significant strides; however, they adopt standard flow formulations that transport from a pure Gaussian prior to the data distribution, discarding the rich structural information already present in the low-quality (LQ) input. Furthermore, existing single-step acceleration techniques often forfeit the model's multi-step inference capability. In this paper, we propose Residual Flow Matching for Image Super-Resolution (RFMSR), a vision-only framework that centers the source distribution at the LQ latent, reducing transport distance and preserving structural priors throughout the flow trajectory. We further introduce a two-phase training strategy: Phase I pretrains the velocity field via conditional flow matching, while Phase II applies end-to-end supervision to the single-step prediction while retaining the velocity loss across all timesteps, achieving high-quality single-step generation without sacrificing multi-step refinement. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RFMSR achieves comparable or even superior perceptual quality compared to state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods. The source code is available at https://github.com/Faze-Hsw/RFMSR.

</details>


### [74] [VisCo: Leveraging Large Language Models as Intrinsic Encoders for Visual Token Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12756)
*Yupeng Zheng,Kai Zou,Bin Liu,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VisCo，一种训练高效的视觉token自压缩框架，利用预训练VLM自身作为内在压缩器，通过参数共享自编码器和记忆token实现视觉token压缩，无需外部压缩模块或大量重训练。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦于视觉语言模型(VLM)的视觉token压缩，属于通用视觉/多模态领域，与遥感、卫星、在轨部署、目标检测均无直接关联。虽然token压缩方法在理论上可迁移至遥感场景，但论文本身未涉及任何遥感相关任务、数据或部署约束。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，阅读优先级最低。除非后续工作将token压缩方法迁移到遥感检测场景，否则不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：视觉token压缩（visual token compression）；应用场景：通用VLM推理加速；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。摘要未说明具体视觉任务（如检测、分割等）。

Model architecture: VisCo框架：参数共享自编码器（parameter-sharing autoencoder），使用少量记忆token（memory tokens）压缩视觉信息，并将编码到解码的分层信息进行传递。核心思想是复用预训练VLM自身作为内在压缩器（intrinsic compressor），不引入外部压缩模块。

Lightweight method: 视觉token压缩（visual token compression），属于推理加速/内存优化方法。训练高效（training-efficient），无需大量重训练。但摘要未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等经典轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等具体部署指标。仅提及降低inference latency和memory overhead，但无量化数据。未涉及星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明使用的数据集和评价指标。

Experiments: 实验表明VisCo在所有评估压缩比下均优于先前方法，在更激进压缩下增益更大，甚至在极端单token设置下仍保持稳定。此外，记忆token与原始视觉token结合还能提升基模型性能，表明VisCo捕获了超越压缩的互补表示。但摘要未给出具体数值对比。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未提供任何部署相关量化指标（参数量、FLOPs、延迟等）；3. 未说明在具体下游任务（如目标检测）上的表现；4. 方法针对VLM设计，对纯视觉检测模型的适用性未知。

Ideas for my research: 1. 记忆token（memory tokens）的思想可借鉴：在遥感目标检测中，可设计少量可学习的"场景记忆token"来压缩高分辨率遥感图像中的冗余信息，降低输入token数量；2. 参数共享自编码器结构可尝试用于遥感图像的轻量特征编码；3. 训练高效的压缩策略（不破坏预训练模型先验）对遥感在轨场景有价值，因为星上重训练成本极高；4. 但需注意本文方法面向VLM，直接迁移到遥感目标检测模型（如YOLO系列、DETR系列）需重新设计。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) process large numbers of visual tokens, resulting in substantial inference latency and memory overhead. This has motivated extensive research on visual token compression. While training-free strategies rely on heuristic metrics and suffer significant performance degradation under high compression ratios, many training-based methods introduce external compression modules that force the VLM backbone to adapt, incurring substantial retraining cost and compromising VLMs' priors. Effective visual token compression hinges on strong information encoding, a capability already present in pretrained VLMs but underutilized by existing approaches. Motivated by this, we propose VisCo, a training-efficient self-compression framework that reuses the pretrained VLM itself as an intrinsic compressor. VisCo is a parameter-sharing autoencoder that compresses visual information using a small set of memory tokens and transfers hierarchical information from encoding to decoding. Experiments show that VisCo surpasses prior methods across all evaluated compression ratios, with larger gains under more aggressive compression, and remains stable even in the extreme single-token setting. Moreover, when combined with the original visual tokens, the learned memory tokens can even improve the base model, suggesting that VisCo captures complementary representations beyond compression.

</details>


### [75] [EvoGraph-R1: Self-Evolving Multimodal Knowledge Hypergraphs for Agentic Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12764)
*Jiashi Lin,Changhong Jiang,Xiangru Lin,Ruifei Zhang,Xinyi Zhu,Jiyao Liu,Cheng Tang,Ye Du,Shujian Gao,Junzhi Ning,Lihao Liu,Ziyan Huang,Tianbin Li,Jin Ye,Junjun He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种自进化知识图谱RAG框架EvoGraph-R1，将检索建模为马尔可夫决策过程，通过智能体交互动态更新图结构以提升多模态大语言模型的知识推理能力。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦多模态大语言模型的知识检索增强生成（RAG），属于自然语言处理与多模态推理领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：多模态知识检索增强生成（GraphRAG）；场景：多模态VQA和文本QA；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: EvoGraph-R1框架，将知识图谱构建为动态环境，检索过程建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），包含GraphRetrieve（图检索）、WebSearch（网络搜索）、GraphEdit（图编辑）、Answer（终止）四种动作。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未涉及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该框架依赖大语言模型和动态图更新，计算开销大，不适合星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：多模态VQA和文本QA基准数据集（具体名称摘要未说明）；评价指标：accuracy（准确率）、coverage（覆盖率）、traceability（可追溯性）。

Experiments: 在多个多模态VQA和文本QA基准上，相比现有RAG基线在准确率、覆盖率和可追溯性上有显著提升。具体数值摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星/目标检测任务；2. 未讨论模型轻量化或边缘部署；3. 依赖大语言模型，计算和存储开销大；4. 动态图更新的实时性未评估。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。但"自进化"思想（根据新证据动态更新模型/知识结构）可抽象迁移：在轨目标检测模型可考虑根据新获取的遥感图像数据自适应调整检测头或类别库，但与本课题核心方向（轻量化、实时推理、星上部署）关联度极低。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has emerged as a critical paradigm for grounding Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) in external knowledge. Recent GraphRAG methods introduce structured entity-relation graphs to improve retrieval and reasoning. However, they remain limited by treating knowledge graphs as static data structures built offline and queried in a single pass. This static paradigm misaligns with the interactive, iterative nature of knowledge-intensive reasoning, creating three bottlenecks: (i) text-centric fragmentation that impedes cross-modal reasoning, (ii) frozen structures unable to incorporate new evidence or correct errors, and (iii) rigid single-pass retrieval without adaptive refinement. To overcome these limitations, we introduce EvoGraph-R1, a self-evolving GraphRAG framework that reconceptualizes knowledge graphs as dynamic environments shaped through agent interactions. We formulate retrieval as a Markov Decision Process (MDP) where the agent observes the graph state and executes actions to query (GraphRetrieve), expand (WebSearch), refine (GraphEdit), or terminate (Answer) the reasoning. These actions reshape the hypergraph structure and generate feedback signals that guide subsequent evolution. Through this closed loop, the hypergraph evolves by integrating new evidence, correcting errors, and refining structure to support multi-hop reasoning. Experiments on multimodal VQA and text QA benchmarks demonstrate substantial improvements over existing RAG baselines in accuracy, coverage, and traceability, establishing self-evolving knowledge graphs as a fundamental paradigm across modalities.

</details>


### [76] [HSEmotion Team at the 11th ABAW Challenge: Multi-Task Learning and Ambivalence/Hesitancy Video Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12774)
*Aleksei Bakin,Andrey V. Savchenko*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文报告了第11届ABAW竞赛的结果，使用冻结的轻量级面部特征提取器（MT-EmotiDDAMFN和MT-EmotiEffNet-B0）进行多任务情感分析，并通过系统后处理和轻量多模态融合在情感识别和犹豫/矛盾视频识别任务上取得有竞争力的性能。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。本文研究的是情感行为分析（Affective Behavior Analysis），属于人脸表情识别、情感计算领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、在轨部署等方向完全无关。虽然文中使用了"lightweight"一词，但轻量化对象是面部特征提取器，应用场景是情感识别竞赛，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：情感行为分析（valence-arousal回归、面部表情分类、动作单元检测）、犹豫/矛盾视频识别。应用场景：人脸情感计算、竞赛（ABAW）。传感器类型：RGB摄像头（人脸视频）、音频、文本。目标类别：情感状态（valence, arousal）、面部表情（6类+中性）、动作单元（AU）、犹豫/矛盾二分类。与遥感无关。

Model architecture: MT-EmotiDDAMFN（轻量面部特征提取器）、MT-EmotiEffNet-B0（轻量面部特征提取器），均为冻结（frozen）的轻量骨干网络，搭配独立预测头（separate heads）。多模态融合：人脸+HuBERT音频+RoBERTa文本分类器，全局文本门控（global-text gate）。

Lightweight method: 使用冻结的轻量级特征提取器（frozen lightweight facial extractors），不微调重型骨干网络（without fine-tuning heavy backbones）。轻量化体现在模型选择层面（选用轻量网络），而非剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩方法。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs等具体轻量化指标。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到"improved efficiency and deployment flexibility"作为定性结论，无定量数据。与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：s-Aff-Wild2（多任务情感分析）、BAH数据集（犹豫/矛盾视频识别）。评价指标：Frame-level Weighted F1、Video-level Macro F1。验证集结果：Weighted F1从0.74提升至0.79，最佳公开测试集Video-level Macro F1达0.73。

Experiments: 在ABAW竞赛验证集上，集成方法显著超过ConvNeXt基线。后处理策略包括：时序高斯平滑、每类表情偏置、AffectNet混合、每AU阈值调优、加权骨干融合。多模态融合采用人脸、音频（HuBERT）、文本（RoBERTa）的晚期融合+时序聚合+全局文本门控。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴。2. 轻量化仅体现在选用轻量网络并冻结，未涉及剪枝、量化等压缩技术。3. 无参数量、FLOPs、推理速度等量化指标。4. 应用场景（人脸情感分析）与遥感场景差异巨大。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可参考的点是"冻结轻量骨干+独立预测头"的设计范式，这种解耦思路在遥感目标检测中也可考虑：冻结预训练的轻量遥感特征提取器，仅训练检测头。但本文未提供任何遥感相关技术细节，且情感分析任务与目标检测差异巨大，不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: This article presents our results for the 11th Affective Behavior Analysis in-the-Wild (ABAW) competition. For multi-task learning with simultaneous prediction of valence, arousal, facial expressions, and action units on s-Aff-Wild2 dataset, we use frozen lightweight facial extractors, MT-EmotiDDAMFN and MT-EmotiEffNet-B0, with separate heads and systematic post-processing: temporal Gaussian smoothing, per-class expression bias, AffectNet blending, per-AU threshold tuning, and weighted backbone fusion. On the official validation set, our ensemble significantly exceeds the performance of the ConvNeXt baseline. For ambivalence/hesitancy video recognition on the expanded BAH dataset, we extend the audiovisual pipeline to video-level Macro F1 by late fusion of face, HuBERT audio, and RoBERTa text classifiers, temporal aggregation, and a global-text gate. Frame-level Weighted F1 on validation set rises from 0.74 in ABAW-8 to 0.79, while the best public-test video-level Macro F1 reaches 0.73. In both tasks, competitive performance is achieved without fine-tuning heavy backbones. These results indicate that systematic prediction calibration and lightweight multimodal fusion can rival substantially heavier end-to-end approaches while offering improved efficiency and deployment flexibility.

</details>


### [77] [MBTI: A Multi-Branch Efficient Fine-Tuning Framework for Hyperspectral Image Classification with Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12782)
*Mingzhen Xu,Haonan Guo,Di Wang,Yinghua Qu,Zhiliang Zhou,Lei Zhang,Huiwen Yao,Rui Zhao,Fengxiang Wang,Gang Wan,Bo Du,Liangpei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MBTI多分支高效微调框架，通过保留全波段光谱信息的分支预处理+LoRA+注意力融合，解决高光谱基础模型因传感器波段配置差异导致的迁移困难问题。

Research relevance: 2分——弱相关。论文涉及高光谱遥感图像分类，属于遥感领域，但任务为分类而非目标检测，且不涉及轻量化模型设计（仅用LoRA减少可训练参数量，但未涉及剪枝/量化/蒸馏/实时推理/星上部署等轻量化核心内容），与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"方向相关性较弱。

Reading priority: 2分——弱相关，阅读优先级较低。虽然涉及高光谱遥感，但任务为分类而非目标检测，且轻量化仅体现在LoRA微调参数量少，未涉及推理加速和部署优化，对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接借鉴价值有限。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：高光谱图像分类（HSI classification）；传感器类型：高光谱遥感传感器；目标类别：摘要未说明具体地物类别；应用场景：遥感图像分类，非目标检测。

Model architecture: 多分支架构：1) 光谱连续性保持的多分支预处理策略——将原始HSI划分为多个连续光谱子集，剩余波段不足时采用波段重用机制；2) 每个分支插入独立LoRA模块进行高效微调；3) 多分支通道注意力融合模块，自适应重标定并融合各分支特征。

Lightweight method: LoRA（Low-Rank Adaptation）——仅微调约2.33%-2.36%的参数，大部分预训练参数冻结。但论文未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等传统模型轻量化/压缩方法，也未讨论推理速度、FLOPs、延迟等部署指标。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到可训练参数占比约2.33%-2.36%（rank=8配置下），但未给出模型总参数量或推理速度。未讨论星上/边缘部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 三个公开高光谱数据集；评价指标：摘要未说明具体指标名称（如OA、AA、Kappa等未提及）；实验设置：摘要未说明训练/测试划分细节。

Experiments: 在三个公开高光谱数据集上与代表性分类方法对比，MBTI取得有竞争力且优越的性能。消融实验：摘要未说明具体消融内容。代码已开源。

Limitations: 1) 任务为分类而非目标检测，与课题方向不直接匹配；2) 未讨论推理速度、模型大小、计算开销等部署关键指标；3) 多分支结构可能引入额外计算开销，是否适合星上资源受限环境存疑；4) 仅在三个公开数据集上验证，未在真实星载场景测试；5) 未涉及实时推理或边缘部署讨论。

Ideas for my research: 1) LoRA微调思路可借鉴：在遥感目标检测模型轻量化中，可探索将LoRA用于检测头或特征提取网络的参数高效微调；2) 多分支+注意力融合的设计思想可用于多光谱/高光谱遥感目标检测中的多波段特征融合；3) 波段重用机制避免信息丢失的思路，对多光谱遥感目标检测中波段选择有参考价值；4) 但需注意：本文是分类任务，需自行将类似思路迁移到检测任务；且需补充轻量化部署指标评估。

Abstract: Hyperspectral foundation models learn transferable spectral-spatial representations from large-scale unlabeled data. They provide an effective paradigm for adapting to downstream hyperspectral image (HSI) classification tasks with limited labeled samples. However, spectral band configurations vary substantially across sensors, which makes direct model transfer difficult. Existing adaptation strategies often compress, select, or reshape the original spectra to match model-specific input requirements. These operations may discard useful spectral information and weaken local spectral continuity. To address this problem, we propose MBTI, a Multi-Branch efficient fine-tuning framework for Hyperspectral Image classification. MBTI adapts hyperspectral foundation models to downstream classification tasks while preserving full-band spectral information. First, we introduce a spectral-continuity-preserving multi-branch preprocessing strategy. The original HSI is divided into multiple continuous spectral subsets, and a band reuse mechanism is used when the remaining bands cannot form a complete branch. This avoids invalid padding and unnecessary spectral loss. Second, independent Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) modules are inserted into each branch. They enable different spectral intervals to learn task-specific discriminative features while keeping most pre-trained parameters frozen. Finally, a multi-branch channel attention fusion module adaptively recalibrates and integrates features from all spectral branches. Experiments on three public hyperspectral datasets show that MBTI achieves competitive and superior performance compared with representative classification methods. Under the final rank-8 configuration, only about 2.33\%--2.36\% of the parameters are trainable. The code will be available at https://github.com/Azhenmiddleblock/MBTI/tree/main.

</details>


### [78] [ExtraGS: Enhancing Endoscopic View Extrapolation via Diffusion-Guided 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12785)
*Cheng-Tai Hsieh,Jiwei Shan,Han Fang,Jianshu Hu,Tao Ni,Lijun Han,Yutong Ban,Shing Shin Cheng,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ExtraGS框架，利用扩散模型引导的3D高斯泼溅增强内窥镜视角外推，用于机器人辅助微创手术中的新视角合成。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦于机器人辅助微创手术（MIS）中的内窥镜视角外推与新视角合成，属于医学图像/手术机器人领域，与遥感卫星、Earth observation、SAR/高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等方向完全无关。不涉及目标检测，也不涉及轻量化/部署。与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：内窥镜新视角合成（novel view synthesis）；场景：机器人辅助微创手术；传感器：内窥镜（endoscope）；目标类别：解剖结构（anatomical structures）。摘要未说明具体目标类别。

Model architecture: 基于3D Gaussian Splatting（3DGS）的神经渲染框架，结合扩散模型（diffusion model）进行生成细化，引入不确定性引导的虚拟相机采样策略和置信度加权微调策略。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。且该工作面向手术机器人场景，与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 多个公开内窥镜数据集（multiple public endoscopic datasets）。评价指标：摘要未说明具体指标名称。实验设置：摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在多个公开内窥镜数据集上，ExtraGS显著减少了外推伪影，在新视角合成任务上达到SOTA性能。摘要未提供具体数值对比。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可迁移的技术点；2. 摘要未说明计算开销和实时性，无法判断是否适合部署；3. 扩散模型推理通常较慢，不满足实时性要求。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与本人课题方向基本无关，无直接可借鉴点。其不确定性引导采样策略在理论上可迁移到遥感数据增强或主动学习场景，但关联性极弱，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Robot-assisted minimally invasive surgery (MIS) critically depends on reliable endoscopic perception for navigation and safety. However, conventional endoscopes provide only a limited field of view, leaving large portions of surrounding anatomy unobserved. Recent neural rendering approaches, such as Neural Radiance Fields and 3D Gaussian Splatting, enable novel view synthesis from endoscopic videos, but their reliance on sparse observations often leads to severe artifacts when extrapolating beyond the training trajectory.In this work, we propose ExtraGS, a framework for enhancing endoscopic view extrapolation via diffusion-guided 3D Gaussian Splatting. Starting from an initial reconstruction, we introduce an uncertainty-guided virtual camera sampling strategy to actively explore blind spots and maximize information gain. The rendered views from these sampled locations are refined using a diffusion model to recover plausible anatomical structures, producing pseudo observations that guide further optimization. To prevent the generated content from degrading reliable regions, we adopt a confidence-weighted fine-tuning strategy when incorporating these pseudo observations.Extensive experiments on multiple public endoscopic datasets demonstrate that ExtraGS significantly reduces extrapolation artifacts and achieves state-of-the-art performance in endoscopic novel view synthesis.

</details>


### [79] [CoRe: A Comprehensive Framework for Cross-Image Comparative Reasoning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12786)
*Lin Peng,Cong Wan,Zeyu Guo,SongLin Dong,Yihong Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CoRe框架，包含大规模三元组训练集CoRe-20K、结构化奖励框架TriSR和跨图像比较推理基准CoRe-Bench，用于提升VLM的细粒度跨图像比较推理能力。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于视觉-语言模型(VLM)的跨图像比较推理能力，不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测或模型轻量化任何一方面。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：跨图像比较推理（cross-image comparative reasoning）；场景：通用视觉-语言理解；传感器类型：自然图像；目标类别：计数、深度、距离、空间关系等属性。不涉及遥感场景。

Model architecture: CoRe框架包含：(i) CoRe-20K数据集自动构建管道；(ii) TriSR结构化奖励框架（包含attribute grounding, judgment alignment, triplet consistency三个监督信号，基于GRPO优化）；(iii) CoRe-Bench评估基准。未提及具体视觉backbone或检测模型结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：CoRe-20K（自建三元组训练集，从结构化视觉元数据自动构建）；CoRe-Bench（首个细粒度跨图像比较推理基准）。评价指标：partial accuracy（部分准确率），在CoRe-Bench上比最强基线提升28.2个百分点；同时在标准多模态基准上保持竞争力。

Experiments: CoRe在CoRe-Bench上显著优于现有VLM，在标准多模态基准上保持竞争力。具体对比方法和消融实验细节摘要未说明。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关。未涉及遥感图像特性（如大尺度变化、密集小目标、旋转目标）、实时推理约束或边缘部署需求。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。如果强行关联，其"结构化奖励框架"（TriSR）中多目标联合监督的思想，或许可迁移到遥感目标检测中多任务学习（如检测+定位+旋转角回归）的损失设计，但需要大幅改造。整体而言不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Cross-image comparative reasoning remains challenging for vision-language models (VLMs), especially when correct prediction requires fine-grained attribute grounding and globally consistent reasoning. We present CoRe, a unified framework for this problem. CoRe includes: (i) CoRe-20K, a large-scale triplet-based training set automatically constructed from structured visual metadata through a multi-expert collaborative pipeline, covering counting, depth, distance, and spatial relations; (ii) TriSR, a structured reward framework that jointly supervises attribute grounding, judgment alignment, and triplet consistency under GRPO optimization; and (iii) CoRe-Bench, the first benchmark dedicated to fine-grained cross-image comparative reasoning. Experiments show that CoRe substantially outperforms existing VLMs on CoRe-Bench while remaining competitive on standard multimodal benchmarks, achieving a 28.2-point gain in partial accuracy over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [80] [UniVR: Thinking in Visual Space for Unified Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12800)
*Zhongwei Ren,Yunchao Wei,Yao Zhao,Weibo Gong,Xiao Liu,Anran Wang,Xiangtai Li,Xiaojie Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UniVR，一种从纯视觉演示中学习复杂推理、物理动力学和长期规划的框架，核心是VR-GRPO强化学习范式和VR-X基准数据集。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于通用视觉推理（visual reasoning）、物理动力学建模和长期规划，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、边缘部署等模型压缩或加速技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：视觉推理（visual reasoning）、物理动力学建模、长期规划。应用场景：通用视觉智能，非遥感场景。传感器类型：纯视觉数据（raw visual data），非遥感传感器。目标类别：无目标检测相关类别。

Model architecture: UniVR框架，核心为VR-GRPO（一种强化学习范式），包含全局和步骤级互补奖励（global and step-level rewards）。摘要未说明具体网络骨干结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。摘要未提及任何剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计或模型压缩方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未提及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: VR-X基准数据集，从16个来源构建，涵盖长时程操作、空间谜题和物理推理。评价指标：VR-X上的性能提升（最高25%），以及多种多模态理解基准上的表现。摘要未说明具体指标名称。

Experiments: UniVR在VR-X上取得最高25%的提升，并在多种多模态理解基准上表现优越。摘要未说明对比方法和消融实验细节。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或目标检测技术。论文聚焦于通用视觉推理，不涉及遥感图像处理、目标检测或模型压缩。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于通用视觉推理领域，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题的研究目标、技术路线和应用场景均不匹配。

Abstract: Learning broad world knowledge directly from raw visual data is a fundamental capability of intelligence. We introduce UniVR, the first investigation into simultaneously learning complex reasoning, fine-grained physical dynamics, and long-term planning from pure visual demonstrations. At its core, UniVR features VR-GRPO, a reinforcement learning paradigm with complementary global and step-level rewards. This approach enforces logical coherence and physical consistency throughout the reasoning process without requiring task-specific heuristics or image-text pairs. To train and evaluate UniVR, we construct VR-X, a large-scale benchmark curated from 16 diverse sources spanning long-horizon manipulation, spatial puzzles, and physical reasoning. It is the first comprehensive suite to assess these heterogeneous capabilities under a purely visual protocol. Remarkably, UniVR achieves up to a 25% improvement on VR-X, and its superior visual reasoning also boosts performance on various multimodal understanding benchmarks. These findings underscore the vast potential of reasoning within visual spaces, with all code, data, and models are open-sourced for further research.

</details>


### [81] [Breaking Déjà Vu: Independent Auditing of Visual Place Recognition through Vision-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12818)
*Sania Waheed,Michael Milford,Sarvapali D. Ramchurn,Shoaib Ehsan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于视觉语言模型(VLM)的视觉地点识别(VPR)后检索验证框架，用于提升机器人SLAM中回环检测的可靠性，不涉及遥感或轻量化。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于机器人SLAM中的视觉地点识别(VPR)问题，不涉及遥感卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：视觉地点识别(VPR)后检索验证（Visual Place Recognition Auditing）；场景：机器人SLAM回环检测；传感器类型：普通光学相机；目标类别：无（场景级匹配，非目标检测）。

Model architecture: 基于Vision-Language Models (VLMs)的独立后检索验证框架，对查询图像和候选图像进行联合推理，不依赖特定架构的置信度度量。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。VLM模型通常计算开销大，不适用于星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 6个基准数据集；使用5种SOTA VPR方法和4种VLM进行评估；评价指标：recall@1、false acceptance rate、precision、coverage。

Experiments: VLM-based auditing相比SOTA方法平均提升recall@1达13.6%，将false acceptance rate降至12%，precision保持在95%以上，coverage保持在75%以上。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感场景，与在轨目标检测无关；2. 未讨论模型计算开销和部署可行性；3. VLM模型参数量大、推理慢，不适合资源受限的星上环境；4. 未涉及任何轻量化技术。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。唯一可能的启发点是：VLM用于后验证的思路或许可以迁移到遥感目标检测的误检后处理中，但VLM本身计算开销大，需先做轻量化才能考虑星上部署，且摘要未提供轻量化方案。

Abstract: Visual place recognition (VPR) is a key enabler of accurate localization and long-term autonomous navigation in robotics applications, such as loop closure detection for simultaneous localisation and mapping (SLAM). However, real-world VPR deployment relies on selecting an image matching threshold that balances precision and recall. These thresholds are typically tuned using labeled validation data and fixed during deployment, making them unreliable under environmental changes where ground truth is unavailable. This is particularly problematic in safety-critical robotics, where accepting a false loop closure can corrupt the estimated trajectory and map. In this work, we introduce Visual Place Recognition Auditing, an independent post-retrieval verification framework that leverages Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to assess retrieved matches by reasoning jointly over query and candidate images. Unlike conventional verification methods, our approach performs instance-level verification without requiring architecture-specific confidence measures, dataset-dependent thresholds, or prior knowledge of the deployment environment. We evaluate our method on six benchmark datasets using five state-of-the-art VPR methods and four VLMs. Results show that VLM-based auditing improves recall@1 by 13.6% on average as compared to state-of-the-art methods while reducing false acceptance rates to 12%, maintaining precision above 95% and coverage above 75%.

</details>


### [82] [AVSCap: Orchestrating Audio-Visual Synergy for Omni-modal Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12820)
*Yanghai Wang,Jiahao Wang,Jiafu Tang,Yuanxing Zhang,Zhe Cao,Hanyan Bian,Zijie Zhang,Weiliang Luo,Zhiyu Pan,Zixuan Dong,Jiaheng Liu,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AVSCap框架，通过显式跨模态事件绑定和强化学习优化音视频字幕生成，构建了训练数据集AVSCap-130K和评测基准AVSCapBench。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于通用领域的音视频字幕生成（audio-visual captioning），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关场景，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化/部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：音视频字幕生成（audio-visual captioning）；场景：通用多模态理解；传感器类型：RGB摄像头+麦克风（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：视觉动作、语音、音乐、音效等事件（非遥感目标）。

Model architecture: AVSCap-7B，基于7B参数的大规模多模态模型，采用两阶段训练策略：监督微调（SFT）+ 基于混合奖励的强化学习（RL）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。模型参数量为7B，属于大规模模型，与轻量化方向背道而驰。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量（除7B外）、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署信息。7B参数量模型在星上/边缘部署完全不现实。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：AVSCap-130K（自构建训练集）、AVSCapBench（自构建评测基准）及外部benchmark；评价指标：细粒度事件召回率（event recall），评估视觉、音频和协同事件。

Experiments: 实验表明：强化学习比增加SFT数据带来更大收益；AVSCap-7B在非语音音频覆盖和跨模态绑定上表现最优，在开源模型中取得最佳综合性能。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关；2. 模型规模过大（7B），不具备任何部署可行性；3. 未讨论推理速度、资源消耗等工程化指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。唯一可能间接参考的点：其"decoupled-then-fused"的数据构建思路或"混合奖励强化学习"策略，如果未来在遥感多模态（如光学+SAR融合检测）场景中有类似需求，可考虑类似范式，但与本课题核心方向（轻量化目标检测）关联甚微。

Abstract: Omni-modal video captioning is not merely combining visual captioning with audio transcription: a useful caption must describe how visual actions, speech, music, and sound effects co-evolve. Existing large multimodal models often fail at this relational step, treating audio and visual streams as loosely coupled observations, relying on automatic speech recognition, and under-specifying non-speech sounds and their links to visual events. We present AVSCap, a framework for audio-visual captioning centered on explicit cross-modal event binding. First, we construct AVSCap-130K, a tri-modal training corpus generated by a decoupled-then-fused pipeline that anchors visual and acoustic evidence before composing grounded omni-modal captions. Second, we train AVSCap-7B, a 7B captioner with a two-stage strategy: supervised fine-tuning establishes baseline capabilities, while sample-efficient reinforcement learning uses hybrid rewards to optimize acoustic completeness and audio-visual synergy. Our scaling analysis shows that reinforcement learning brings larger gains than increasing SFT data. Third, we introduce AVSCapBench, a benchmark that decomposes captions into visual, audio, and synergy events and evaluates them with fine-grained event recall. Experiments on AVSCapBench and external benchmarks show that AVSCap-7B improves non-speech audio coverage and cross-modal binding, delivering the best overall performance among evaluated open-source models.

</details>


### [83] [LARAD: Layout-Aware Road Anomaly Detection via Spatial-Logic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12858)
*Shiyi Mu,Xujie Chen,Shugong Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LARAD方法，通过空间逻辑推理替代纹理新颖性检测，实现高效的道路异常目标检测，仅需单模型架构即可达到SOTA。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于自动驾驶场景下的道路异常目标检测（open-world obstacle detection / anomaly segmentation），属于地面自动驾驶领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、航空图像、SAR/高光谱/多光谱等遥感传感器，也不涉及在轨部署或星上智能。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向基本无关，仅空间逻辑推理思路有极有限的间接借鉴价值，不建议优先精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：异常分割/开集障碍物检测（anomaly segmentation / open-world obstacle detection）；场景：自动驾驶（autonomous driving）；传感器：摘要未说明（推测为车载RGB相机）；目标类别：道路异常目标（road anomalies / OoD objects）。

Model architecture: 基于标准闭集分割网络（standard closed-set segmentation network），增加一个轻量级的OoD引导注意力分支（lightweight OoD-guided attention branch）。整体为单模型架构（single-model architecture）。

Lightweight method: 轻量级注意力分支（lightweight attention branch），但摘要未说明具体参数量、FLOPs或压缩手段（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏等）。核心轻量化贡献在于避免级联多个大模型，保持单模型高效推理。

Onboard deployability: 摘要强调"retaining the high efficiency of a single-model architecture"和避免"unacceptable inference latency"，但未给出具体参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等量化指标。且面向的是车载边缘部署，而非星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。

Experiments: 实验表明LARAD在逻辑异常鲁棒性上显著提升，达到新SOTA。但摘要未给出对比方法的具体名称和消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星方向完全无关；2. 未提供任何量化轻量化指标（参数量、FLOPs、延迟等）；3. 仅针对自动驾驶道路场景，空间逻辑先验（如道路布局）难以直接迁移到遥感场景；4. 未涉及旋转框检测、小目标检测等遥感特有挑战。

Ideas for my research: 1. 其"空间逻辑违反合成（SLVS）"思路可借鉴：在遥感场景中，目标的空间上下文关系（如舰船应在水域而非陆地上、飞机应在跑道/停机坪上）可用于减少遥感目标检测的误检（false positives）；2. 轻量级OoD注意力分支的设计思路可迁移到遥感在轨模型，用于区分已知目标和未知异常目标；3. 单模型架构避免级联的思路与星上资源受限场景高度契合，但需进一步量化评估。

Abstract: Accurate open-world obstacle detection is critical for autonomous driving. Current anomaly segmentation methods suffer from a fundamental blind spot: they over-rely on texture novelty to identify out-of-distribution (OoD) objects while ignoring contextual spatial logic. Furthermore, mitigating the resulting false positives often requires cascading massive vision models, introducing unacceptable inference latency. To address these issues, we propose Layout-Aware Road Anomaly Detection (LARAD), shifting the paradigm from appearance matching to spatial-logic reasoning. First, we introduce the Spatial-Logic Violation Synthesis (SLVS) pipeline, which generates training samples that are texture-consistent yet spatially invalid, forcing the model to learn contextual violations. Second, we augment a standard closed-set segmentation network with a lightweight, OoD-guided attention branch. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LARAD significantly enhances robustness against logical anomalies and establishes a new state-of-the-art, all while retaining the high efficiency of a single-model architecture.

</details>


### [84] [Statistical Non-linear Reconstruction Loss for Image Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12866)
*Nguyen Minh Tri,Hoang Khuong Duy,Huynh Cong Viet Ngu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于sigmoid非线性重建损失和统计校准机制的图像异常检测方法，用于解决无监督异常检测中的异常泄露问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于工业图像异常检测（MVTec-AD, VisA数据集），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。既不是遥感场景，也不是目标检测任务，更不涉及轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：无监督图像异常检测（unsupervised image anomaly detection）；场景：工业检测（industrial inspection）；传感器类型：普通光学图像；目标类别：工业产品异常区域。与遥感/卫星/目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 基于重建的方法（reconstruction-based methods），核心改进是提出Non-linear Reconstruction Loss（非线性重建损失），使用sigmoid类挤压函数抑制高幅值特征。摘要未说明具体网络骨架。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：MVTec-AD（工业异常检测基准）、VisA（视觉异常检测数据集）；指标：Image-AUROC（图像级AUROC）、Pixel-AUROC（像素级AUROC）。与遥感数据集无关。

Experiments: 在MVTec-AD上达到99.0% Image-AUROC和97.3% Pixel-AUROC；在VisA上达到95.3% Image-AUROC和99.0% Pixel-AUROC。与SOTA方法对比具有竞争力或更优。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对工业异常检测场景验证，未在遥感图像上测试；2. 未讨论计算开销和模型效率；3. 统计校准方案中置信区间选择对性能的影响未深入分析；4. 未涉及任何轻量化或部署问题。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但非线性损失抑制异常特征的思想，在遥感目标检测中处理背景杂波（clutter）或噪声时可能有启发，例如在SAR图像舰船检测中抑制海杂波干扰。不过需要大幅改造才能适配遥感目标检测任务。

Abstract: Reconstruction-based methods are a cornerstone of unsupervised image anomaly detection, but they remain vulnerable to \emph{outlier leakage}, where standard mean squared error (MSE) loss drives the model to faithfully reconstruct anomalous patterns. We propose a Non-linear Reconstruction Loss that applies a sigmoid-based squashing function to suppress high-magnitude features, preventing outliers from dominating optimization while preserving sensitivity to normal patterns. In addition, we introduce a statistical calibration scheme that selects the scaling factor $k$ from the confidence interval (CI) of the normal feature distribution, enabling data-driven control of the suppression strength. Our approach achieves competitive or superior anomaly detection performance compared to state-of-the-art methods, reaching 99.0\% Image-AUROC and 97.3\% Pixel-AUROC on MVTec-AD, and 95.3\% Image-AUROC and 99.0\% Pixel-AUROC on VisA. These results indicate that non-linear gradient suppression is an effective mechanism for mitigating outlier leakage and improving anomaly localization in unified industrial inspection settings. The implementation is available at https://github.com/mintii13/Statistical-Non-linear-Reconstruction-Loss.git.

</details>


### [85] [Metric-Guided Synthetic Image Data Rendering for Deep Learning compatible with Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12874)
*Martina Radoynova,Samuel Pantze,Trina De,Ulrik Günther,Artur Yakimovich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GraNatPy工具包，通过量化指标指导合成数据渲染场景改进，以缩小真实与合成图像之间的域差距，提升目标检测模型的零样本性能，并进一步将过程式数据渲染自动化（SynthClaw）。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于合成数据生成与域差距量化，不涉及遥感/卫星场景、在轨部署或模型轻量化。虽然涉及目标检测和小目标检测，但应用场景是病毒学斑块测定（virological plaque assays），非遥感图像。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非后续工作专门扩展到遥感合成数据生成领域，否则参考价值有限。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：合成数据生成与域差距量化；应用场景：科学应用中的计算机视觉（virological plaque assays）；传感器类型：普通相机照片；目标类别：病毒斑块（小目标）。不涉及遥感、卫星、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构，仅提及使用了object detection model进行零样本性能评估。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性或星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：合成渲染数据集 + 真实照片（virological plaque assays）；评价指标：gradient similarity（梯度相似度）、zero-shot performance of object detection、visual perception（视觉感知）。未提及遥感常用数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR等）。

Experiments: 实验表明：渲染数据集的真实感、多样性和尺寸的量化提升与视觉感知改善及目标检测零样本性能提升相关；梯度相似度影响小目标检测性能；混合真实与合成数据可提升小目标检测。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对病毒学斑块这一特定科学应用，泛化性未知；2. 未讨论合成数据在遥感场景（如卫星图像中的舰船/飞机检测）中的域差距问题；3. 未涉及任何轻量化或部署相关技术；4. 未给出模型参数量、推理速度等工程指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。可能的间接启发：1）合成数据生成与域差距量化方法（GraNatPy的梯度相似度指标）可迁移到遥感场景中合成遥感图像（如模拟卫星视角的舰船/飞机）与真实遥感图像的域差距评估；2）混合真实与合成数据提升小目标检测的思路可用于遥感小目标检测数据增强；3）过程式参数自动优化（SynthClaw）的思路可用于自动化生成遥感目标检测训练数据。但论文本身不涉及轻量化，对在轨部署无直接帮助。

Abstract: Deep learning computer vision for scientific applications requires collecting and annotating large datasets in a laborious, expensive and error-prone process. Synthetic data generation through 3D modelling and rendering may simplify this process and increase the accuracy of annotations by generating them programmatically. However, minimising the domain gap between real and synthetic images visually is subjective and lacks systematic quantitative guidance. We present GraNatPy, a Python package with metrics to guide improvement of the rendered scene. We show that quantifiable increase in realism, diversity and size of rendered dataset correlates with improved visual perception of the scene and higher zero-shot performance of an object detection model. Furthermore, we demonstrated using photographs of virological plaque assays that gradient similarity affects performance on small object detection, which can be improved by mixing real and synthetic data. Finally, we turn procedural data rendering into an agentic skill (SynthClaw) to automate the procedural parameter optimisation.

</details>


### [86] [Inhibited Self-Attention: Sharpening Focus in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12881)
*Peter R. D. van der Wal,Nicola Strisciuglio,George Azzopardi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种抑制性自注意力机制(ISA)，通过保留并利用负注意力分数来抑制背景干扰，增强ViT对目标相关区域的聚焦能力，提升分布外泛化性能。

Research relevance: 2分——弱相关。论文提出的ISA注意力机制属于通用视觉Transformer改进方法，实验在ImageNet-1k和COCO上进行，完全不涉及遥感/卫星场景、遥感目标检测或轻量化部署。虽然注意力机制的改进可能对遥感目标检测有潜在借鉴价值，但论文本身与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向无直接关联。

Reading priority: 2分——与本研究方向弱相关。虽然ISA的注意力抑制机制在理论上对遥感复杂背景下的目标检测有启发意义，但论文本身不涉及遥感场景、不涉及轻量化、不涉及在轨部署，且实验仅在通用数据集上进行。建议作为背景知识阅读，但非核心文献。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：通用图像分类(ImageNet-1k) + 通用目标检测(COCO)；应用场景：通用计算机视觉；传感器类型：自然图像；目标类别：通用物体(非遥感特定目标如舰船/飞机/车辆)。

Model architecture: 基于Vision Transformer (ViT)架构，核心创新是Inhibited Self-Attention (ISA)模块。ISA修改了标准自注意力中的softmax归一化，允许并利用负注意力分数来抑制背景/无关特征的响应，增强对目标相关区域的聚焦。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。ISA作为注意力机制的改进，可能引入额外的计算开销（负值处理），但摘要未提供任何效率指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：ImageNet-1k（分类）、COCO（检测）；评价指标：分类准确率、检测mAP、分布外泛化鲁棒性指标；摘要未说明具体数值。

Experiments: 在ImageNet-1k和COCO上进行了实验，并包含多个鲁棒性benchmark测试。通过相关性图(relevance maps)分析验证了ISA能产生更锐利、更局部化的目标关注区域，减少背景干扰。摘要未提供具体数值对比。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星场景，未在遥感数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR等）上验证；2. 未讨论轻量化或部署可行性；3. 未提供计算开销分析（FLOPs、参数量、推理速度）；4. 仅在分类和检测任务上验证，未涉及旋转框检测、小目标检测等遥感核心挑战；5. 负注意力分数的引入可能增加计算复杂度，但未分析。

Ideas for my research: 1. ISA的抑制机制思路可借鉴到遥感目标检测中：遥感图像背景复杂（云、雾、城市纹理），ISA的抑制背景注意力机制可能有助于提升遥感小目标检测的聚焦能力；2. 可将ISA作为轻量ViT（如MobileViT、EfficientViT）中的注意力模块替换方案，在保持轻量的同时提升目标定位精度；3. 需在遥感数据集（DOTA、DIOR、HRSC2016）上验证ISA对旋转框检测和小目标检测的有效性；4. 需评估ISA引入的计算开销是否适合星上部署（FPGA/NPU）。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) have demonstrated remarkable performance in computer vision tasks. However, their self-attention mechanism often diffuses focus across background regions, relying on spurious correlations rather than object-relevant cues. Inspired by inhibitory mechanisms observed in biological vision systems, we propose the Inhibited Self-Attention (ISA), a novel self-attention that integrates inhibitory signals to enhance feature selectivity and suppress spurious responses. In contrast to conventional self-attention, which relies solely on positive attention values due to softmax normalization, our approach retains and utilizes negative attention scores to suppress irrelevant features and sharpen focus on objects of interest. Experiments across multiple datasets, including ImageNet-1k and COCO, and several robustness benchmarks demonstrate that ISA enhances object-centric selectivity, reduces shortcut reliance, and improves out-of-distribution generalization. Our analysis of relevance maps confirms that ViTs with ISA exhibit sharper, more localized focus on object-relevant regions while reducing distractions from non-relevant (background) features, enabling more reliable models. We release our code at https://github.com/prdvanderwal/inhibited-self-attention

</details>


### [87] [Hy-Embodied-VLM-1.0: Efficient Physical-World Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12894)
*Ziyi Wang,Xumin Yu,Yongming Rao,Yonggen Ling,Yunheng Li,Oran Wang,Mingqi Gao,Yuchen Zhou,Yves Liang,Zuyan Liu,Yani Zhang,Rui Huang,Xiaoran Xu,Bowen Yuan,Yifu Yuan,Xu Tan,He Zhang,Yufei Huang,Shenghao Zhang,Hongsheng Wu,Han Hu,Zhengyou Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向具身智能体的多模态基础模型Hy-Embodied-VLM-1.0，采用MoE架构，3B激活参数达到接近32B模型的性能。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于具身智能体(embodied agents)的多模态感知与交互，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更未提及星上部署或模型轻量化在遥感领域的应用。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：具身智能体多模态理解与推理；应用场景：物理世界中的具身代理(embodied agents)；传感器类型：多模态视觉-语言输入；目标类别：未涉及具体目标检测类别。

Model architecture: 基于Hy3-A3B语言骨干和Hy-ViT2视觉编码器，采用Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)架构，总参数量未说明，激活参数为3B。

Lightweight method: MoE架构实现高效推理（3B激活参数），但未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等传统轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明星上/边缘部署相关信息。提及"latency-sensitive deployment"但未给出具体参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等量化指标。

Datasets and metrics: 38个benchmark，涵盖embodied perception、physical-world understanding、embodied reasoning。具体数据集名称摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在38个benchmark中19个取得同规模模型最佳性能；相比Qwen3.6-A3B和Cosmos 3显著领先；相比上一代Hy-Embodied-0.5 MoT-2B平均提升8.4%；3B激活参数接近32B激活参数模型的性能。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；MoE架构在星上部署的可行性未讨论；未涉及目标检测任务；未提供FLOPs、延迟等部署关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。MoE架构在保持模型容量的同时降低激活参数量的思路，理论上可迁移至遥感目标检测模型，但摘要未提供具体实现细节。若需借鉴，可关注其"action-centric capability taxonomy"的数据构建思路，但与本课题方向关联度低。

Abstract: Building capable embodied agents requires not only multimodal perception and understanding, but also agentic capabilities for reasoning about actions, adapting to evolving situations, and interacting with the physical world. In this report, we introduce Hy-Embodied-VLM-1.0, an efficient and powerful embodied foundation model specifically designed for embodied agents operating in the physical world. To cultivate such capabilities from the pre-training stage onward, we define an action-centric capability taxonomy comprising three progressive dimensions: Action-Relevant State Understanding, Action-Transition Reasoning, and Sequential and Adaptive Reasoning. Guided by this taxonomy, we develop a systematic data pipeline and curate data mixtures spanning both pre-training and post-training. To deliver strong physical-world understanding and interaction capabilities while supporting latency-sensitive deployment, we build our model on the Hy3-A3B language backbone and the Hy-ViT2 vision encoder. Its efficient Mixture-of-Experts architecture combines strong model capacity with high inference efficiency. We evaluate Hy-Embodied-VLM-1.0 on a comprehensive suite of 38 benchmarks covering embodied perception, physical-world understanding, and embodied reasoning. The model achieves the best performance among similarly sized models on 19 of the 38 benchmarks and substantially outperforms strong competitors, including Qwen3.6-A3B and Cosmos 3. Compared with the previous-generation Hy-Embodied-0.5 MoT-2B, Hy-Embodied-VLM-1.0 improves average performance by 8.4%. Despite activating only 3B parameters, it achieves performance close to that of the previous-generation model with 32B activated parameters. Beyond static benchmark evaluation, Hy-Embodied-VLM-1.0 also demonstrates strong performance on embodied agentic tasks requiring multi-turn interaction and long-horizon reasoning.

</details>


### [88] [UniMedSeg: Unified In-Context Learning for Multi-Paradigm 2D/3D Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12896)
*Yunzhou Li,Jiesi Hu,Yanwu Yang,Hanyang Peng,Chenfei Ye,Jianfeng Cao,Yixuan Yuan,Ting Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个面向医学图像分割的统一基础模型UniMedSeg，将视觉上下文、交互式、语言引导等不同范式以及2D/3D图像统一到共享序列空间中，并引入解耦分裂注意力机制降低长序列计算复杂度。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦医学图像分割（medical image segmentation），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及轻量化部署（虽然提出了降低注意力的线性复杂度，但动机是处理长序列而非边缘/星上部署）。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：医学图像分割（visual in-context segmentation, interactive segmentation, language-guided segmentation）；场景：临床医学；传感器类型：CT, MRI等医学影像；目标类别：器官、病灶等医学结构。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 基于Transformer的通用分割框架，将视觉示例、几何交互、语言指令、2D/3D图像映射到共享序列空间；核心模块为Decoupled Split Attention（解耦分裂注意力），将注意力复杂度降至线性。

Lightweight method: 摘要仅提及Decoupled Split Attention降低了注意力计算复杂度（从二次降至线性），但动机是解决长序列记忆瓶颈，并非面向模型轻量化（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。未提及参数量、FLOPs、延迟等部署指标。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。注意力复杂度降至线性对推理效率有潜在帮助，但论文未从边缘/星上部署角度进行讨论。

Datasets and metrics: 从27个公开医学数据集构建的大规模语料库进行训练和评估；评价指标为分割性能（具体指标摘要未说明），在视觉上下文、交互式、语言引导三种分割任务上达到SOTA。

Experiments: 在多种医学分割任务上达到SOTA，无需任务特定微调，在多个held-out任务上展现强泛化性。消融实验摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 完全面向医学领域，与遥感场景无关；2. 未涉及目标检测任务；3. 未提供参数量、FLOPs、推理速度等轻量化关键指标；4. 未讨论在资源受限设备上的部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。可参考的点：1) Decoupled Split Attention将注意力复杂度降至线性的思路，若用于遥感目标检测的Transformer backbone，可能有助于降低计算量；2) 统一多范式输入的设计思想，对遥感中多模态（光学+SAR+高光谱）融合有启发。但整体与在轨遥感目标检测轻量化方向关联度低。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation foundation models are expected to generalize across diverse clinical scenarios, yet existing universal methods remain fragmented by prompt paradigms and spatial dimensions. Visual in-context learning, interactive segmentation, and language-guided segmentation are typically handled by paradigm-specific models, while 2D and 3D images are also modeled separately. Such isolation prevents heterogeneous annotations and data from being jointly absorbed by a single scalable model and limits cross-paradigm knowledge transfer. To address this bottleneck, we propose UniMedSeg, a Transformer-centric universal segmentation framework that maps visual examples, geometric interactions, language instructions, and 2D/3D images into a shared sequence space, enabling heterogeneous medical supervision to be jointly learned through a unified in-context interface without prompt- or dimension-specific branches. To overcome the long-sequence memory bottleneck caused by visual contexts, we introduce Decoupled Split Attention, which reduces attention complexity to linear while preserving hardware-friendly computation and focused context-target interaction. Extensively trained and evaluated on a large corpus curated from 27 public datasets, UniMedSeg achieves state-of-the-art performance across visual in-context, interactive, and language-guided segmentation without task-specific fine-tuning, demonstrating strong generalization on diverse held-out tasks. The code and model weights are publicly available at https://github.com/Lii1228/UniMedSeg

</details>


### [89] [Rank-1 Identity Consensus Predicts Gallery Enrollment in 1:N Face Matching More Accurately than Score Thresholding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12903)
*Gabriella Pangelinan,Aman Bhatta,Michael C. King,Kevin W. Bowyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于多独立匹配器排序一致性（1-consistency）的1:N人脸识别方法，无需设定阈值即可判断待识别者是否在库中，在图像质量退化场景下达到甚至超越理论最优阈值方法的性能。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文研究的是1:N人脸识别中的mate-present/mate-absent判定问题，属于人脸识别/生物特征识别领域，不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测或轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：1:N人脸识别中的开集判定（mate-present vs mate-absent）；场景：执法/安防人脸识别；传感器类型：普通摄像头人脸图像；目标类别：人脸身份。与遥感无关。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。方法基于多个独立训练的matcher（匹配器）的rank-1身份排序一致性，不依赖单一模型结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。本文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性、星上/边缘部署等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 实验设置：36种(gallery, probe quality)场景，涵盖4个质量等级和2个结构轴（每个身份的图像数、总注册身份数）。评价指标：MP recall（mate-present召回率）、MA recall（mate-absent召回率）、正确匹配率（correct mate rate）。未说明具体数据集名称。

Experiments: 对比方法：Fixed Score-Thresholding (FST) 和 Oracle Score-Thresholding (OST)。主要结果：FST在质量退化时MP recall降至2%以下；1-consistency在退化场景下与理论最优OST性能相当；在严重退化下，1-consistency标记为MP时正确匹配率97-100%，而OST仅66-84%。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 方法依赖多个独立训练的matcher，计算开销可能较大，不适合资源受限的星上部署；3. 仅针对人脸识别场景，未验证在遥感目标检测任务上的有效性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但"多模型排序一致性"的思想在理论上可迁移：在星上资源允许时，可用多个轻量模型对遥感目标检测结果进行一致性投票，提高检测置信度。不过这与轻量化（减少计算量）的核心目标相悖，需权衡。

Abstract: In operational 1:N face identification, a crucial question arises for each probe: is this person enrolled in the gallery or not? The stakes are high and asymmetric. Rejecting a mate-present (MP) probe loses a valid lead; accepting a mate-absent (MA) probe makes every returned candidate a false identification, at worst a wrongful arrest. Most approaches threshold match scores, but scores shift substantially with image quality and gallery size and composition, making thresholds fixed before deployment brittle under realistic conditions. Our prior work introduced 1-consistency, the only method based on rank consensus across multiple independently trained matchers: a probe is labeled MP if all matchers return the same rank-1 identity. This work stress-tests 1-consistency across 36 (gallery, probe quality) scenarios spanning four quality levels and two structural axes: images per identity and total enrolled identities. We benchmark against two score-thresholding methods that bracket what any deployed threshold could achieve. Fixed Score-Thresholding (FST), calibrated once on baseline conditions, collapses asymmetrically as quality degrades: MP recall falls below 2% while MA recall holds near 100%. Oracle Score-Thresholding (OST), re-tuned per scenario, is the best any threshold could theoretically do, yet for degraded probes 1-consistency matches it with zero tuning. The two differ mainly in error type (OST favors MP recall, 1-consistency favors MA recall), but on one axis 1-consistency does not merely match the oracle: when it labels a probe MP, it returns the correct mate 97-100% of the time versus OST's 66-84% under severe degradation. In short, 1-consistency delivers oracle-level accuracy without the impossible requirement: it sets no threshold, so it needs no advance knowledge of the conditions a probe will arrive in, which is what makes it usable.

</details>


### [90] [Open-KNEAD: Knowledge-grounded Nutrition Estimation via Agentic Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12911)
*Bruce Coburn,Jingbo Yue,Jinge Ma,Siddeshwar Raghavan,Gautham Vinod,Fengqing Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Open-KNEAD框架，利用检索增强的MLLM进行膳食营养评估，无需训练且可本地部署，但完全与遥感卫星在轨目标检测无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是膳食营养评估（dietary assessment from meal images），属于多模态大语言模型在食品图像理解中的应用，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等方向完全无关。不涉及任何目标检测任务，也不涉及轻量化模型压缩方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：膳食营养评估（meal nutrition estimation）；场景：临床饮食评估；传感器类型：普通餐食图像（meal images）；目标类别：食品类别与营养成分。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 基于MLLM（多模态大语言模型）的agentic框架，包含检索增强接地（retrieval-augmented grounding）、nutrient-aware检索、recipe-prior步骤。具体MLLM架构摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 摘要提到"training-free"和"locally deployable"，但未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。本地部署仅指推理在本地硬件上进行，不涉及模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到"locally hosted inference"和"keep every meal image on local hardware"，但未给出具体性能指标。与星上部署（FPGA/NPU/TensorRT）完全无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：ACETADA（经营养师验证）、另外两个开放MLLM家族和三种菜系的数据；评价指标：portion estimates准确度（与ground truth比较）。与遥感数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR、NWPU VHR-10等）无关。

Experiments: Open-KNEAD在大多数backbone-dataset设置下优于先前的接地方法和直接估计方法；在ACETADA数据集上，本地开放agent比两个前沿闭源模型的直接portion估计分别高出约30%和53%。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感相关技术或方法。研究场景（餐食图像）与遥感图像（卫星/航空影像）的成像机理、目标尺度、背景复杂度差异巨大。

Ideas for my research: 基本无可借鉴点。虽然"training-free"和"locally deployable"的概念在星上部署中也有价值，但该论文的方法高度依赖MLLM和食品知识库（FNDDS），与遥感目标检测的轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏等）路径完全不同。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly used for dietary assessment from meal images, where retrieval-augmented grounding was shown to sharpen nutrition estimates. However, we find this premise no longer holds for current MLLMs. A modern MLLM's direct estimate now matches or surpasses the full retrieval pipeline. This raises a question: if retrieval no longer improves the overall estimate, can it still deliver the two things clinicians value, accurate portions and a traceable, item-by-item record? We pursue this while preserving what matters for clinical adoption: minimal user burden (a single, unannotated meal image), explainability (an auditable record), and privacy (locally hosted inference). We introduce Open-KNEAD, a knowledge-grounded agentic framework for meal nutrition estimation that is training-free and locally deployable. Each decomposed food item is grounded to a Food and Nutrient Database for Dietary Studies (FNDDS) code via selective, nutrient-aware retrieval, composing an auditable per-item record. Across two open MLLM families and three cuisines, Open-KNEAD improves portion estimates over both prior grounding methods and direct estimation in most backbone-dataset settings. An agent-internal recipe-prior step further recovers the invisible cooking-added energy that biases estimates on non-US cuisine. The advantage is largest on the dietitian-verified ACETADA dataset, where the local open agent surpasses the direct portion estimates of two frontier closed models by roughly $30\%$ and $53\%$, all while keeping every meal image on local hardware. We release the Open-KNEAD framework and its agent-ready FNDDS knowledge base.

</details>


### [91] [Domain-Incremental Remote Sensing Change Detection via Difference-Guided Adaptation and Frequency-Decoupled Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12934)
*Daifeng Peng,Yaning Li,Haiyan Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DG-FDD框架，通过差异引导适配和频域解耦蒸馏解决遥感变化检测模型在域增量学习中的灾难性遗忘问题。

Research relevance: 2分——弱相关。论文涉及遥感（remote sensing change detection），但任务类型是变化检测（change detection），而非目标检测（object detection）；方法涉及知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation），但蒸馏目的是缓解灾难性遗忘（catastrophic forgetting），而非模型轻量化或加速推理；不涉及在轨部署、轻量化、实时推理等核心方向。

Reading priority: 2分——弱相关。论文聚焦遥感变化检测的域增量学习问题，与遥感目标检测任务不同，且不涉及任何轻量化或部署技术。仅当课题涉及遥感模型跨域泛化+蒸馏的交叉方向时，可作为辅助参考。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：遥感变化检测（RSCD）；场景：域增量学习（domain-incremental learning），跨域连续学习；传感器类型：高分辨率遥感影像（high-resolution RSCD datasets）；目标类别：变化区域（change regions），非具体目标类别（如舰船/飞机/车辆）。

Model architecture: DG-FDD框架包含两个核心模块：(1) Difference-Guided Dynamic Adapter (DGDA)——建模双时相特征差异，实现变化感知的域适配；(2) Frequency-Decoupled Knowledge Distillation with Cross-domain Synthesis (FDKD-CS)——在频域分离结构信息与域风格，实现无历史数据的稳定知识迁移。

Lightweight method: 涉及知识蒸馏（Knowledge Distillation），但蒸馏目的是域增量学习中的知识保留（缓解灾难性遗忘），而非模型压缩或轻量化。未涉及剪枝、量化、NAS、轻量网络设计等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。论文关注的是域泛化能力而非部署效率，不涉及星上/边缘部署可行性分析。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：三个公开高分辨率RSCD数据集（具体名称摘要未说明）；实验协议：两域和三域增量协议（two- and three-domain incremental protocols）；评价指标：F1-score和IoU（Intersection over Union）。

Experiments: 在六组两域序列上，相比独立训练的单任务模型，F1和IoU的平均相对变化仅为-0.23%和-0.45%；在三组三域序列上，分别为-0.69%和-1.31%。表明模型在历史知识保留和新域适应之间取得了良好的稳定性-可塑性平衡。

Limitations: (1) 任务为变化检测而非目标检测，与课题核心任务不匹配；(2) 未涉及任何轻量化技术（剪枝、量化、NAS等）；(3) 未报告模型效率指标（参数量、FLOPs、推理速度等）；(4) 未讨论在轨/边缘部署可行性；(5) 数据集名称未明确给出，可复现性受限。

Ideas for my research: (1) 频域解耦蒸馏（FDKD）的思想可借鉴：将遥感图像的结构信息与域风格在频域分离，这一思路可用于遥感目标检测模型在跨域场景下的知识蒸馏，但需将蒸馏目标从"缓解遗忘"改为"模型压缩"；(2) 差异引导适配（DGDA）中建模双时相特征差异的思路，可类比到遥感目标检测中不同传感器/分辨率下的特征对齐；(3) 但整体而言，该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接可借鉴点有限，不建议作为核心参考。

Abstract: Remote sensing change detection (RSCD) models are prone to catastrophic forgetting when incrementally adapted to new domains. Existing domain-incremental learning (DIL) methods mainly preserve image-level representations but often overlook bitemporal discrepancy cues, which are critical for robust change detection under domain shifts. To address this limitation, we propose DG-FDD, a domain-incremental change detection framework that integrates Difference-Guided Adaptation and Frequency-Decoupled Distillation. Specifically, the Difference-Guided Dynamic Adapter (DGDA) models bitemporal feature discrepancies to promote change-aware feature adaptation and reduce domain-specific interference. Meanwhile, the Frequency-Decoupled Knowledge Distillation strategy with Cross-domain Synthesis (FDKD-CS) separates structural information from domain style in the frequency domain, enabling stable knowledge transfer without historical data. Extensive experiments on three public high-resolution RSCD datasets under two- and three-domain incremental protocols demonstrate that DG-FDD effectively mitigates catastrophic forgetting.
