Compared with independently trained single-task models, DG-FDD records mean relative changes in F1 and IoU of only -0.23% and -0.45%, respectively, across six two-domain sequences, and -0.69% and -1.31%, respectively, across the three evaluated three-domain sequences. These results indicate a favorable stability-plasticity balance between historical knowledge retention and new-domain adaptation in continual cross-domain change detection.

</details>


### [92] [Point Tracking in Surgery--The 2025 Surgical Tattoos in Infrared Challenge (STIRC2025)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12939)
*Adam Schmidt,Mert Asim Karaoglu,Zijian Wu,Jiaming Zhang,Yuxin Chen,Tim Salcudean,Ho-Gun Ha,Minkang Jang,Kyungmin Jung,Ihsan Ullah,Hyunki Lee,Suresh Guttikonda,Sarah Latus,Alexander Schlaefer,Xinkai Zhao,Yuichiro Hayashi,Masahiro Oda,Takayuki Kitasaka,Kensaku Mori,Peng Liu,Chenyang Li,Stefanie Speidel,Aoife Gardiner,Agostino Stilli,Danail Stoyanov,Francisco Vasconcelos,Anwesa Choudhuri,Meng Zheng,Zhongpai Gao,Benjamin Planche,Van Nguyen Nguyen,Terrence Chen,Ziyan Wu,Alexander Ladikos,Omid Mohareri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍2025年外科手术点跟踪挑战赛（STIRC2025），聚焦手术视频中的点跟踪算法精度与推理延迟，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化无关。

Research relevance: 1分，基本无关。论文研究的是**外科手术视频中的点跟踪（point tracking）**，属于医学图像分析领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能没有任何关联。不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测等遥感任务，也不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏等模型压缩方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：外科手术点跟踪（point tracking）；应用场景：手术导航、组织标记、自主探针扫描；传感器类型：红外（infrared）手术视频；目标类别：手术组织上的标记点（tattoos）。与遥感场景完全无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及效率组件测试算法推理延迟，但未给出具体数值。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：STIR（surgical tattoos in infrared），包含in vivo和ex vivo序列；评价指标：精度（accuracy）和效率（inference latency）。与遥感常用数据集（DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR、NWPU VHR-10、SSDD、SARDet等）无关。

Experiments: 7支队伍参赛，论文总结挑战结果和参与者方法，但摘要未给出具体实验结果数值。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无可借鉴的遥感相关结论或方法。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于医学图像分析中的手术点跟踪任务，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化的研究主题不相关。

Abstract: Point tracking in surgery is crucial to enable applications in downstream tasks such as segmentation, 3D reconstruction, virtual tissue landmarking, autonomous probe-based scanning, and subtask autonomy. This paper introduces the 2025 iteration of a point tracking challenge to address this, wherein participants submit their algorithms for quantification. Their algorithms are evaluated using a dataset named surgical tattoos in infrared (STIR), with the challenge named the STIR Challenge 2025 (STIRC2025). The STIR Challenge 2025 comprises two quantitative components: accuracy and efficiency. The accuracy component tests the accuracy of algorithms on in vivo and ex vivo sequences. The efficiency component tests algorithm inference latency. The challenge was conducted as a part of MICCAI EndoVis 2025, and seven teams participated in this challenge. In this paper we summarize the challenge results and participant methods. The challenge dataset is available at: https://zenodo.org/records/20191078, and the code for baseline models and metrics calculation is available here: https://github.com/athaddius/STIRMetrics

</details>


### [93] [ViCo3D: Empowering LiDAR-based Collaborative 3D Object Detection with Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12959)
*Haojie Ren,Songrui Luo,Lingfeng Wang,Yan Xia,Yao Li,Jing Li,Lu Zhang,Jiajun Deng,Yanyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ViCo3D框架，将视觉基础模型（VFM）DINOv2适配到LiDAR点云的BEV表示中，用于V2X协同3D目标检测，通过多尺度BEV融合和自车为中心跨智能体融合策略提升检测性能。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。原因：(1) 传感器为LiDAR（激光雷达），非遥感卫星常用的光学/多光谱/高光谱/SAR传感器；(2) 场景为V2X车联网协同感知，非遥感/卫星/Earth observation场景；(3) 任务为3D目标检测，非遥感中的2D目标检测/旋转框检测/小目标检测；(4) 未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）；(5) 未涉及星上/边缘部署相关指标。虽然使用了视觉基础模型（VFM）这一通用技术，但整体方向与遥感在轨轻量化目标检测差距较大。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非对VFM适配非图像模态的技术路线感兴趣，可作为跨领域参考。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：协同3D目标检测（collaborative 3D object detection）；场景：V2X（Vehicle-to-Everything）车联网；传感器：LiDAR（激光雷达）；目标类别：车辆等3D目标（摘要未明确列出具体类别）。

Model architecture: ViCo3D框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 将点云投影到BEV平面作为三通道图像，适配DINOv2（视觉基础模型）提取BEV空间视觉特征；(2) 多尺度BEV融合模块（multi-scale BEV fusion module），集成DINOv2特征与LiDAR几何特征；(3) 自车为中心跨智能体融合策略（ego-centric cross-agent fusion strategy），聚合多智能体互补信息。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法。未提及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。使用DINOv2（视觉基础模型）通常参数量较大，不利于边缘/星上部署，但摘要未给出具体数值。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：DAIR-V2X、V2XSet；评价指标：3D检测性能（具体指标如AP/mAP等摘要未明确说明）。

Experiments: 在DAIR-V2X和V2XSet上达到SOTA 3D检测性能；在DAIR-V2X上协同增益比先前方法高出1.8倍。摘要未提供消融实验、对比方法具体数值等详细信息。

Limitations: (1) 与遥感卫星方向完全无关，传感器模态（LiDAR vs 光学/SAR）和应用场景（V2X vs 遥感）差异巨大；(2) 使用DINOv2等大模型，计算开销大，未考虑轻量化；(3) 未提供任何部署相关指标；(4) 仅适用于V2X协同场景，无法直接迁移到遥感在轨场景。

Ideas for my research: 虽然整体方向不相关，但以下思路可能有借鉴意义：(1) 将视觉基础模型（VFM）适配到非自然图像模态的思路——可尝试将遥感图像特征通过某种投影方式适配到预训练VFM中，利用其通用语义先验提升遥感目标检测性能；(2) 多尺度BEV融合模块的设计思路可借鉴到遥感多尺度特征融合中；(3) 跨智能体融合策略的思想可类比到多星协同在轨感知场景。但需注意：VFM通常参数量大、推理慢，与轻量化需求矛盾，需配合蒸馏/量化等方法使用。

Abstract: LiDAR-based collaborative 3D perception in Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) systems typically relies on fusing bird's-eye-view (BEV) features across agents. However, current BEV representations, typically extracted by LiDAR backbones trained from scratch, are geometry-dominated and lack general semantic priors, inherently limiting the efficacy of feature-level collaboration. Meanwhile, vision foundation models (VFMs) pretrained on large-scale image data have demonstrated strong capability in learning general-purpose and informative visual representations for 2D tasks, and have the potential to enhance agent-wise LiDAR BEV representations for collaboration. Despite this potential, adapting VFMs to LiDAR-based 3D detection remains challenging due to the substantial image-point cloud modality gap. To bridge this gap, we propose ViCo3D, a collaborative 3D object detection framework powered by VFMs. Specifically, ViCo3D adapts VFMs to LiDAR-based collaborative perception from three aspects: First, ViCo3D projects point clouds onto the BEV plane as three-channel images, enabling DINOv2 to extract BEV-space visual features from LiDAR inputs. Besides, to effectively integrate these DINOv2-derived features with LiDAR geometric features, ViCo3D introduces a multi-scale BEV fusion module within the single-agent encoder. In addition, ViCo3D adopts an ego-centric cross-agent fusion strategy to aggregate complementary information from multiple agents. Experiments on DAIR-V2X and V2XSet demonstrate that ViCo3D achieves state-of-the-art 3D detection performance. Remarkably, it delivers up to 1.8x greater collaborative gains than prior methods on DAIR-V2X. The code will be made public available for future investigation.

</details>


### [94] [Controllable Generation of Diverse Dermatological Imagery for Fair and Efficient Malignancy Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12987)
*Héctor Carrión,Narges Norouzi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个可控皮肤病图像生成框架cgDDI，通过合成多样化皮肤样本解决医学图像中肤色和罕见病种的数据不均衡问题，提升诊断公平性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：皮肤病恶性分类（malignancy classification）；场景：医学影像诊断；传感器类型：皮肤镜/临床图像；目标类别：皮肤病变（良性/恶性）。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: cgDDI混合框架，包含三部分：(1)健康皮肤样本合成模块，(2)单样本罕见病变到新肤色/位置的非参数映射模块，(3)高效参数化生成模块（仅需10个训练样本）。支持人工和自动分割掩码。摘要未说明具体网络结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络、实时推理、边缘/星上部署等任何轻量化或模型压缩方法。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：DDI（Diverse Dermatology Images，活检确认）、Fitzpatrick17k（专家验证）；指标：恶性分类准确率（accuracy）、公平性指标（fairness metrics）；数据规模：原始656张图像，合成扩展400倍以上，公开释放266k+合成图像。

Experiments: DDI基准上：纯合成训练86.4%准确率，真实数据微调后达90.9% SOTA；跨数据集实验：在未见过的F17k上准确率提升+13.9%。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或目标检测方法。该论文是医学图像生成/数据增强工作。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。唯一可能的间接启发：数据增强思想（如合成多样化遥感图像以解决数据稀缺问题），但论文本身不涉及遥感场景或目标检测任务，且不涉及任何轻量化技术。

Abstract: Accurate dermatological diagnosis naturally necessitates equitable performance across diverse populations, yet a systematic lack of expertly annotated images, especially for underrepresented skin tones and rare diseases, impedes progress toward measurably fair methods. We introduce cgDDI (Controllable Generation of Diverse Dermatological Imagery), a hybrid framework that (1) synthesizes realistic healthy skin samples without disturbing other input properties, (2) maps single-sample rare lesions onto novel skin-tones and locations non-parametrically, and (3) allows for efficient parametric generation with as few as 10 training samples. The framework supports both human and automated segmentation masking, enabling scalability to datasets without pre-made lesion masks. We grow a 656-image dataset by more than 400x and validate across two datasets: biopsy-confirmed Diverse Dermatology Images (DDI) and expert-verified Fitzpatrick17k (F17k). On the DDI benchmark, we achieve malignancy classification accuracy of 86.4% under synthetic-only training and 90.9% state-of-the-art performance with real data fine-tuning, alongside leading fairness metrics. Cross-dataset experiments show +13.9% accuracy improvements on unseen F17k data despite minimal disease overlap. We openly release 266k+ synthetic images, code, and generative models to further support fairness research at https://github.com/hectorcarrion/ControllableGenDDI.

</details>


### [95] [X-Lens: Real-Time Metric Depth Estimation with Heterogeneous Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12993)
*Heng Zhou,Shuhong Liu,Yonghao He,Bohao Zhang,Fa Fu,Chenhui Hou,Xianbao Hou,Lijun Han,Wei Sui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: X-lens是一个紧凑的前馈模型，用于从可变数量的鱼眼和针孔视图进行度量深度估计，参数量仅0.04B，速度达41 FPS。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。该论文研究的是多视角度量深度估计（metric depth estimation），使用鱼眼和针孔相机，面向自动驾驶/机器人等通用视觉场景，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、航空图像、在轨智能等遥感核心要素。也不涉及目标检测任务。虽然模型轻量（0.04B参数、41 FPS），但轻量化不是本文核心贡献方向，且应用场景与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：度量深度估计（metric depth estimation）；场景：室内/室外通用场景（自动驾驶/机器人）；传感器：鱼眼相机（fisheye）和针孔相机（pinhole）；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。

Model architecture: 紧凑前馈模型（compact feed-forward model），基于几何感知异构相机公式（geometry-aware heterogeneous camera formulation），包含两个关键组件：可学习校准token（learnable calibration tokens）和雅可比参数化畸变偏置（Jacobian-parameterized distortion bias）注入交叉注意力机制。

Lightweight method: 模型本身设计为紧凑结构（compact feed-forward model），参数量仅0.04B（约4000万），但未使用剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等典型轻量化方法。轻量是模型设计的结果而非方法。

Onboard deployability: 参数量0.04B，推理速度达41 FPS，具有实时推理能力。但摘要未说明FLOPs、显存、能耗、延迟等详细部署指标，且未涉及FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等边缘/星上部署方案。场景为通用视觉而非星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 训练数据：多个公开数据集 + OmniScene（新发布的大规模合成数据集，约266K同步六视角帧、1.7M单张图像、103个室内外场景）。评价指标：AbsRel（绝对相对误差），在OmniScene-Full上比最强基线降低25.4% AbsRel，同时参数量减少88.9%。

Experiments: 在真实和合成的室内外数据集上进行实验，在异构相机度量深度估计上表现优越，在传统鱼眼-only和针孔-only设置上也具有竞争力。摘要未说明消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星场景；2. 不涉及目标检测任务；3. 不涉及在轨部署或边缘部署；4. 轻量化仅为模型设计附带结果，非核心贡献；5. 依赖合成数据集OmniScene，真实遥感场景泛化能力未知。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。可参考的点：1）0.04B参数+41 FPS的紧凑设计思路，说明在有限资源下仍可实现实时推理；2）可学习校准token的设计思路或可迁移到多模态遥感数据（如可见光+SAR）的对齐问题；3）但整体方向（深度估计、鱼眼/针孔相机、通用场景）与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题差异较大，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: We present X-lens, a compact feed-forward model for metric depth estimation from a variable number of calibrated fisheye and pinhole views. To support real-time downstream perception, X-lens is built around a geometry-aware heterogeneous camera formulation with two key components. Learnable calibration tokens provide a coarse alignment between fisheye and pinhole projective spaces, while a Jacobian-parameterized distortion bias injected into cross-attention models local projection changes and promotes cross-camera consistency, enabling robust generalization with only 0.04B parameters and up to 41 FPS. The model predicts dense depth together with a global metric scale, avoiding auxiliary reconstruction targets that increase computation and optimization complexity. To learn such cross-camera generalization at scale and depth, X-lens is trained on multiple public datasets and OmniScene, our newly released large-scale synthetic dataset containing approximately 266K synchronized six-view frames, 1.7M individual images, and 103 indoor and outdoor scenes. Extensive experiments on both real-world and synthetic indoor and outdoor datasets demonstrate superior heterogeneous-camera metric depth accuracy, reducing AbsRel by 25.4\% on OmniScene-Full over the strongest baseline while using 88.9\% fewer parameters, with competitive performance on conventional fisheye-only and pinhole-only settings.

</details>


### [96] [DermDepth: Toward Monocular Metric Scale 3D Reconstruction Models for Dermatology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13010)
*Héctor Carrión,Narges Norouzi*

TL;DR: 提出首个面向皮肤病学领域的单视图度量尺度3D重建模型DermDepth，利用合成数据集D-Synth实现从2D图像恢复3D皮肤表面几何与纹理，无需额外硬件。

Research relevance: 1分，基本无关。该论文聚焦于皮肤病学（dermatology）领域的单视图3D重建，属于医学图像分析范畴，与遥感卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等方向无任何关联。不涉及目标检测，也不涉及轻量化或边缘部署。

Experiments: 在D-Synth上训练DermDepth，将真实皮肤镜数据的度量尺度误差从>16x校正至<1.1x；在少量真实临床样本上微调后泛化至三个真实基准；测量结果与医学文献报告的病灶大小基本一致。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关；2. 未涉及任何目标检测任务；3. 未涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法；4. 未讨论实时推理或边缘部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与本人研究方向基本无关，无可借鉴点。唯一可提及的是合成数据（synthetic dataset）用于解决真实标注稀缺的思路，但这一思路在遥感领域（如合成遥感图像用于目标检测训练）已有广泛研究，且该论文的3D重建任务与遥感目标检测任务差异巨大。

Abstract: Dermatological practice routinely involves measuring and tracking lesion size, morphology and texture, as critical components of wound or skin cancer screening, monitoring and diagnosis. To accomplish this task, practitioners often image the skin surface with commonly available off-the-shelf camera sensors. This has led to an overwhelming research focus on 2D methods while these objectives naturally benefit from 3D information. In this paper, we demonstrate that dense monocular 3D reconstructions, metric scale measurements and rich surface normal texture estimates are achievable for both dermoscopic and macroscopic cases without the need for additional hardware or multiple captures. We present DermDepth, the first single-view metric scale 3D model for the dermatological domain and D-Synth, the first synthetic dermoscopic dataset with pixel-perfect 3D information. Our experiments show training DermDepth on D-Synth corrects metric scale error from over 16x to under 1.1x for real dermoscopic data, while preserving geometric quality and increasing texture richness. Fine-tuning on a small amount of real clinical samples generalizes our method across three real-world benchmarks spanning the few mm to hundred cm range, diverse skin-tones, chronic wound cases and produces measurements broadly consistent with disease size reported in medical literature. All code, data and models are available at https://github.com/hectorcarrion/dermdepth.

</details>

Abstract: We develop a rigorous theoretical framework for optimal market making in perpetual futures markets with zero maker fees. We model the market maker's problem as a stochastic optimal control problem on a filtered probability space, where the controls are adaptive bid-ask spreads and inventory hedging decisions across two exchanges. Our contributions include: (i) a PnL decomposition theorem separating revenue into spread income, adverse selection loss, inventory carrying cost, hedging friction, and funding rate exposure; (ii) the Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation for the joint spread-inventory-hedging control problem under CARA utility with a verification theorem; (iii) High-APY Regime Theorems characterizing profitable regions via five dimensionless parameters, culminating in a Master APY Formula; (iv) analysis of zero-fee economics on decentralized perpetual exchanges with optimal entry-exit thresholds; (v) optimal cross-exchange hedging policies with funding rate dynamics and a hedge regime trichotomy; (vi) a robustness margin quantifying parameter uncertainty tolerance; (vii) exponential drawdown probability bounds and a universal APY-VaR identity; (viii) ergodic inventory distribution under optimal control with Bayesian adaptive estimation; (ix) Kelly-optimal leverage with ruin boundaries; and (x) multi-pair portfolio allocation with diversification saturation results. Numerical analysis with twenty-three figures reveals phase transitions between profitable and unprofitable regimes. Our framework unifies and extends the Avellaneda-Stoikov, Gueant-Lehalle-Fernandez-Tapia, and Glosten-Milgrom paradigms for modern decentralized venue microstructure.

</details>


### [98] [In-Context Reinforcement Learning under Non-Stationarity: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11906)
*A Run,Ziluo Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文综述非平稳环境下的上下文强化学习（ICRL），探讨智能体如何在不更新参数的情况下，通过上下文窗口适应环境变化，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与轻量化方向无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感相关概念；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测等检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署等模型压缩或部署技术。论文属于强化学习（RL）领域的综述，聚焦于in-context reinforcement learning (ICRL)在非平稳环境下的适应问题，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及遥感场景或目标检测任务，属于强化学习领域的理论综述。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及星上部署、边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文为综述性质，未提及具体数据集或评价指标。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于强化学习领域的综述，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化课题的研究范式、技术路线和应用场景均不相关。

Abstract: The development of decision-pretrained transformers, algorithm distillation, long-context meta-RL, and retrieval-augmented agents has renewed interest in in-context reinforcement learning (ICRL): the ability of a pretrained or fine-tuned decision model to infer latent task rules and improve future behavior from interaction context, without test-time parameter updates. This line of work asks when trial-and-error evidence, rewards, transitions, demonstrations, feedback, or retrieved experience can make learning-like computation happen inside the context window. However, existing surveys of ICRL mainly organize the field around pretraining objectives, architectures, context formats, evaluation protocols, and theoretical mechanisms, while the non-stationary setting remains comparatively underexamined. In changing environments, accumulated context is not merely more evidence about a fixed task: the reward specification, transition kernel, observation channel, action interface, constraint model, or demonstration and memory distribution can fall out of alignment with the current regime. Previously useful context can therefore become stale, misleading, or useful again when an old regime returns. We survey non-stationary ICRL as the problem of adapting through context while deployed policy parameters remain fixed: the policy must infer both the current decision rule and which parts of its accumulated evidence still support that rule. We define non-stationary ICRL, relate it to meta-RL, decision sequence modeling, retrieval-augmented RL, value- and model-aware ICRL, and reward-feedback agents, and organize the literature along three questions: what changes, how the change unfolds, and how observable the change is to the agent.

</details>


### [99] [Ontology-Amplified Distillation and Contextuality Auditing for Sovereign Enterprise Language Models: A Combined Proof-of-Mechanism and Negative-Results Method Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11948)
*Thanh Luong Tuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究金融监管场景下基于本体蒸馏的租户专属语言模型，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。论文主题是金融监管机构内的租户专属大语言模型（LLM）蒸馏与本体对齐，涉及Qwen3.6-27B学生模型、GPT-5教师、DPO、上下文审计等NLP技术。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、遥感场景理解；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏（虽然用了"distillation"一词，但面向LLM而非视觉检测模型）、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等。唯一可能相关的"distillation"概念也完全面向NLP领域，与视觉检测模型压缩无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大语言模型（LLM）的本体蒸馏与对齐；应用场景：金融监管机构数据驻留合规；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 教师模型：GPT-5 frontier；学生模型：Qwen3.6-27B；训练方法：监督微调（SFT）+ 本体对齐直接偏好优化（DPO）。

Lightweight method: 摘要中提到的"distillation"是LLM领域的知识蒸馏（从GPT-5蒸馏到Qwen3.6-27B），并非面向遥感目标检测模型的轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文讨论的是金融机构内部部署，而非星上/边缘部署。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：47个合成英语跨领域偏好对，40个保留的越南语金融领域任务；评价指标：grounded rate（0.90）、本体术语覆盖率r_onto（0.95，下限0.50）、配对差异95%置信区间（±4个任务）。

Experiments: 蒸馏学生模型在40个越南语金融任务中ground 36个（grounded rate 0.90），与GPT-5基线持平（36/40）。但统计功效不足，无法证明等价性或超越性。上下文审计实验显示规范化的Contextuality-by-Default度量为零，无显著上下文效应。

Limitations: 摘要明确承认：统计功效不足（underpowered），无法建立等价性；未验证预注册的amplification预测（学生应超越教师）；上下文审计结果为阴性（negative-results pilot）。与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。本文是NLP领域的LLM蒸馏研究，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在任务领域、传感器模态、部署约束、评价体系上均无交集。不建议参考。

Abstract: Regulated financial institutions operating under data-residency rules need tenant-owned language models that can run inside the institution's perimeter. This paper combines two related FAOS studies into one mechanism-and-control article. First, it reports a reduced-power proof-of-mechanism study of ontology-amplified distillation: a Qwen3.6-27B student is adapted to the Foundation AgenticOS ontology through supervised fine-tuning on frontier-teacher trajectories and ontology-grounded direct preference optimization (DPO), trained locally on a single Apple M5 Max from 47 synthetic, English-language, cross-domain preference pairs. On 40 held-out Vietnamese financial-domain tasks, the distilled student grounds 36 of 40 tasks (grounded rate 0.90; mean ontology term-coverage r_onto = 0.95 on a metric floored at 0.50), equal to the GPT-5 frontier baseline, which also grounds 36 of 40. The outcome is underpowered to establish equivalence: the paired-difference 95% confidence interval spans +/-4 tasks, and the run does not test or show the pre-registered amplification prediction that the student should exceed the frontier. Second, the paper consolidates a contextuality-audit method for enterprise-agent routing. In a separate negative-results pilot, the corrected canonical Contextuality-by-Default degree is zero for all Phase 1.3 groups in both the local-Qwen run and an explicitly labeled Gemma replication check; the useful signal is direct influence and construct coupling, not surviving residual contextuality. Together, the studies pair an ontology-grounded model-building mechanism with a governance diagnostic for deciding when apparent disagreement should trigger prompt standardization, multi-agent synthesis, or human review. The evidence supports neither deployability, safety, superiority, statistical equivalence, nor a contextuality-positive routing rule.

</details>


### [100] [GRID: Grammar-Railed Decoding for Enterprise SQL Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11951)
*Mohsen Arjmandi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GRID（Grammar-Railed Decoding）引擎，利用LALR(1)解析器状态驱动token掩码生成，实现带形式化保证的语法约束解码，用于企业级SQL生成场景。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于大语言模型生成SQL时的语法约束解码问题，属于自然语言处理与数据库交叉领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：语法约束解码（grammar-constrained decoding）；场景：企业级SQL生成；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: GRID引擎基于LALR(1)解析器状态（lexer scan state × LALR(1) stack）作为缓存键，利用增量推进的LALR(1)解析器作为可行前缀预言器；LLM token通过字节级trie行走桥接到语法终结符，采用上下文无关/上下文相关拆分保证缓存键正确性；Rust内核实现。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文讨论的是企业级SQL部署场景，与星上/边缘部署无关。但提供了per-token掩码延迟中位数3.6-6.7μs（Rust内核），以及per-token开销在n=16,000时位置平坦等性能数据。

Datasets and metrics: Spider数据集；评价指标：执行准确率（execution accuracy）；per-token掩码延迟（中位数、p90）；零误拒率（zero false rejects）。

Experiments: 在Spider上，约束解码在0.5B模型上带来+13个执行准确率百分点；通过一次检查器引导的修复pass（针对掩码无法强制执行的列级策略），将7B模型提升至94.5%可执行率。

Limitations: 摘要明确说明了掩码无法做到的三件事：分布忠实性（distribution faithfulness）、列级RBAC、非LALR(1)语言；以及测量成本仍存在的方面。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但以下思路可抽象迁移：（1）利用解析器状态而非token序列作为缓存键的思想，在遥感模型部署中若涉及结构化输出约束（如检测框格式、类别标签语法）时可能有参考价值；（2）形式化保证（soundness, completeness, termination）的思维方式可用于星上推理的可靠性验证；（3）Rust内核实现低延迟的思路对星上FPGA/NPU部署的延迟优化有启发，但具体方法不直接相关。

Abstract: Large language models can write SQL, but enterprise deployment demands more than plausible text: outputs must be syntactically valid, must respect per-role and per-schema policy, must carry provable (not best-effort) guarantees, must not slow down as generations grow, and must leave a compliance-grade record of every decision. We present GRID (Grammar-Railed Decoding), a grammar-constrained decoding engine that keys exact next-token masks on parser configurations (lexer scan state x LALR(1) stack) rather than on token sequences, and uses the incrementally advanced LALR(1) parser itself as a viable-prefix oracle. LLM tokens are bridged to grammar terminals by a byte-level trie walk with a context-independent/context-dependent split that makes cache-key soundness hold by construction. Role-based access control is compiled into the language: role projections subset the grammar's productions and schema lexicons restrict identifier terminals, so forbidden verbs and identifiers are unreachable at mask level. Four guarantees (soundness, completeness, termination, and near-constant per-token cost) are stated with explicit preconditions and each paired with a test or benchmark. Rust kernels bring the per-token mask to a 3.6-6.7 us median, ahead of llguidance at p50 and p90 on two tokenizers with zero false rejects; per-token guard cost is position-flat at n=16,000. On Spider, constrained decoding is worth +13 execution-accuracy points at 0.5B, and one checker-guided repair pass over the provably mask-unenforceable residue (column-level policy) lifts a 7B model to 94.5% executable. A hash-chained per-token audit trail replays bit-identically with 100% tamper detection. We state plainly what the mask cannot do (distribution faithfulness, column-level RBAC, non-LALR(1) languages) and where measured cost remains.

</details>


### [101] [Calibration-First Reward-Component Auditing for Reinforcement Learning Control in Smart Greenhouses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11959)
*Yuhui Bie,Guowei Xu,Yaojun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种温室强化学习的可复现校准优先奖励审计框架GreenLight-Gym，用于分解标量奖励并跨模拟器训练、设施部署、竞赛记录和规则蒸馏保持可比性。

Research relevance: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于温室气候控制的强化学习奖励审计框架，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：温室气候控制的强化学习奖励审计与可复现性评估。应用场景：智能温室（smart-greenhouse）。传感器类型：温度、CO2、湿度、蒸汽压差等气候传感器。目标类别：无目标检测相关内容。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体的模型架构。提出的是奖励审计框架GreenLight-Gym，而非检测或分类模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。虽然提到"actuator-rule distillation"，但这是指将强化学习策略蒸馏为可解释规则，属于可解释性方法，而非模型轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：使用GreenLight模拟器及第二届自主温室挑战赛（Autonomous Greenhouse Challenge）的气候轨迹记录。评价指标：分解后的条件温度、CO2、湿度、蒸汽压差、遮阳帘、执行代理等奖励分量。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体的实验结果和对比方法。框架在模拟器训练、设施部署、竞赛记录和规则蒸馏四个场景下保持奖励分量可比性。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向上的任何技术信息或借鉴点。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Greenhouse reinforcement learning can test climate-control ideas at a speed and scale that is difficult to achieve with crop experiments alone. For smart-greenhouse control, however, a single simulator return is not enough: a grower or control engineer also needs to know when the policy heats, enriches CO2, vents, manages humidity, deploys screens, or uses lamps.We propose a reproducible calibration-first reward audit framework that keeps named greenhouse-control reward components comparable across simulator training, facility-adapted rollouts, logged Autonomous Greenhouse Challenge records, and actuator-rule distillation. In GreenLight-Gym, the framework decomposes the scalar reward into conditional temperature, CO2, humidity and vapor-pressure-deficit, screen, and actuation-proxy terms; adapts GreenLight to the second Autonomous Greenhouse Challenge logged climate traces; and scores the same components on logged greenhouse data.

</details>


### [102] [Optimization Is Not All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11977)
*Minh Hua,Rita Raley*

Lightweight method: 不适用。论文不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不适用。论文完全不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 不适用。论文未提及任何遥感或目标检测数据集及评价指标。

Experiments: 不适用。论文未提供任何实验对比或消融实验结果。

Limitations: 该论文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何技术性借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于AI伦理与语言技术批判研究，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何技术交集。

Abstract: In 2019, OpenAI released two million GPT-2 outputs-ungrammatical, half broken-to aid the detection of machine-generated text. The alignment that produced their more fluent successors is usually regarded as an engineering achievement; we read it instead as the newest expression of optimization culture: the conviction, older than the technology, that measurable improvement along predefined axes exhausts the question of value. Tracing that conviction through the stack-pretraining, decoding, preference tuning, benchmarking, interface-and back through its genealogy in the audit society, we arrive at the limit: an optimization procedure can measure how improbable a piece of generated text is; it cannot tell whether that unlikelihood is error or invention. A procedure that cannot make that distinction has nonetheless, within half a decade, assumed the authority to set the protocols of legitimate language. Held for centuries by academies and schoolrooms, grammars and examiners, this authority has been given over to loss functions, reward models, benchmarks, and system prompts: an apparatus that executes the office of judgment with no capacity for judging.

</details>


### [103] [LP Mining with LP2Graph: A Use Case for Railway Rescheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11980)
*Jörn Maurischat,Nikola Bešinović,Michael Färber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LP2Graph方法，从已发表的LP/MILP铁路重调度模型中自动挖掘结构，生成可复现数据集和客观分类体系。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化完全无关，属于运筹优化/铁路调度领域的MILP建模方法研究。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：LP/MILP formulation mining（线性/混合整数规划公式挖掘）；场景：铁路重调度（railway rescheduling）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: LP2Graph：将每个MILP公式表示为类型化变量-方程图（typed variable–equation graph），基于规范语法（canonical grammar）和规范模型（canonical model）构建；采用自底向上聚类（变量→约束与目标→全模型结构），以及按应用领域和求解方法分别聚类；使用规则种子自更新分类器（rule-seeded, self-updating classifier）标注。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文不讨论任何模型压缩、加速或轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文不讨论星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：从已发表的铁路重调度论文中提取的LP/MILP公式；评价指标：聚类代表性样本可复现性（重新生成独立LaTeX并用CBC、HiGHS、Gurobi求解，与原文最优值对比）。

Experiments: 验证方式：对每个聚类代表性样本重新生成LaTeX公式，使用CBC、HiGHS、Gurobi三种求解器求解，与原文报告的最优值对比。摘要未给出具体数值结果。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的检测或轻量化技术信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于运筹优化领域，不涉及遥感、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Abstract: Like many optimization-driven domains, railway rescheduling relies on Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP), yet the field's modeling knowledge is scattered across hundreds of papers in incompatible notations, and narrative surveys organize it subjectively: they classify models by vocabulary rather than by structure, and reproduce neither. We present LP Mining with LP2Graph, a method that mines the structure of published LP and MILP formulations into a reproducible dataset and an induced taxonomy. Its core, LP2Graph, represents each formulation admitted by its canonical grammar as a typed variable--equation graph derived from a single canonical model; once a source is extracted into that model, everything downstream is deterministic. Each source is parsed into this model, homologized, and clustered bottom-up (over variables, then constraints and the objective, then whole-model structure) and, separately, by application domain and solution approach; the resulting groups are labeled by a rule-seeded, self-updating classifier. We validate the representation rather than assume it: per-cluster representatives are regenerated as independent LaTeX and re-solved across CBC, HiGHS and Gurobi against the optimum reported in the source paper. The outcome is an objective, repeatable taxonomy of variables, constraints and model types: the principled foundation on which our raiLPminer line of automated railway-rescheduling model development builds.

</details>


### [104] [Designing Agent-Ready Websites for AI Web Agents: A Framework for Machine Readability, Actionability, and Decision Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12056)
*Said Elnaffar,Farzad Rashidi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"Agent-Ready Website"设计框架，通过增强网站结构清晰度、可操作性和决策可靠性信号，提升AI浏览器代理在电商场景中的任务完成成功率与效率。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于电商网站设计对AI代理的适配性，属于人机交互与电子商务领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：AI代理驱动的电商网站交互优化；应用场景：在线购物、产品搜索与比较；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 不适用。论文未提出视觉或检测模型，而是提出网站设计框架（agent interpretability, agent executability, agent decision reliability三个维度）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。论文评估了token consumption和step count，但这是针对AI代理的调用效率，与模型参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标无关。

Datasets and metrics: 实验设置：受控实验，对比human-oriented baseline与agent-ready版本的网站原型；使用三个浏览器代理模型（GPT-4.1, Gemini-2.5 Flash, Grok-4 Fast）；5个任务，300次运行。评价指标：PASS/PARTIAL/FAIL结果、严格成功率（strict success rate）、功能成功率（functional success rate）、错误模式、步数（step count）、token消耗量。

Experiments: Agent-Ready网站：134/150 PASS vs 基线74/150 PASS；严格成功率89.3% vs 49.3%；PARTIAL结果从43降至3；平均步数从9.31降至6.49。最大提升体现在产品详情提取、比较和多约束选择任务上。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法。论文本身局限包括：仅评估了三个特定AI代理模型，网站原型为简化版本，未涉及真实电商平台复杂性。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于人机交互/电商领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在问题定义、技术路线、评价体系上均无交集。

Abstract: Online shopping is increasingly shifting toward a model in which AI agents independently search for products, compare options, evaluate constraints, and carry out parts of the purchasing process for users. Website design must now support both human and agent-mediated interaction. This paper introduces the agent-ready website, a design framework for enhancing the readability, interpretability, verifiability, and actionability of e-commerce platforms for AI agents. Existing web design, SEO, and generative engine optimization (GEO) metrics do not fully assess a website's capacity for agent-mediated interaction. The proposed framework is structured around three dimensions agent interpretability, agent executability, and agent decision reliability supported by features such as machine readability, semantic clarity, agent actionability, and contextual decision-reliability signals. The framework is evaluated through a controlled experiment comparing a human-oriented baseline and an agent-ready version of an identical website prototype, with identical catalogs, pricing, stock, and shopping workflows. The evaluation involved five tasks, three browser-agent models (GPT-4.1, Gemini-2.5 Flash, and Grok-4 Fast), and 300 runs, measuring PASS,PARTIAL,FAIL outcomes, strict and functional success rates, error patterns, step counts, and token consumption. The agent-ready website achieved 134 PASS runs out of 150 versus 74 out of 150 for the baseline (strict success rates of 89.3% vs. 49.3%), with the largest gains in product detail extraction, comparison, and multi-constraint selection. It also reduced PARTIAL outcomes from 43 to 3 and lowered the average step count from 9.31 to 6.49. These results provide preliminary evidence that enhanced structural clarity, action cues, evidence signals, and temporal validity indicators can substantially improve the reliability and efficiency of AI browser agents.

</details>


### [105] [Graph Feedback Controls Consensus and Clique Formation in Open-Weight Language-Model Populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12077)
*Samer Saab,Chaouki Abdallah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究多智能体语言模型系统中基于命名游戏的惯例形成机制，分析不同路由策略对共识达成的影响。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关，属于多智能体语言模型与群体共识形成的交叉研究。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多智能体语言模型中的惯例形成（convention formation）研究；应用场景：开放权重语言模型群体交互；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 使用开放权重语言模型（1.1B-32B参数），涉及命名游戏协议（naming-game protocol）、状态相似性图（state-similarity graphs）、阈值相似性路由（threshold-similarity routing）、桥接路由（bridge-seeking routing）等机制。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然提及模型参数规模（1.1B-32B），但这是语言模型本身的规模，与星上部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 使用命名游戏协议生成交互数据；评价指标包括：首token得分（first-token scores）、标签一致性（label agreement）、状态空间共识（state-space consensus）、图能量特征（graph-energy features）等。

Experiments: 在多种干预条件下进行实验：同质模型群体（homogeneous model populations）、混合四模型网格（mixed four-model grid）、不同种子数（three-seed）、不同记忆保留条件（retained memory vs. no memory）、不同路由策略（阈值相似性 vs. 桥接路由）。主要发现：阈值相似性路由加剧碎片化，桥接路由在有记忆时可修复碎片化；Qwen2.5-32B在有记忆的充分混合设置中达到稳定共识。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。摘要本身未讨论模型效率、部署约束等关键问题。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文研究的是多智能体语言模型的社会性交互行为，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在问题定义、技术路线、评价体系上均无交集。

Abstract: Multi-agent language-model systems increasingly route local interactions, yet the runtime interaction graph is often treated as an implementation detail. We study convention formation in open-weight LM populations spanning 1.1B-32B parameters with a naming-game protocol. Restricted first-token scores over tokenizer-safe labels let us measure prompt-conditioned score-state distributions, construct state-similarity graphs, and separate sampled-label agreement from latent state-space consensus. Across controlled interventions, in the main open-weight repair grids, retained partner-label evidence is necessary but not sufficient: homophilous threshold-similarity routing deletes cross-basin exposure and amplifies fragmentation, while bridge-seeking routing often repairs fragmentation when memory is available. In a three-seed mixed four-model grid, threshold-similarity produces no final behavioral or state consensus in 189 setting-seed runs, whereas state-component and label-disagreement bridges recover final behavioral consensus in 14/18 retained-memory runs. Across homogeneous model populations, retained history generally shifts fragmented dynamics toward consensus; the clearest case is Qwen2.5-32B, which reaches stable behavioral and final state consensus in all 18 retained-history well-mixed settings, while threshold-similarity reaches neither form of consensus in 189 settings. Robustness over state thresholds, population size, and vocabulary size preserves the qualitative ordering, and early-window graph-energy features provide useful within-grid diagnostics.

</details>


### [106] [Operationalising Multi-Dimensional Evaluation for Conversational Agents: A Scalable, Governed Pipeline with Selective Re-evaluation and Model Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12085)
*Niranjan Kumar M,Balaji Nagarajan,Karthik Nair,Faysal Satter,Nithin Surendran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种面向零售对话系统的生成式AI评估流水线（GenAI Evaluation），用于大规模评估聊天机器人的意图对齐、事实性、帮助性、清晰度、语气等质量维度，不涉及遥感、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于零售对话系统的评估框架（LLM-as-a-judge），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化/部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：零售对话系统评估；应用场景：电商客服聊天机器人质量评估；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。框架使用LLM作为评判器（LLM-as-a-judge），包含归一化、分片、异步执行、schema约束的LLM评分等组件。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。摘要提到"production deployment"但指的是零售对话系统的生产部署，与遥感卫星在轨部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：12,980条分层随机人工标注样本（4名标注员），覆盖14个意图、156个子意图、18个主要领域、129个子领域；评价指标：Macro F1=0.93，人类可接受准确率89%（翻译维度）。

Experiments: 流水线每日处理约50,000条记录，累计评估超过200万次交互。验证集上Macro F1达到0.93。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于自然语言处理/对话系统评估领域，与遥感目标检测轻量化无任何交集。

Abstract: Evaluating retail conversational agents requires methods beyond lexical-overlap metrics to assess intent alignment, factuality, helpfulness, clarity, tone, and overall response quality. Although LLM-as-a-judge methods provide scalable alternatives to human evaluation, production deployment introduces challenges in governance, reproducibility, cost, schema consistency, traceability, and reliability. We present GenAI Evaluation, a governed, configuration-driven pipeline for large-scale evaluation of retail conversational systems. It processes production chatbot logs through normalization, sharding, asynchronous execution, and schema-constrained LLM scoring. The framework evaluates helpfulness, truthfulness, clarity, tone alignment, and translation-specific dimensions. Selective re-evaluation processes only incomplete, malformed, or schema-invalid records, while schema locking, versioned configurations, validation logs, and record-level provenance support auditability. The framework processes approximately 50,000 records daily and has evaluated more than two million interactions. Validation used 12,980 stratified-random human-labeled records from four trained annotators. Classification covered 14 intents, 156 sub-intents, 18 major domains, and 129 sub-domains. The pipeline achieved a macro F1 score of 0.93 and 89% human-acceptability accuracy for translation.

</details>


### [107] [Representing and Generating Levels Over Time through Playtrace Reconstructive Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12097)
*Emily Halina,Matthew Guzdial*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向视频游戏关卡随时间演变的"蛋糕"表示方法及Playtrace Reconstructive Partitioning (PRP)生成算法，用于Sokoban游戏关卡生成。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于视频游戏中的程序化内容生成（PCG），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：视频游戏关卡程序化生成；应用场景：Sokoban（推箱子）游戏；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 提出"cake"表示法用于编码游戏关卡随时间的动态信息；提出Playtrace Reconstructive Partitioning (PRP)生成算法。具体网络结构摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 实验在Sokoban游戏领域进行，与六种state-of-the-art PCG方法对比。具体数据集名称和评价指标摘要未说明。

Experiments: 与六种SOTA PCG方法对比，PRP方法能生成有效关卡且不牺牲解多样性。具体数值结果摘要未说明。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术点。论文仅在游戏领域验证，未讨论在其它领域的泛化性。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于游戏AI/程序化内容生成领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Video games are a dynamic medium experienced over time. While there are many Procedural Content Generation (PCG) approaches for generating video game levels, they often use representations that abstract away this dynamic nature. In this paper, we introduce a novel, domain-independent ``cake'' representation for game levels over time which implicitly encodes dynamic information. We present a novel level generation approach Playtrace Reconstructive Partitioning (PRP) specifically developed for this cake representation. We compare against six state-of-the-art PCG approaches in the game domain of \textit{Sokoban}, and find that our approach can generate valid levels without sacrificing solution diversity. We believe our cake representation more neatly encodes the implicit dynamic nature of games compared to existing representations, which allows for our domain-agnostic level generation algorithm PRP.

</details>


### [108] [Connected by Construction: Learning Tractable Near-Tour Marginals for Traveling Salesman Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12127)
*Ke Sun,Xinyuan Zhang,Xinwu Qian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出C2TSP，一种通过隐式微分学习TSP哈密顿结构的无监督端到端方法，核心是学习有结构意义的潜在对象（根1-树Gibbs族），而非仅依赖解码阶段。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是组合优化问题（旅行商问题TSP），不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一个核心要素。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：旅行商问题（TSP）求解；场景：组合优化；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。摘要未说明任何遥感或目标检测相关内容。

Model architecture: 基于根1-树Gibbs族的端到端无监督学习管道C2TSP，包含隐式微分学习残差边扰动、平滑Held-Karp层恢复度平衡、证书引导锐化推动分布向类tour结构演化。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未明确说明使用的数据集和评价指标，仅提及tour cost和tour-like structure作为评估维度。

Experiments: 实验表明C2TSP在保持可解释结构信息的同时取得了强解码性能；消融实验验证了边扰动和证书引导锐化共同改善了tour cost和类tour结构。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。论文聚焦于TSP组合优化问题的结构学习，不涉及视觉感知任务。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。唯一可能的间接启发是：如果未来在遥感目标检测中需要解决路径规划或扫描序列优化（如卫星任务调度），其隐式微分和结构约束方法可能有参考价值，但与本课题核心方向距离较远。

Abstract: Learning-based methods for the traveling salesman problem (TSP) are often evaluated through the tours produced after decoding or search, but the learned object itself frequently lives in a surrogate space such as heatmaps, assignments, construction policies, or search-guidance scores. This hides the fundamental question: what Hamiltonian structure has actually been learned before decoding? In this study, we directly answer this question by learning TSP through a structurally meaningful latent object, rather than leaving most of the Hamiltonian structure to the final decoding stage. Based on a connected-by-construction rooted $1$-tree Gibbs family, we propose an end-to-end unsupervised learning pipeline called \emph{C2TSP}. The pipeline learns residual edge perturbations from unbiased TSP cost through implicit differentiation. For structural correction, a smoothed Held--Karp layer restores expected degree balance, while certificate-guided sharpening further pushes the connected distribution toward more tour-like structures. Experiments show that C2TSP yields strong decoding performance while preserving interpretable structural information. Ablations further verify that edge perturbation and certificate-guided sharpening jointly improve both tour cost and tour-like structure.

</details>


### [109] [The Emerging Paradigm of Geospatial Foundation Models: From Pre-Training to Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12177)
*Shelley Cazares*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统阐述了地理空间基础模型（GeoFMs）的概念、范式转变、分类体系及工程化部署框架，提出将大规模预训练与下游任务微调分离，并展望了基于大语言模型驱动的智能地理空间推理代理。

Research relevance: 2分（弱相关）。论文主题是地理空间基础模型（GeoFMs），属于遥感/地理空间AI领域，但核心关注点是预训练范式、模型适配策略和LLM驱动的推理代理，而非目标检测任务本身，更不涉及模型轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、边缘/星上部署等具体技术。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向的相关性较弱。

Reading priority: 2分。本文是地理空间AI领域的综述性概念文章，对理解遥感AI前沿趋势有参考价值，但与"在轨目标检测模型轻量化"这一具体技术方向直接关联度低。建议作为背景阅读，非核心文献。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：地理空间基础模型（GeoFMs）的预训练范式、模型适配策略、零样本/少样本推理。应用场景：卫星与航空影像分析。传感器类型：摘要未说明具体传感器类型（光学/SAR/高光谱等）。目标类别：摘要未说明具体目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。提及了两类GeoFM：1）基于掩码自编码（masked auto-encoding）的自监督微调视觉模型；2）基于对比学习（contrastive learning）的视觉-语言模型（vision-language models），支持开放词汇零样本分析。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文讨论的是模型适配策略（fine-tuning vs. prompting）的成本效益分析，而非模型压缩或轻量化技术。未提及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署指标。虽然提及"operationalizing GeoFMs"和"performance-cost analysis"，但未涉及星上/边缘部署的具体约束。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。提及GeoFMs在大规模地理空间数据集上预训练，但未给出数据集名称、规模或评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明实验结果。本文是一篇概念综述/观点文章，未提供实验对比或消融实验。

Limitations: 1）本文为概念性综述，缺乏定量实验验证；2）未讨论模型轻量化、推理加速等对在轨部署至关重要的技术；3）GeoFMs通常参数量巨大（亿级），与星上资源受限场景存在根本矛盾；4）未涉及实时推理、边缘计算等工程约束；5）Agentic Geospatial Reasoning的构想在实际星上部署中可行性存疑。

Ideas for my research: 1）GeoFMs的"预训练-微调分离"范式可启发在轨场景：地面预训练大模型，星上部署轻量化版本，通过知识蒸馏将GeoFM的知识迁移到轻量检测网络；2）开放词汇检测（open-vocabulary）概念可借鉴用于在轨多类别目标检测，减少星上模型更新频率；3）模型适配策略的成本效益分析框架可迁移到在轨场景，用于评估不同轻量化方法（剪枝/量化/蒸馏）的部署性价比；4）但需注意，GeoFMs本身参数量过大，直接用于星上不现实，需结合轻量化技术。

Abstract: The analysis of satellite and aerial imagery has entered a new era with the advent of foundation models. This paper describes the concept of Geospatial Foundation Models (GeoFMs), which are artificial intelligence/machine learning (AI/ML) models pre-trained on massive geospatial datasets through varied methodologies. We first articulate the core paradigm shift that GeoFMs enable: a separation of duties, where large-scale model providers perform the computationally intensive pretraining, allowing domain experts to rapidly fine-tune or prompt these models for specific, mission-critical tasks. This approach democratizes access to state-of-the-art AI/ML while maintaining the security and confidentiality of the downstream task. We then explore the novel capabilities unlocked by different types of GeoFMs, distinguishing between the finetunable vision models produced by self-supervised techniques like masked auto-encoding, and the vision-language models produced by contrastive learning which enable zero-shot tasks like open-vocabulary image analysis. Next, we discuss the practical considerations for operationalizing GeoFMs, from performance-cost analysis to the broader MLOps ecosystem. To that end, we introduce a taxonomy of model adaptation strategies and propose a framework for domain experts to select the most cost-effective adaptation approach for their particular mission set. Finally, we present a forward-looking vision of Agentic Geospatial Reasoning, where Large Language Models act as intelligent orchestrators, leveraging GeoFMs as tools to answer high-level user queries in natural language and automate complex analytical workflows, moving the field from perception to cognition.

</details>


### [110] [Cost-Governed RAG: Unified Per-Tenant Cost Attribution Across Retrieval and Generation in Multi-Tenant LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12188)
*Navnit Shukla*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向企业RAG系统的成本治理架构，通过TurboVec向量索引与多租户LLM网关实现检索与生成成本的联合归因，核心贡献在成本可观测性与多租户计费公平性，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、遥感场景理解等任何检测任务；不涉及模型轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等任何轻量化或部署技术。论文主题是RAG系统的多租户成本归因与治理，属于LLM系统工程/云成本管理领域，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：企业RAG系统的多租户成本归因与治理。应用场景：云数据平台上的LLM检索增强生成服务。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: TurboVec（codebook-oblivious向量索引）+ 多租户LLM治理网关，形成统一的可观测性栈。摘要未说明具体LLM模型结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要提到了"codebook-oblivious quantization"，但这是用于实现确定性成本归因的量化方法，而非用于模型轻量化或加速推理。不涉及剪枝、知识蒸馏、NAS等轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上或边缘部署。部署平台为Snowpark Container Services（云数据平台）。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 模拟数据集：100个模拟租户，1000万向量，对数正态分布。评价指标：端到端成本归因准确率（99.96%）、遥测开销（低于查询延迟的0.04%）、检索基础设施成本降低（3.1-9.0倍）。

Experiments: 在100个模拟租户、1000万向量的设置下，系统达到99.96%的端到端成本归因准确率，遥测开销低于查询延迟的0.04%，检索基础设施成本相比托管向量数据库服务降低3.1-9.0倍。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法。2. 摘要中提到的"codebook-oblivious quantization"的去共享码本泄露特性仅为探索性观察，存在局限性（作者自述）。3. 实验基于模拟数据，未在真实遥感或边缘部署场景中验证。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题无直接可借鉴点。唯一可能间接相关的思路是：论文提出的"确定性成本归因"思想或许可以类比到星上资源（计算、内存、功耗）的精细化建模与分配，但这一关联非常牵强，不建议深入参考。

Abstract: Enterprise Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) deployments face a critical governance gap: while LLM generation cost is metered per token, the retrieval layer - vector memory, similarity compute, and embedding API calls - remains an unattributed shared cost, enabling invisible cross-subsidization among tenants. We present Cost-Governed RAG, an architecture that integrates a codebook-oblivious vector index (TurboVec) with a multi-tenant LLM governance gateway, creating a unified observability stack where embedding, retrieval, and generation costs are jointly attributable per tenant. The architecture exploits TurboVec's deterministic, closed-form memory formula to enable near-exact per-tenant retrieval cost calculation - a property unavailable in graph-based indexes with non-linear memory overhead. Deployed on Snowpark Container Services within a cloud data platform's governance boundary, the system achieves 99.96% end-to-end cost attribution accuracy across 100 simulated tenants (10M vectors, log-normal size distribution) with telemetry overhead below 0.04% of query latency. The architecture reduces retrieval infrastructure cost by 3.1-9.0x compared to managed vector database services under the pricing assumptions detailed in Section IV. We formalize a three-layer cost model and demonstrate that codebook-oblivious quantization enables deterministic per-tenant cost attribution while also removing the shared-codebook leakage surface present in trained quantizers - the latter observation being exploratory and subject to the limitations described in Section VII.

</details>


### [111] [A Threshold Exceedance Framework for CBRN Uplift Evaluation in Frontier Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12200)
*Rahul Gupta,Abhinav Mohanty,Payal Motwani,Venkatesh Saligrama,Satyapriya Krishna,Connor Harris,Gary Anthony Ackerman,Brandon Behlendorf,Tom Hobson,Theodore Wilson,Spyros Matsoukas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于评估大语言模型是否显著提升非专家用户实施CBRN（化学、生物、放射、核）攻击能力的阈值超标准则（TEC）框架，并通过大规模实证研究验证了该框架的有效性。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关领域；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测等任何视觉检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等任何模型压缩或部署技术。论文属于AI安全与治理领域，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文属于AI安全与治理方向，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大语言模型安全评估（CBRN misuse uplift evaluation）；应用场景：AI安全治理、模型发布前的风险评估；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。提及"frontier language models"（前沿语言模型），但未指定具体模型名称或结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化、压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何星上/边缘部署相关内容，未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：未说明具体数据集名称，提及通过大规模实证研究（large-scale empirical study）产生CBRN攻击计划并由领域专家评审。评价指标：material uplift（实质性提升）、generative uplift（生成性提升）、revisionist uplift（修订性提升）、expert-equivalent instructional ratings（专家等效指导评分）。

Experiments: 进行了大规模实证研究，设计了两种提升形式（生成性和修订性），覆盖CBRN四个领域。结果显示领域异质性：模型辅助的计划有时获得专家等效指导评分，但确认的实质性提升仅限于放射学领域。研究结果为缓解措施和部署治理决策提供了依据。

Limitations: 论文本身与遥感/目标检测/轻量化完全无关，无法从该角度评价其局限性。从论文自身角度看，摘要提到研究是在受控的预发布评估条件下进行的，不能表征已部署模型的行为；且实质性提升仅在放射学领域得到确认，其他领域结论有限。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题无直接可借鉴点。论文的评估框架设计方法论（如预设准则、显式基线、分离不同评估维度）在方法论层面有一定通用参考价值，但与遥感目标检测轻量化的技术核心无关。

Abstract: As frontier language models advance, policymakers and model developers need methods for assessing whether model access materially increases a non-expert actor's ability to plan high-consequence Chemical, Biological, Radiological, or Nuclear (CBRN) misuse relative to public tools alone. Existing CBRN evaluations differ in non-expert definitions, threat scope, baselines, scoring rubrics, and decision rules, making results difficult to compare across studies. We introduce a Threshold Exceedance Criteria (TEC) framework that decomposes an uplift study into independently executable components: determining non-expert participant eligibility, defining the CBRN threat scope for the study, and statistically estimating material uplift. We then operationalize the TEC framework in a large-scale empirical study using a design that determines two forms of uplift: generative (where a model assists plan creation from scratch) and revisionist (where a model assists refinement of an existing plan). The study produced attack plans across the CBRN domains, which we evaluated through subject-matter-expert review to estimate generative and revisionist uplift. Applying the framework, our empirical study revealed domain heterogeneity: under this controlled pre-release evaluation, model-assisted plans sometimes received expert-equivalent instructional ratings, but confirmed material uplift was limited to the radiological domain. These findings informed mitigation and deployment-governance decisions rather than characterizing deployed model behavior. We conclude with methodological lessons for future CBRN uplift evaluations, emphasizing prespecified criteria, explicit baselines, separation of generative and revisionist estimates, and careful distinction between preliminary screening signals and confirmed risk determinations.

</details>

### [113] [Rethinking the Evaluation of Harness Evolution for Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12227)
*Yike Wang,Huaisheng Zhu,Zhengyu Hu,Yige Yuan,Zhengyu Chen,Shakti Senthil,Hannaneh Hajishirzi,Yulia Tsvetkov,Pradeep Dasigi,Teng Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究LLM智能体的自动harness进化评估方法，质疑现有评估协议存在过拟合和与简单测试时缩放方法对比不公平的问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文主题是LLM agent的harness进化评估，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM agent的harness自动进化评估；应用场景：大语言模型智能体；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构，涉及GPT-5.4和Claude Opus 4.6作为被测试的LLM。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 完全不涉及星上/边缘部署相关内容。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Terminal-Bench 2.1；评价指标：摘要未说明具体指标名称。

Experiments: 在Terminal-Bench 2.1上使用GPT-5.4和Claude Opus 4.6进行实验，结果表明自动harness进化并不一致优于简单测试时缩放方法，且泛化能力有限。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于LLM agent评估领域，与遥感目标检测轻量化课题无任何技术交集。

Abstract: We revisit the evaluation of automatic harness evolution for LLM agents. Existing harness evolution methods use unit test cases to search for harness configurations and then report final performance on the same public benchmark. This protocol raises two fundamental concerns. First, harness evolution is itself an iterative search procedure that repeatedly evaluates and revises candidate harnesses using task feedback. As in agentic test-time scaling, it should therefore be compared with simple task-level search baselines under matched feedback and inference budgets to determine whether its gains arise from improved harness design or from additional search alone. Second, because the search and the final evaluation share the same benchmark, the reported gains risk overfitting to that specific task set. To address these concerns, we conduct an extensive evaluation comparing harness evolution with simple test-time scaling and discovery baselines under comparable feedback and inference budgets, and also evaluate evolved harnesses on held-out tasks to assess whether the discovered improvements generalize. Experiments on Terminal-Bench 2.1 with GPT-5.4 and Claude Opus 4.6 show that automatic harness evolution does not consistently outperform simple test-time scaling methods and exhibits limited generalization. Our results raise important questions about the effectiveness of automatic harness evolution and highlight the need for fairer evaluation protocols and benchmarks for automatic harness design. Our code is available at https://github.com/rethinking-harness-evolution.

</details>


### [114] [On-Device Deep Research at 4B: Exposure Bounds Faithfulness, Retrieval Bounds Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12257)
*Vinay Kumar Chaganti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究了一个4B参数的小模型在笔记本上做检索-阅读-引用的可信度问题，分离了"引用声明可信度"和"可信覆盖率"两个指标，发现曝光量决定前者，检索召回率决定后者。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是**on-device research agent**（设备端研究助手）的**citation faithfulness**（引用可信度），属于自然语言处理/信息检索/大模型应用领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：citation faithfulness评估（引用可信度）；场景：on-device research agent（设备端研究助手）；传感器/数据：文本语料（corpus, sources）；目标类别：无。

Model architecture: 一个4B参数的生成式语言模型（generator），运行在24GB笔记本电脑上。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明轻量化方法。模型本身是4B小模型，但论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化技术，仅将其作为固定条件使用。

Onboard deployability: 运行在24GB笔记本电脑上，输出约235个额外token。但完全不涉及星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT、实时推理、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：未说明具体数据集名称；指标：cited claim faithfulness（引用声明可信度）、trustworthy coverage（可信覆盖率）、exposure（曝光量，400 vs 1500字符）、retrieval recall（检索召回率，约0.40）。

Experiments: 交叉实验：曝光量（400 vs 1500字符）× 源质量（gold papers vs retrieved papers）。结果：曝光量从0.45提升到0.58（retrieved）和0.37到0.58（gold）；可信覆盖率在retrieved sources下始终约0.22。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关，无任何可借鉴的视觉检测或轻量化方法。研究的是文本生成任务中的引用可信度问题。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于NLP/信息检索领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型压缩或边缘部署。

Abstract: On-device research agents search a corpus, read sources, and write a cited brief on a personal laptop. Whether their citations are faithful, and at what cost, is unmeasured for a deployable small model. This study fixes one 4B generator on a 24 GB laptop and asks what makes its citations faithful. It separates two quantities usually reported as one number. Cited claim faithfulness asks whether the cited source supports the claim. Trustworthy coverage asks whether the agent also cites the right sources. The study crosses how much of each source the generator sees, 400 against 1500 characters, with the quality of the sources supplied, gold papers against retrieved papers. Two levers fall out, and they act on different outcomes. Exposure sets faithfulness. More of each source lifts faithfulness from 0.45 to 0.58 on retrieved sources and from 0.37 to 0.58 on gold sources, and the two settings converge, so faithfulness is bound by exposure, not by whether the source is correct. The exposure lift is robust to a second, independent judge; the exact convergence is tight under the primary judge and only approximate under the second. Retrieval sets coverage. Trustworthy coverage stays near 0.22 on retrieved sources at any exposure, because recall is held near 0.40, so exposure cannot fix which sources are cited. The extra exposure costs about 235 output tokens. The practical recipe is to raise per source exposure first, cheaply, and then treat retrieval recall as the only remaining lever.

</details>

Abstract: Agent benchmarks often compare two agents after all tasks have run, but costly evaluations make partial runs tempting. A task fraction alone does not show whether a partial run supports the same pairwise conclusion as the completed benchmark. We study this question by replaying completed public task-level records from SWE-bench, AppWorld, and tau-bench. A partial budget counts as enough only when it supports the completed benchmark's decision, covers required task groups, and leaves no more than a target fraction of comparisons unresolved. The required task fraction varies sharply. At the strict 0 percentage point threshold on a 5 percentage point budget grid, AppWorld first meets all targets at 15 percent, tau-bench at 25 percent, and SWE-bench Verified at 90 percent; SWE-bench Lite does not meet all targets by 95 percent under the primary coverage rule. Partial-evaluation reports should state how much one agent must outperform another, how tasks are selected, what coverage rule is required, what decision rule is used, and how many comparisons may remain unresolved.

</details>


### [116] [PM-Bench: Evaluating Prospective Memory in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12385)
*Genglin Liu,Saadia Gabriel*

TL;DR: 本文提出PM-Bench，一个用于评估LLM智能体前瞻记忆能力的文本基准，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。论文研究的是LLM智能体的前瞻记忆（prospective memory）能力，属于认知科学和自然语言处理交叉领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化均无任何关联。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：PM-Bench（自建文本基准，模拟7天虚拟周）；评价指标：F1 score（最佳方法GPT-5.4达到65.1%）。

Experiments: 在8种agent配置下对比了8种state-of-the-art LLMs，结果显示PM-Bench对所有设置均有挑战性，且没有单一策略在所有模型上占优。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于LLM智能体认知能力评估，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交叉。

Abstract: A significant challenge in agentic AI is prospective memory: the ability to execute an intention at a specific future cue or state while other activities are ongoing. We introduce PM-Bench, a text-based benchmark for measuring prospective memory capabilities in modern LLM agents. Inspired by the Virtual Week paradigm from cognitive science, PM-Bench evaluates how well LLM agents maintain user intentions, execute delayed intentions, and monitor latent environment changes. Over the course of a simulated seven-day week, agents must continue an ongoing activity while deciding whether any deferred task is due. We compare eight state-of-the-art LLMs on PM-Bench under eight different agent configurations. PM-Bench proves challenging across all settings: the best method, a GPT-5.4 agent, reaches only 65.1\% F1 score under our evaluation. Furthermore, no single strategy for improving prospective memory dominates across models. We release PM-Bench as a controlled testbed for diagnosing these failures and developing training or inference-time interventions that support reliable prospective behavior.

</details>


### [117] [Critic Experience Bank: Self-Evolving Step-Level Confidence Estimation for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12397)
