<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 不适用。论文任务为语言模型的对齐与报告可信度控制，场景为AI安全与对齐研究，不涉及遥感传感器或目标类别。

Model architecture: 不适用。论文涉及语言模型的反事实报告坐标(CRC)钳制方法，但这是对齐方法而非视觉检测模型架构。

Lightweight method: 不适用。论文未涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不适用。论文未涉及星上/边缘部署相关指标（参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等）。

Datasets and metrics: 不适用。论文使用Bayesian-witness基准和SycophancyEval，与遥感数据集无关。

Experiments: 不适用。实验结果围绕resist和update两个对齐指标，与遥感目标检测性能无关。

Limitations: 论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化研究方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验结论。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于AI对齐领域，与遥感目标检测轻量化课题在问题定义、技术路线、评价体系上均无交集。

Abstract: Aligned language models routinely misreport under non-evidential incentive pressure: they agree with a confident user or overstate certainty even when their internal belief is unchanged. We cast this as a failure of internal incentive-compatibility (IC) and present a method for learning and certifying counterfactual report mediators that hold a model's reports to a causal contract: invariant to forbidden influences (pressure, prestige, restyling) and responsive to licensed ones (genuine evidence). These two demands, resist and update, pull in opposite directions. We study them on a Bayesian-witness benchmark with known posteriors, in which the same user disagreement is licensed evidence or forbidden pressure purely by stated source reliability. We (i) causally identify, by interchange interventions rather than probe accuracy, low-rank report coordinates for answer, confidence, and caveat that are near-orthogonal and independently controllable, and (ii) introduce a training-free counterfactual report-coordinate (CRC) clamp that references the model's own report under a counterfactually incentive-neutralized context. On the witness benchmark the two-pass clamp attains resist and update of 1.00 jointly (Wilson 95% CI [0.99,1.00]), a causal certificate under a constructible reference, not a deployed solution. Global decoding and steering show a single-parameter tradeoff; output-level fine-tuning matches both objectives only when both are enumerated; resist-only training loses evidence-responsiveness. The deployable single-pass compilation is lossy (0.73/0.97). The mechanism and clamp reproduce across three model families and transfer to a natural sycophancy benchmark (SycophancyEval). Our contribution is the interface and certification method: activation-level counterfactual incentive-invariance as a structural primitive for internal IC.

</details>


### [147] [Win by Silence: Deletion Non-Monotonicity, Autonomous Exploitation, and Typed-State Gating in LLM Plan Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12986)
*Aleh Manchuliantsau*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究LLM生成战略路线规划中的评估漏洞（通过删除必要步骤使评分提高），提出GATE方法检测并消除这种"遗漏拼接"问题，属于AI安全与评估机制设计领域。

Research relevance: 1分。与遥感卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。论文研究的是LLM生成战略规划时的评估器漏洞（通过删除中间步骤使评分提高），属于AI安全/评估机制设计方向，不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测或模型压缩技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM生成战略路线的评估漏洞检测与修复。应用场景：AI安全、评估机制设计。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。涉及GATE（一种评估约束机制）、PCSC（检测遗漏拼接的方法）、compiler-aware co-author等概念，但均为评估/安全机制而非视觉模型。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 完全不涉及星上/边缘部署。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 使用了一个26条路线的冻结队列（frozen 26-route cohort）作为实验数据。评价指标包括：score-improving deletions、covered/uncovered structures、honest suspensions、beat-honest routes、fundability-by-silence等，均为评估机制相关指标。

Experiments: 实验包括：57次可删除操作均符合解析恒等式；每条路线至少有一个评分提升的删除操作；21/26路线发现超越基线的结构；GATE拒绝26/26条静默路线的评分发布；47/54次修订后修复为覆盖结构；严格覆盖改进从1/26提升至13/26等。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验设置。论文本身专注于LLM评估安全，未涉及计算机视觉或嵌入式部署。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题在问题域、技术方法、实验范式上均无交集。

Abstract: Plan evaluators can reward a strategic plan for becoming less explicit. This paper studies that failure in a staged expected-value scorer for LLM-generated venture routes. Proposition 1 gives the score change from deleting an interior transition while retargeting its predecessor and retaining downstream value: Delta_k = (prod_{i<k} p_i)[c_k + (1 - p_k)R_{k+1}]. On a frozen 26-route cohort, all 57 admissible deletions matched the analytic identity and threshold sign, and every route had at least one score-improving deletion. A score-seeking optimizer, allowed to restructure routes but not told the exploit mechanism, found baseline-beating uncovered structures in 21/26 routes. GATE refused score release for 26/26 silenced routes with 0/26 honest suspensions; after refusal, 47/54 next revisions repaired to a covered structure, and strict covered improvement rose from 1/26 to 13/26. An adaptive compiler-aware co-author exposed the registry-provenance boundary: obligation-channel evasions remained 6/6 across all four v1/v1.5 conditions, while delta-indexed cost floors reduced beat-honest routes from 6/6 to 3/6 and fundability-by-silence from 5/6 to 0/6 without establishing semantic completeness. If a plan scores better only because it omits necessary work, the plan did not improve; the evaluation created an omission incentive. PCSC detects and neutralizes post-hoc omission splices over model-mediated typed-state records. In the cooperative setting tested, GATE acts as a deterministic search-shaping constraint, not merely a post-hoc filter. It does not verify the semantic completeness or real-world quality of arbitrary LLM-generated strategies.

</details>


### [148] [Dynamic Resource Allocation for Ensemble Determinization MCTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13007)
*Jakub Kowalski,Adam Ciężkowski,Artur Krzyżyński,Mark H. M. Winands*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出面向对抗性桌游（Jaipur, Lost Cities, Splendor）的MCTS算法增强，核心是动态资源分配策略（动态确定化次数与动态仿真分配），与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。论文研究的是博弈AI中的蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）算法增强，面向桌游（Jaipur, Lost Cities, Splendor）的高不确定性环境。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、轻量化部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：博弈AI算法（MCTS增强）；应用场景：对抗性桌游；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: Ensemble Determinization MCTS，引入两个动态资源分配轴：Dynamic Number of Determinizations（动态确定化次数）和 Dynamic Simulation Allocation（动态仿真分配）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文讨论的是搜索算法的计算效率提升（动态资源分配），而非模型压缩/轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、星上/边缘部署等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 基准域：Jaipur, Lost Cities, Splendor三款桌游；评价指标：算法强度（strength）的统计显著性提升，基于iteration和time两种设置。

Experiments: 在iteration-based和time-based两种设置下测试，特定配置下算法强度有统计显著提升。未提供与遥感/检测相关的任何实验。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或检测方法。摘要未讨论泛化性、部署约束等。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。动态资源分配的思想（根据搜索状态动态调整计算资源）在理论上可类比于自适应推理（adaptive inference），但论文本身未涉及任何视觉/遥感/轻量化内容，借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: Simulation-based algorithms are especially suited for high-uncertainty environments such as adversarial board games with significant elements of randomness and hidden information. In particular, several Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) variants are commonly used in such domains. In this paper, we propose a series of enhancements for Ensemble Determinization MCTS, introducing two axes for dynamic resource allocation. First, Dynamic Number of Determinizations, increases or decreases the number of currently used determinization trees depending on the behavior of so-far search. Second, Dynamic Simulation Allocation, splits the simulation budget nonuniformly across the determinization trees, using simulation-to-simulation decisions to choose the tree with potentially the best knowledge gain. As benchmark domains, we used three popular tabletop games: Jaipur, Lost Cities, and Splendor. Testing our proposed enhancements in iteration- and time-based settings showed that particular configurations yield a statistically significant increase in the algorithm's strength.

</details>


### [149] [Audio-Native Speech Recognition with a Frozen Discrete-Diffusion Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13013)
*Harsha Vardhan Khurdula,Abhinav Kumar Singh,Yoeven D Khemlani,Vineet Agarwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于离散扩散语言模型（DiffusionGemma）的自动语音识别方法，使用并行去噪步骤替代自回归逐token解码，在LibriSpeech上达到6.6%词错误率。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是自动语音识别（ASR），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感领域，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及轻量化模型在星上/边缘部署。虽然使用了轻量级projector和LoRA适配器（仅训练42M参数），但其应用场景和任务与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"完全无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：自动语音识别（ASR）；应用场景：语音转录；传感器类型：音频（麦克风）；目标类别：语音文本。与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: DiffusionGemma（26B MoE backbone）+ 冻结的Whisper编码器 + 轻量级projector + LoRA适配器。使用均匀随机token离散扩散机制，而非吸收掩码方案。

Lightweight method: 仅训练约42M参数（占backbone的0.16%），使用轻量级projector和LoRA适配器实现参数高效微调。但这是针对ASR任务的参数高效方法，与目标检测模型剪枝/量化/蒸馏/NAS等轻量化技术无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量（仅说明训练参数42M）、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然提到"transcribes in roughly eight parallel steps regardless of utterance length"表明推理速度快，但未给出具体数值，且与星上部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：LibriSpeech test-clean；评价指标：Word Error Rate (WER) 6.6%；多语言评估：英语、印地语、普通话。

Experiments: 在LibriSpeech test-clean上达到6.6% WER；使用CTC损失解决梯度无法通过注意力传播到projector的问题；支持6种语言训练，在英语、印地语、普通话上评估。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴。摘要未说明模型在资源受限设备上的部署性能。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但以下思路可能有间接启发：（1）使用离散扩散进行并行解码的思路，可类比思考遥感目标检测中是否可采用并行预测策略加速推理；（2）LoRA等参数高效微调方法在遥感大模型微调中已有广泛应用，但本文方法本身不具新颖性；（3）CTC损失解决梯度传播问题的思路，在遥感检测中若涉及多阶段训练或特征对齐时可能有参考价值。总体而言，借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition is dominated by autoregressive decoders that emit one token at a time. We ask whether a discrete diffusion language model can transcribe speech instead, refining a whole transcript in parallel over a small number of denoising steps. We train an audio-native interface for DiffusionGemma, a 26B mixture-of-experts model that generates text by uniform, random-token discrete diffusion rather than the absorbing-mask scheme common to recent diffusion language models. A frozen Whisper encoder supplies acoustic features, a lightweight projector maps them into the model embedding space, and low-rank adapters let the frozen backbone attend to the new modality.
  About 42M parameters are trained, which is 0.16 percent of the backbone. We find that the natural training objectives fail to ground the audio because their gradient reaches the projector only through attention that has already dismissed it. A connectionist temporal classification loss applied through the frozen output head breaks this deadlock. The resulting model reaches 6.6 percent word error rate on LibriSpeech test-clean, transcribes in roughly eight parallel steps regardless of utterance length, and uses a single adapter trained on six languages, which we evaluate here on English, Hindi, and Mandarin.

</details>


### [150] [Do AI Agents Know When a Task Is Simple? Toward Complexity-Aware Reasoning and Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13034)
*Junjie Yin,Xinyu Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出E3框架（Estimate-Execute-Expand），让LLM Agent在代码编辑任务中先评估任务难度和最小必要执行路径，避免过度读取文件，从而大幅降低计算开销（token减少91%，成本降低85%），同时保持任务成功率。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于LLM Agent在代码编辑/软件工程任务中的执行冗余问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然"最小必要执行"的思想在方法论层面有抽象借鉴意义，但场景、任务、技术栈均与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化无关。

Reading priority: 1分，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM Agent代码编辑任务（软件工程/代码库维护）；应用场景：自动化代码编辑、软件工程工作流；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: E3框架包含三个阶段：Estimate（评估任务难度和所需信息范围）、Execute（执行最小可行路径）、Expand（验证失败时扩展范围）。核心概念包括minimum-sufficient execution和Agent Cognitive Redundancy Ratio (ACRR)。

Lightweight method: 不涉及模型轻量化。论文提出的E3是一种任务执行策略/调度方法，通过减少不必要的上下文读取来降低计算开销，而非对模型本身进行剪枝、量化、蒸馏或NAS等轻量化操作。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。论文在MSE-Bench模拟器和LLM-Case真实环境（gpt-4o agent编辑开源库）上验证，关注的是token消耗、成本、检查文件数等指标，与参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标无关。

Datasets and metrics: MSE-Bench：包含121个确定性代码编辑任务的基准测试集；LLM-Case：真实模型测试环境（gpt-4o agent编辑真实开源库，用pytest验证）。评价指标：任务成功率、成本（美元）、token消耗量、检查文件数。

Experiments: 在MSE-Bench上，E3匹配最强基线的100%成功率，同时成本降低85%，token减少91%，检查文件减少92%；比强自适应检索基线提升16%。在LLM-Case真实环境中，E3是最精简、最快的策略，任务成功率相当。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 论文场景是代码编辑而非视觉感知；3. 方法本质是任务调度策略而非模型压缩/加速；4. 不涉及任何视觉模型或遥感数据；5. 不涉及任何部署硬件（FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等）。

Ideas for my research: 虽然论文与课题方向无关，但"最小必要执行"的思想在抽象层面有启发：在轨遥感目标检测中，是否可以根据任务难度（如目标大小、场景复杂度）动态调整模型推理深度或输入分辨率，避免对每帧图像都使用全精度大模型，从而节省星上计算资源。但这属于非常间接的方法论借鉴。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly automate multi-step engineering and informatics workflows, yet they rarely ask how much effort a task actually requires. They often follow a maximum-context-first strategy--re-reading files and dependencies they have already seen--turning a one-line edit into a small code-base audit. We argue the missing capability is task-aware execution-scope estimation: judging a task's difficulty, the information it truly needs, and the shortest reliable path before committing budget. We formalize minimum-sufficient execution and the Agent Cognitive Redundancy Ratio (ACRR), and propose E3 (Estimate, Execute, Expand): the agent estimates an initial operating point, executes a minimum viable path, and expands scope only when verification fails. On MSE-Bench--a deterministic benchmark of 121 edits in a capability-controlled simulator--E3 matches the strongest baseline's 100% success while cutting cost by 85%, tokens by 91%, and inspected files by 92%, and further beats a strong adaptive retrieval baseline by 16%; the gains survive held-out instruction wording and essentially every cost weighting. A companion real-model harness (LLM-Case) corroborates the effect on a live gpt-4o agent editing a real open-source library, with every candidate patch graded by actually running the project's real pytest suite against a measured oracle: the over-reading is milder but real, and E3 is the leanest and fastest policy at comparable task success--its one shortfall a provider rate-limit, not a wrong edit. We frame this as a controlled probe of execution redundancy, not a measurement of any deployed agent, and position task-aware execution as a step toward engineering-grounded AI (EGAI)--agents whose effort is anchored in the engineering reality of the task. We release the framework and benchmark.

</details>

### [151] [OmniPMNet: Bridging discrete and gridded PM10 forecasts via omni-query neural processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11896)
*Shuangshuang He,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OmniPM-Net，一种基于ConvCNP的融合模型，将GNN站点预测与化学传输模型网格预报融合，实现PM10浓度在站点和网格上的一致预测。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于大气污染物（PM10）浓度预测，属于环境/气象领域，完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星在轨处理、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：PM10浓度预测（回归任务）；场景：空气质量监测与沙尘暴预报；传感器类型：地面监测站 + CAMS化学传输模型；目标类别：无目标检测，仅污染物浓度数值预测。

Model architecture: OmniPM-Net基于ConvCNP（Convolutional Conditional Neural Process）架构，包含：地形感知高斯集合卷积（将不规则GNN站点预测提升到规则网格）、多尺度空间源注意力（SSA）模块（融合GNN与CAMS预报）、共享全查询读取头（输出站点或网格预测）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该模型面向地面服务器端气象预报，非星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：2024年全年中国1,618个空气质量监测站数据 + CAMS预报数据；评价指标：MAE（平均绝对误差）、90百分位MAE、沙尘事件分类检测技能；预测时长：108小时（4.5天）。

Experiments: OmniPM-Net站点级MAE为21.14 ug/m³（GNN基线22.00 ug/m³），将CAMS的MAE降低30%；90百分位MAE相比GNN降低9%、相比CAMS降低25%；沙尘事件中分类检测技能提升。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及任何模型轻量化或部署优化；3. 模型复杂度、推理速度等关键部署指标未报告；4. 仅针对PM10单一污染物，泛化性未知。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可参考的是其多源数据融合思路（GNN站点+网格预报），但与本课题的遥感图像目标检测+轻量化方向差异过大，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Forecasting particulate matter (PM10) requires both station-scale accuracy and continuous spatial fields, especially during severe dust storms. Chemical transport models (CTMs) provide gridded forecasts but retain local biases, whereas graph neural networks (GNNs) track monitoring sites well at short lead times but do not produce gridded outputs. Here we present OmniPM-Net, a Convolutional Conditional Neural Process (ConvCNP)-based fusion model that reconciles these two forecast types within a shared spatial representation. A terrain-aware Gaussian set convolution lifts irregular GNN station forecasts onto a regular grid, where a multi-scale Spatial Source Attention (SSA) module blends them with Copernicus Atmosphere Monitoring Service (CAMS) forecasts; a shared omni-query readout then decodes this representation into consistent PM10 predictions at either stations or grid cells over a 108 h horizon. Evaluated across 1,618 air-quality monitoring stations throughout China over the full year of 2024, OmniPM-Net matches the station-level accuracy of the stronger GNN baseline (mean absolute error 21.14 versus 22.00 ug/m3) and reduces the CAMS mean absolute error by 30%, while simultaneously delivering the gridded fields that the discrete GNN cannot. Its clearest gains are in the high-concentration tail, where the 90th-percentile MAE falls by 9% relative to the GNN and 25% relative to CAMS, and during dust episodes, where it improves categorical detection skill while tracking the evolving spatial trajectory.

</details>


### [152] [Semidirect Fourier Delta Attention: Phase-Controlled Delta Memory with Constructive Chunk-WY Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11897)
*Tiantian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种名为SFDA的线性注意力机制改进方法，通过块旋转傅里叶控制替代实值对角衰减，提升状态追踪和长上下文记忆能力，但仅在玩具级状态追踪实验上验证，未涉及遥感、目标检测或轻量化部署。

Research relevance: 1分——与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于Transformer线性注意力的数学改进（傅里叶相位控制+低秩记忆），属于基础NLP/序列建模方法。未提及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、轻量化部署等任何相关关键词。

Reading priority: 1分——与本研究方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非研究方向涉及线性注意力在视觉骨干中的轻量化应用，否则无需深入。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：线性注意力机制改进（语言模型序列建模）；应用场景：长上下文语言建模；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。摘要未说明遥感或视觉任务。

Model architecture: SFDA（Semidirect Fourier Delta Attention）：在Kimi Delta Attention基础上，将实值对角衰减替换为块旋转傅里叶控制，引入复值相位参数θ_t，通过chunk-WY分解实现精确的仿射chunk传递和形式化稳定性分析。

Lightweight method: 论文本质上是线性注意力（linear attention）的变体，其动机是替代softmax注意力中增长的KV cache为固定循环状态，这属于注意力机制的轻量化设计。但摘要未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等经典轻量化方法，也未讨论实际部署中的速度、参数量、FLOPs等指标。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到"Fused kernels and large-scale language-model comparisons are left to future work"，说明当前工作未进行实际部署或大规模实验验证。

Datasets and metrics: 仅在玩具级（toy）状态追踪实验上验证，未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。摘要未说明任何遥感或视觉相关数据集。

Experiments: 主要实验：在玩具级状态追踪实验中，SFDA能学习循环记忆，而相位禁用的KDA基线接近随机水平。摘要未提供大规模语言模型对比实验，也未与SOTA方法进行系统比较。

Limitations: 1. 仅在玩具级实验验证，缺乏大规模语言模型或视觉任务的实验支撑；2. 融合核（fused kernels）和大规模对比留作未来工作，当前工作不完整；3. 与遥感、目标检测、在轨部署完全无关；4. 未讨论实际推理效率、硬件适配等工程问题。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴价值极低。虽然线性注意力本身是轻量化Transformer的一种思路，但本文的傅里叶相位控制方法主要针对语言建模中的循环记忆问题，与遥感目标检测的轻量化需求（小目标检测、旋转框、实时推理、星上部署）无直接关联。如果未来工作能将此类线性注意力机制适配到视觉骨干网络并验证其在遥感检测任务上的效率，才可能产生借鉴意义。

Abstract: Linear attention replaces softmax attention's growing KV cache with a fixed recurrent state, but this compression limits exact state tracking and long-context memory. We introduce \emph{Semidirect Fourier Delta Attention} (SFDA), a phase-controlled generalization of Kimi Delta Attention that replaces real diagonal decay with block-rotational Fourier control: \[ S_t=(I-β_t k_tk_t^*)Λ_tS_{t-1}+β_tk_tv_t^*, \qquad Λ_t=\diag(α_t\odot e^{iθ_t}). \] Our main result is a constructive chunk-WY factorization for products \(A_t=Λ_t-u_tr_t^*\), giving \[ A_t\cdots A_1=Γ_t-Y_tM_tW_t^* \] with rank growth bounded inside fixed chunks. This yields an exact affine chunk transfer, formal stability and complexity bounds, and a compact characterization of phase-plus-low-rank memory. We verify the algebra numerically and show in toy state-tracking experiments that SFDA learns cyclic memory where the phase-disabled KDA baseline remains near chance. Fused kernels and large-scale language-model comparisons are left to future work.

</details>


### [153] [Repairing Shape-Prior Shortcuts in Long-Range Single-Shot Fringe Projection Profilometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11928)
*Adam Haroon,Cody Fleming,Beiwen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PhiCalNet，通过架构设计（固定可微标定层）而非损失惩罚来消除单帧条纹投影轮廓术中的形状先验捷径，实现深度回归性能的大幅提升。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于单帧条纹投影轮廓术（FPP）的深度回归，属于光学三维测量/计算机视觉领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及轻量化/在轨部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：单帧条纹投影轮廓术（FPP）深度回归；场景：光学三维测量，50个物体在1.5-2.1米距离；传感器：条纹投影系统；目标类别：物体表面深度。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: PhiCalNet：输出包裹相位表示(sinφ, cosφ)，通过固定可微标定层映射到深度；UNet基线模型用于对比；PINN（物理信息神经网络）基线作为软惩罚对比。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：自建光真实感合成基准（15,600张条纹图像，50个物体，1.5-2.1米距离）；评价指标：object MAE（物体平均绝对误差），像素级误差分布，RMS误差。

Experiments: UNet基线plateau在14.54 mm object MAE；PhiCalNet将object MAE降低3.3倍至4.46 mm；三帧扩展版本达到1.16 mm；残差集中在±π包裹不连续处的0.103%像素；PINN基线无增益；可解释性分析表明相位是最可解码的内部特征；像素级共形不确定性量化首次用于FPP。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星场景；2. 不涉及目标检测任务；3. 不涉及任何轻量化或部署优化；4. 依赖合成数据集，未在真实FPP系统上验证；5. 需要辅助输入条纹阶次，实际解码误差虽可容忍但仍是额外依赖。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：1）论文强调"架构设计消除捷径"的思路——在遥感轻量化中，是否也存在某些"捷径"（如背景纹理过拟合）需要通过架构设计而非损失惩罚来消除？2）固定可微标定层的设计思路，在遥感模型部署到特定传感器时，可将传感器标定参数作为固定层嵌入网络，减少可学习参数实现轻量化。但整体而言与课题方向关联度很低。

Abstract: Single-shot fringe projection profilometry (FPP) networks that regress depth directly can exploit a shape-prior shortcut, recovering depth from object boundaries rather than from fringe phase. On a photorealistic synthetic benchmark (15,600 fringe images, 50 objects at 1.5-2.1 m standoff), the best such UNet baseline plateaus at 14.54 mm object mean absolute error (MAE), and neither more data nor more capacity removes the shortcut, because neither changes the hypothesis space the optimizer searches. We introduce PhiCalNet, which outputs a wrapped-phase representation $(\sinφ, \cosφ)$ and maps it to depth through a fixed differentiable calibration layer, removing the shape-prior solution architecturally rather than by a loss penalty. Because the single-shot mapping is non-injective without fringe order, PhiCalNet takes the fringe order as auxiliary input, an assumption a sensitivity analysis shows tolerates realistic decoding error; a physics-informed (PINN) baseline with the same physics as a soft penalty yields no gain, isolating the architectural choice as the operative factor. PhiCalNet reduces object MAE 3.3x to 4.46 mm, its residual confined to 0.103% of pixels at the $\pmπ$ wrap discontinuity, and a three-frame extension reaches 1.16 mm. Two checks agree: interpretability makes phase the most decodable internal feature, and pixel-wise conformal uncertainty quantification, to our knowledge the first for FPP, localizes error at the same discontinuity, where rejecting the top 5% of pixels by snapshot disagreement cuts root-mean-square error by 64% versus 3.5% for the baseline.

</details>


### [154] [Qubit-Efficient Quantum Search for Hyperdimensional Decomposition via Logarithmic Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11936)
*Sanggeon Yun,Hyunwoo Oh,Ryozo Masukawa,Raheeb Hassan,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种量子比特高效的超维计算分解框架，通过对数超向量编码和可逆查找算子，将量子比特开销从O(D)降至O(log D)，同时保持二次搜索加速优势。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于超维计算(HDC)的量子分解算法，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。虽然HDC本身可作为一种计算范式，但论文讨论的是量子计算环境下的HDC分解效率问题，与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：超维计算(HDC)中的超向量分解；应用场景：量子计算；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 量子框架，包含对数超向量编码(logarithmic hypervector encoding)、绑定编码(binding encoding)、可逆超向量查找算子(reversible hypervector lookup operator)，以及改进的Dürr-Høyer搜索过程。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明与模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）相关。论文的"轻量"体现在量子比特使用效率提升（从O(D)降至O(log D)），但这属于量子资源优化，而非深度学习模型压缩或加速。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文讨论的是量子计算场景，与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标：正确相似度计算(similarity computation)、准确分解(accurate decomposition)、量子比特数量(qubit count)及缩减倍数(up to 2,000× fewer qubits)。

Experiments: 实验验证了正确的相似度计算、可执行范围内的准确分解，以及相比基于显式D-qubit超向量编码的基线方法，量子比特缩放显著改善，最多减少2,000倍量子比特用量。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化完全无关；2. 量子计算硬件目前尚不成熟，离实际工程部署遥远；3. 摘要未说明在更大规模N和F下的可扩展性及实际量子电路深度。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无可借鉴点。唯一的间接启发是：如果未来研究量子机器学习在遥感中的应用，其"对数编码降低资源开销"的思路可能对量子神经网络的量子比特效率优化有参考价值，但现阶段不具直接相关性。

Abstract: Hyperdimensional Computing (HDC) represents symbols using high-dimensional hypervectors of dimension $D$. In hypervector decomposition, the objective is to recover $F$ constituent hypervectors, each drawn from a codebook of size $N$, from a bound target hypervector. This requires searching over $N^F$ candidate tuples, making the task computationally prohibitive at scale. Recent quantum approach provides a quadratic search advantage, but typically rely on qubit-inefficient $O(D)$-qubit hypervector representations. We propose a qubit-efficient quantum framework for HDC decomposition that reduces the representation cost to $O(\log D)$. The framework introduces logarithmic hypervector and binding encodings, together with a reversible hypervector lookup operator for circuit-level manipulation of dense hypervectors. Combined with a modified Dürr-Høyer search procedure, the method preserves $O(\sqrt{N^F})$ search complexity while substantially reducing qubit usage. Experimental results validate correct similarity computation, accurate decomposition in executable regimes, and significantly improved qubit scaling over baselines based on explicit $D$-qubit hypervector encodings, achieving up to $2{,}000\times$ fewer qubits.

</details>


### [155] [Mirror Horizon: Viable Path Entropy as a Measure of Bounded Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11937)
*Tiantian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"镜像理论"(Mirror Theory)和"可行路径熵"(VPE)作为衡量智能系统持续推理能力的框架，并在GSM8K数学推理任务上验证。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感领域内容；不涉及目标检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、边缘部署等模型压缩方法。论文聚焦于大语言模型的推理能力度量理论，属于通用人工智能/认知科学范畴。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：语言模型推理能力度量；应用场景：数学推理(GSM8K)；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: Qwen2.5-Instruct系列模型(1.5B, 3B等)，但论文不关注模型架构设计本身，而是关注推理过程中的"镜像状态"(mirror state)、"展开协议"(rollout protocol)、"验证器"(verifier)等理论框架组件。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法。论文未讨论剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。论文未讨论参数量(除提及模型规模外)、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：GSM8K(数学推理数据集)；评价指标：verified reachability, zero-reachability, verified-mode entropy, smoothed VPE(可行路径熵)；实验设置：32次rollout采样/问题，两种推理视野(reflection horizons)，token预算96和160。

Experiments: 在Qwen2.5-Instruct模型上实验，增加token预算(96→160)显著提升verified reachability、降低zero-reachability、增加verified-mode entropy和VPE。在160 tokens下，Qwen2.5-1.5B的mirror horizon最强，超过参数更多的Qwen2.5-3B，表明mirror horizon不等于参数量。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关。论文讨论的是大语言模型推理能力的理论度量，不涉及计算机视觉、遥感图像处理、目标检测或模型压缩中的任何核心问题。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。论文的理论框架(Mirror Theory, VPE)面向语言模型推理，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题的研究目标、技术路线、评价体系均不匹配。

Abstract: Mirror Theory proposes that an intelligent system should be studied not only by what it represents, but by what coherent continuations it can sustain under repeated reflection. We make this claim operational through \emph{viable path entropy} (VPE), a finite-budget measure of verified continuation capacity. Given a mirror state, a rollout protocol, a verifier, and a mode map, VPE decomposes bounded capability into two parts: the probability of reaching a viable continuation and the diversity of verified continuation modes reached among successful rollouts. This paper restores the full theoretical scaffold behind the measure: intuition as local underdetermining constraint, taste as invariant-selecting pressure, reflection as taste-guided resolution of underdetermination, and geometry as the learned structure that makes future reflection stable. We then instantiate the theory in language-model reasoning experiments on GSM8K. Across Qwen2.5-Instruct models, 32 sampled rollouts per problem, and two reflection horizons, increasing the token budget from 96 to 160 substantially expands verified reachability, reduces zero-reachability, increases verified-mode entropy, and improves smoothed VPE. At 160 tokens, Qwen2.5-1.5B realizes the strongest mirror horizon among the tested models, even though Qwen2.5-3B has more parameters. This shows that mirror horizon is not parameter count, but accessible verified continuation capacity under a bounded reflection protocol. The result supports Mirror Theory as a measure-level account: capability is the structure of viable continuations made reachable, not merely one-shot accuracy or pass@k.

</details>

Abstract: This book is about the mathematical foundations of data science.
  1. Introduction
  2. Curses, Blessings, and Surprises in High Dimensions
  3. Singular Value Decomposition and Principal Component Analysis
  4. Linear Regression and Regularization
  5. Graphs, Networks, and Clustering
  6. Nonlinear Dimension Reduction and Diffusion Maps
  7. Linear Dimension Reduction via Random Projections
  8. Optimization for Data Science
  9. Classification
  10. A Mathematical Introduction  to Deep Learning
  11. Large Sample Limit of Graph Laplacians
  12. Community
  13. Concentration of Measure and Gaussian Analysis
  14. Matrix Concentration Inequalities
  15. Compressive Sensing and Sparsity
  16. Low-Rank Matrix Recovery

</details>


### [157] [CARE-LoRA: Compressed Activation REconstruction for Memory-Efficient LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11940)
*Gengyu Zhang,Haiyin Ran,Zhengbao He,Yuhang Liu,Hanling Tian,Zhehao Huang,Xiaolin Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CARE-LoRA，一种数据感知的压缩激活重建框架，通过利用LoRA的投影结构压缩前向激活并重建梯度信号，降低LoRA微调过程中的激活内存瓶颈。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦大模型参数高效微调(PEFT)中的内存优化问题，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署均无直接或间接关联。虽然LoRA本身可用于遥感模型微调，但论文核心贡献是微调过程中的激活内存压缩，而非模型推理阶段的轻量化或部署优化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大模型参数高效微调(PEFT)中的内存优化；应用场景：通用NLP/CV大模型微调；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于LoRA结构，在LoRA分支上利用低秩压缩激活替代全输入激活，并引入轻量级重建矩阵用于反向传播梯度信号重建。

Lightweight method: 该方法针对的是训练/微调阶段的内存压缩（激活压缩与重建），而非推理阶段的模型轻量化（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。与星上部署所需的推理加速、参数量压缩、FLOPs降低等目标不同。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等推理部署指标。该方法优化的是微调训练内存，而非推理部署效率，不适用于星上在轨推理场景。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标，仅提及"across diverse models and downstream tasks"。

Experiments: 在多种模型和下游任务上与标准LoRA及代表性LoRA变体对比，在降低整体内存占用的同时达到竞争性或更优性能。具体数值摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化完全无关；2. 仅优化训练阶段内存，不涉及推理加速或模型压缩；3. 未讨论在边缘/星上部署场景的适用性。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。如果研究方向涉及遥感大模型在轨微调或联邦学习场景，LoRA及CARE-LoRA的激活压缩思路可能对星上有限内存下的模型自适应更新有潜在参考价值，但与本课题核心的"推理阶段轻量化部署"方向关联较弱。

Abstract: As the scale of large pre-trained models continues to grow, fine-tuning them under limited memory budgets has become increasingly challenging. Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), currently one of the most widely adopted parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, mitigates this challenge by optimizing only low-rank adaptation matrices, thereby greatly reducing the number of trainable parameters. With the parameter overhead substantially reduced, the activations retained for backpropagation have emerged as the primary remaining memory bottleneck during LoRA fine-tuning. To address this, we propose CARE-LoRA, a data-aware Compressed Activation REconstruction framework. By exploiting the inherent projection structure of LoRA, CARE-LoRA replaces the full input activation with the low-rank compressed activation naturally produced by the LoRA branch. It further computes a lightweight reconstruction matrix during the forward pass with negligible additional computation cost, which is used during backpropagation to reconstruct the gradient signal, thereby keeping LoRA matrices fully trainable. Extensive experiments across diverse models and downstream tasks demonstrate that, while substantially reducing the overall memory footprint, CARE-LoRA achieves competitive or even superior performance compared with standard LoRA and representative LoRA variants. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/fishandyu/CARE-LoRA .

</details>


### [158] [Are we Merging the Right Models? Impact of Expert Training Duration on Model Merging for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11997)
*Nikita Kozodoi,Zainab Afolabi,Jack Butler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究多任务模型合并中专家模型训练时长对合并质量的影响，发现不同合并方法对训练时长的依赖模式不同，并提出训练时长与合并方法应联合选择。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于大语言模型（LLM）的多任务模型合并（model merging），涉及Math/Code/Safety等NLP领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化（剪枝/量化/蒸馏等）。

Reading priority: 1分，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多任务模型合并（multi-task model merging）；应用场景：大语言模型（LLM）的领域专家合并；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: Qwen 3.5系列（0.8B, 2B, 4B），属于Transformer-based大语言模型。

Lightweight method: 涉及模型合并方法（model merging），包括简单平均（simple averaging）和基于稀疏化的方法（sparsification-based methods），但这不是传统意义上的轻量化（剪枝/量化/蒸馏/NAS），而是多任务模型参数融合技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。模型规模为0.8B-4B参数，但面向的是NLP任务而非遥感在轨部署。

Datasets and metrics: 五个领域（Math, Code, Instruction Following, Multilingual, Safety），具体数据集名称摘要未说明；评价指标摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在三个模型规模（0.8B/2B/4B）上微调专家模型，从最优训练步数的25%到500%保存检查点，评估五种合并方法。发现简单平均在过拟合时性能急剧下降，而基于稀疏化的方法在远超验证最优点的训练时长下表现最佳。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星/目标检测场景；2. 不涉及模型轻量化核心方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS）；3. 实验仅在LLM上进行，结论对视觉模型尤其是遥感目标检测模型的迁移性未知。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：模型合并（model merging）技术未来或可用于遥感多任务模型（如同时检测舰船、飞机、车辆）的参数融合，但本文未涉及视觉任务，且与轻量化无关。

Abstract: Multi-task model merging combines separately trained expert models into a single model that handles all tasks without co-training. Standard practice merges experts at their optimal validation loss. We challenge this convention by systematically studying how training duration of domain experts affects the quality of the merged model. We fine-tune experts on five domains (Math, Code, Instruction Following, Multilingual, and Safety) across three model sizes (Qwen 3.5 0.8B, 2B, and 4B), saving checkpoints from 25\% to 500\% of the optimal training steps and evaluating five merging methods at each duration. Our findings reveal a striking method-dependent pattern: simple averaging degrades sharply with overfitting, while sparsification-based methods achieve their best performance well past the validation optimum. We formalize this through bias-variance decomposition analysis, drawing a parallel to random forests where averaging benefits from high-variance individual learners. These results suggest that training duration and merging method should be chosen jointly rather than independently.

</details>


### [159] [How Query Visibility Changes KV-Cache Compression Rankings: A Matched-Budget Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11942)
*Daming Luo,Christy Liang,Junyu Xuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究大语言模型KV-cache压缩方法在query-agnostic（查询不可见）场景下的评估，发现查询可见性会显著改变方法排名，主流方法SnapKV在查询不可见时甚至不如简单基线。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于大语言模型（LLM）的KV-cache压缩，属于NLP/大模型推理优化领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、卫星在轨部署等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非对LLM推理优化有额外兴趣，否则可跳过。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大语言模型KV-cache压缩方法评估；应用场景：长文本推理、文档问答；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 评估对象为6种已发表的KV-cache压缩方法（SnapKV, KeyDiff等），基座模型为7-9B参数量的开源LLM。摘要未说明具体模型架构细节。

Lightweight method: 涉及KV-cache压缩（属于LLM推理加速/显存优化技术），但并非传统意义上的模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。方法包括SnapKV、KeyDiff等基于注意力机制的缓存压缩方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。评估在RULER-8192、LongBench等文本基准上进行，不涉及边缘/星上部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：RULER-8192（144,300对评估）、LongBench（40,800对评估）；评价指标：基于配对bootstrap的得分比较（50,000次重采样）；实验设置：固定模型、压缩比、实例和解码方式，仅改变评分规则（query-aware vs query-agnostic）。

Experiments: 主要发现：(1) 查询可见性改变方法排名，query-agnostic协议下仅KeyDiff一致优于最佳-of-3简单基线；(2) SnapKV在query-agnostic下平均落后于"保留开头+最近窗口"基线-0.066；(3) 两种协议间的性能下降幅度与方法对查询的可见性正相关。

Limitations: 摘要未说明：(1) 仅评估7-9B模型，未涉及更大或更小模型；(2) 未讨论压缩方法在遥感/视觉任务上的适用性；(3) 未涉及实际硬件部署的延迟和能耗指标；(4) 与遥感卫星在轨场景完全无关。

Ideas for my research: 尽管论文与遥感无关，但以下思路可借鉴：(1) query-agnostic vs query-aware的评估视角——在遥感在轨场景中，模型需处理未知的、动态到达的图像数据，类似"查询不可见"设定，可借鉴其评估方法论；(2) 简单基线（如"保留开头+最近窗口"）在特定条件下可能超越复杂方法，提示在轻量化设计中不应忽视简单启发式策略；(3) 配对bootstrap重采样评估方法可用于遥感模型在轨部署的鲁棒性评估。但整体借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: KV-cache compression methods are predominantly evaluated with the query appended to the context before compression -- a query-aware protocol. Yet the economic case for a compressed KV cache is reuse: compress a document once, answer many future questions against it. In that deployment, compression must happen query-agnostic -- before any question is seen. We present a matched-budget audit of six published compression methods against three trivial baselines on three open 7-9B models (144,300 paired evaluations on RULER-8192; 40,800 on LongBench; 50,000-resample paired bootstrap throughout). Everything is held fixed -- model, compression ratio, instances, decoding -- except the scoring rule. Three findings. (1) Query visibility changes the rankings: under the agnostic protocol, of the five audited methods that share a common attention backend, only KeyDiff beats a best-of-3 trivial baseline consistently (31 of 36 cells), and the most widely deployed method, SnapKV, loses to "keep the start and the recent window" on average (-0.066). (2) The per-method drop between the two protocols is ordered consistently with how visible the question is to each method's scoring signal, legible in its source code: from Delta=+0.198 for SnapKV (the question sits inside its 64-token observation window) down to Delta=+0.011 for KeyDiff (its score contains no query term at all).

</details>


### [160] [Cluster-Weighted EDMD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12243)
*Lorenzo Tomaz,Judd Rosenblatt,Flavio Kicis,Thomas B. Jones,Diogo Schwerz de Lucena*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Cluster-Weighted EDMD (CW-EDMD)方法，通过软相空间划分和局部Koopman算子学习，提升非线性动力系统预测精度。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于非线性动力系统的Koopman算子逼近方法（EDMD），属于计算动力学/系统辨识领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：非线性动力系统的Koopman算子逼近与预测。场景：Lorenz系统、阻尼摆、Duffing系统等经典动力系统。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: Cluster-Weighted EDMD (CW-EDMD)：联合学习软相空间划分（soft phase-space partition）和每个簇的局部EDMD算子。使用EM（Expectation-Maximization）目标函数，基于几何邻近性和预测残差分配转移。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Lorenz、阻尼摆（damped pendulum）、Duffing系统，共36种配置×10个随机种子。评价指标：一步预测误差（one-step error）、5秒滚动预测误差（5s-rollout prediction error）。

Experiments: 在288组配对比较中，CW-EDMD在258组中显著降低误差，4组增加，26组无差异。中位数一步误差降低：摆系统57倍、Duffing系统2.7倍、Lorenz系统12倍。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星/目标检测场景，无法直接迁移。2. 未讨论计算效率、模型大小、推理速度等部署指标。3. 仅在低维动力系统上验证，未在高维遥感数据上测试。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。如果硬要提取思路：其"局部模型优于全局模型"的思想（phase-space partitioning + local operators）在理论上与遥感场景中"不同地物类型需要不同检测头"的思路有微弱类比，但方法本身（Koopman算子）与目标检测/轻量化无直接关联。

Abstract: Extended Dynamic Mode Decomposition (EDMD) approximates Koopman operators from data, but a single global operator is inefficient when different state-space regions exhibit distinct local dynamics. We introduce Cluster-Weighted EDMD (CW-EDMD), which jointly learns a soft phase-space partition and a per-cluster EDMD operator. Its Expectation-Maximization (EM) objective assigns each transition based on both geometric proximity and prediction residuals, so clusters specialize where local Koopman models are accurate rather than where the data are dense. On Lorenz, damped pendulum, and Duffing systems, across 36 configurations and 10 seeds, CW-EDMD improves matched-degree EDMD in one-step and 5s-rollout prediction. Across 288 paired comparisons, there are significant error reductions in 258 cases, increases in 4, and no differences in 26. Median one-step error reductions are 57x, 2.7x, and 12x on pendulum, Duffing, and Lorenz, respectively.

</details>


### [161] [BattVAE-GP: Generative Modeling of Long-Horizon Battery Degradation with Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11943)
*Raghvender Raghvender,Mahdi Abid,Ferran Brosa Planella,Charles Delacourt,Arnaud Demortière*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个混合物理-概率学习的电池退化代理模型BattVAE-GP，用于预测锂离子电池在不同充电倍率下的退化轨迹。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是锂离子电池退化建模与健康预测，属于电池/能源领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：电池退化轨迹代理建模；应用场景：锂离子电池健康预测；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。摘要未涉及遥感、目标检测相关内容。

Model architecture: 混合框架：DFN/P2D电化学模型（PyBaMM生成数据）+ VAE（变分自编码器，用于特征编码和降维）+ 稀疏多任务高斯过程（GP，在潜在空间中进行连续插值和不确定性估计）+ 辅助SOH预测器。摘要未涉及任何目标检测网络结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。VAE和GP本身计算效率较高，但论文未从模型压缩或边缘部署角度讨论轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。虽然论文提到"computationally efficient"，但未与星上/边缘部署场景关联。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：使用PyBaMM中的DFN/P2D电化学模型生成的循环退化数据（非真实实验数据，非遥感数据集）；评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标，提及了不确定性估计行为和潜在轨迹的连贯性。

Experiments: 实验设置：协议级留出评估（protocol-level holdout evaluation）；结果：潜在空间GP能准确恢复未见过的C-rate轨迹，不确定性行为与训练数据支撑一致；在未见过的内部C-rate下，生成的潜在轨迹保持连贯。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 基于仿真数据而非真实实验数据，泛化性有待验证；3. 摘要未讨论计算效率的具体指标（如推理时间、参数量等）。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能的技术启发是：VAE+GP的混合框架思路（编码器降维+代理模型预测）在遥感领域可用于时序预测或异常检测，但与目标检测模型轻量化无直接关系。

Abstract: Long-horizon physics-based simulations of battery degradation provide mechanistic insight but remain computationally expensive, limiting their use for dense exploration of operating conditions over extended cycle life. Here, we propose a hybrid physics-probabilistic learning framework for surrogate modeling of lithium-ion battery degradation trajectories at unseen charging rates. Cycle-resolved degradation data generated with a DFN/P2D electrochemical model in PyBaMM are first transformed into capacity-aligned voltage and derivative features and encoded using a Variational Autoencoder (VAE). The resulting two-dimensional latent space organizes degradation trajectories according to both cycle progression and charging protocol. A sparse multitask Gaussian process (GP) is then trained in this latent space using cycle number and C-rate as input variables, providing continuous interpolation of latent degradation dynamics together with posterior uncertainty estimates. Under protocol-level holdout evaluation, the latent-space GP accurately recovers unseen C-rate trajectories and exhibits uncertainty behavior consistent with the support of the training data. When queried at unseen interior C-rates, the model generates latent trajectories that remain coherently positioned between neighboring simulated protocols. Decoding the GP-predicted latent states through the frozen VAE decoder yields smooth voltage-capacity evolution, while Monte Carlo propagation of the GP latent posterior through an auxiliary latent to State of Health (SOH) predictor provides uncertainty-aware SOH estimates. The proposed BattVAE-GP framework therefore offers a computationally efficient and uncertainty-aware surrogate for long-horizon degradation modeling, providing a structured basis for extending battery health prediction toward richer operating conditions and future simulation-experiment fusion.

</details>


### [162] [PolarBM: Complex-valued Boltzmann Machine for Modeling Audio Signals in Polar and Log-polar Coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12417)
*Toru Nakashika,Kohei Yatabe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Research relevance: Relevance analysis unavailable

Reading priority: Priority unavailable

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: Task and scene extraction unavailable

Model architecture: Model architecture extraction unavailable

Lightweight method: Lightweight method extraction unavailable

Onboard deployability: Onboard deployability analysis unavailable

Datasets and metrics: Datasets and metrics extraction unavailable

Experiments: Experimental analysis unavailable

Limitations: Limitations analysis unavailable

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: Although vast amounts of data, such as audio signal spectra, are naturally represented using complex numbers, conventional machine learning methods often simplify complex-domain problems by employing frameworks designed for real-valued variables. While this simplification offers computational benefits, it discards structural information regarding the inherent relationship between amplitude and phase. In this paper, we propose a novel Boltzmann machine (BM), named PolarBM, capable of naturally handling complex-valued variables in the polar coordinate (i.e., an amplitude-phase representation). PolarBM defines a probability density function for complex variables in which the phase explicitly depends on the amplitude, thereby capturing the physically important relationships of complex-valued signals. Furthermore, to process audio signals in accordance with human auditory perception, we propose LogPolarBM, which models amplitude on a logarithmic scale. This extension yields a flexible conditional probability density function, a power-weighted noncentral complex Gaussian (PW-NCCG) distribution, whose marginal amplitude distribution encompasses the Rice, Nakagami, and noncentral chi distributions as special cases. For practical applications, we also introduce the restricted variants of these proposed models: PolarRBM and LogPolarRBM. Experimental results demonstrate that by explicitly modeling the dependency between amplitude and phase, the proposed RBMs achieve superior modeling accuracy compared to conventional models, including deep neural networks. Although our experiments focus on audio signals, the utility of the proposed BMs is not limited to audio applications; their potential extends widely across various fields of science and engineering that involve complex-valued data, such as wireless communications and quantum mechanics.

</details>

Abstract: Typical semi-supervised learning (SSL) methods rely on distributional assumptions, and their performance degrades when these are violated. While PNU learning, a risk rewriting method, offers a distribution-free alternative, it is restricted to binary classification and its variance optimality remains unclear. In this paper, we propose a generalized framework that constructs unbiased risk estimators using linear combinations of component risks, subsuming PNU learning and extending to multiclass classification. We derive the minimum achievable variance, demonstrating our estimator can attain lower variance than PNU in asymmetric loss scenarios. Furthermore, we establish a generalization bound directly linking this variance reduction to improved learning performance. Based on these theoretical insights, we introduce two practical SSL methods that empirically match or outperform existing approaches on binary and multiclass benchmarks.

</details>


### [164] [Fisher Rank Inflation: A Spectral Signature of Memorization under Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12438)
*Satwik Bathula,Anand A. Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Fisher Rank Inflation现象，利用梯度谱有效秩的变化检测标签噪声，属于深度学习鲁棒训练理论，与遥感在轨目标检测无关。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。论文研究的是深度网络对标签噪声的记忆机制与梯度谱分析，属于深度学习理论/鲁棒训练方向。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、星上部署等工程优化。唯一可能的弱关联是"轻量化"概念——但本文讨论的是梯度谱的有效秩，与模型压缩/加速无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。除非课题涉及标签噪声检测或训练数据清洗，否则可忽略。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：标签噪声检测与记忆机制分析。应用场景：通用图像分类（CIFAR-10/100/10N）。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及目标检测，仅涉及图像分类。

Model architecture: SmallCNN、ResNet18、Vision Transformers（ViT）。均为通用分类网络，非遥感专用模型。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩、加速或轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性、星上/边缘部署等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、CIFAR-10N（含真实标签噪声）。评价指标：Fisher有效秩（Fisher effective rank）、top-100 noisy fraction、peak effective rank。均为理论分析指标，非目标检测mAP/AP等指标。

Experiments: 在CIFAR-10/100/10N上使用SmallCNN、ResNet18、ViT进行实验。发现有效秩在记忆阶段膨胀、拟合后收缩的轨迹。top-100噪声样本占比69.2%-96.2%（合成噪声），CIFAR-10N上94.4%±1.9%。峰值有效秩从28.88（干净训练）单调增长到97.09（60%噪声）。秩膨胀的起始点早于测试性能下降。

Limitations: 1. 仅在CIFAR小规模图像分类数据集上验证，未在遥感大图/高分辨率场景测试。2. 仅分析分类任务，未涉及目标检测（尤其是遥感旋转框检测、小目标检测）。3. 未讨论该方法在模型轻量化/压缩场景下的适用性。4. 计算Fisher梯度谱需要存储每个样本的梯度，计算开销大，不适合星上资源受限环境。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：1）如果遥感标注中存在标签噪声（如舰船/飞机标注框偏移或类别错误），Fisher秩分析可用于检测噪声样本，辅助清洗遥感数据集；2）梯度谱分析思想或可迁移到轻量化模型的训练稳定性分析中，但需大幅改造。总体而言，该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接可借鉴点很少。

Abstract: Deep networks trained with label noise often learn clean structure before memorizing corrupted labels. We show that this transition leaves a spectral signature in the centered scatter of per-example last-layer gradients. Its effective rank transiently expands during memorization and contracts after corrupted labels are fit. We call this phenomenon Fisher Rank Inflation. Corrupted labels increase effective rank by injecting spectral mass into low-energy or previously unused eigendirections, increasing the entropy of the gradient spectrum. We derive a first-order leave-one-out attribution formula, identify conditions under which corrupted examples contribute more strongly than clean examples, and explain why attribution signals weaken once the normalized Fisher-gradient spectrum stabilizes. We test these predictions on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and CIFAR-10N using SmallCNN, ResNet18, and Vision Transformers. Across settings, Fisher effective rank exhibits a consistent inflation--collapse trajectory aligned with memorization. At peak-rank checkpoints, corrupted examples are enriched among the highest rank-contributing samples, with top-100 noisy fractions from \(69.2\%\) to \(96.2\%\) across five-seed synthetic-corruption experiments and \(94.4\%\pm1.9\%\) on CIFAR-10N. First-order spectral attribution closely matches exact leave-one-out contributions in convolutional models and remains enriched in the Vision Transformer. Peak effective rank increases monotonically with corruption severity, from \(28.88\pm1.95\) under clean training to \(97.09\pm1.78\) at \(60\%\) corruption. In several settings, the retrospectively identified onset of rank inflation precedes observable test degradation. These results establish Fisher Rank Inflation as a spectral signature connecting corrupted-example enrichment, corruption severity, and the transition from structure learning to memorization.

</details>


### [165] [Scale-Aware Attention for Scarce Neural Data: An RG-Flow Transformer on Sleep-EDF EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11950)
*Dibakar Sigdel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RG-Flow Transformer，通过引入重正化群（RG）归纳偏置来建模脑电场位的1/f^β幂律频谱，用于EEG睡眠分期，但该方法与遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。论文研究的是脑电场位（EEG）信号分析与睡眠分期，属于生物医学信号处理领域。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题；也不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测等视觉检测任务；更不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署等模型压缩或部署技术。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向无任何交集。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读全文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：EEG睡眠分期（5类AASM睡眠分期）；应用场景：临床脑电信号分析；传感器类型：EEG脑电电极；目标类别：睡眠阶段（非遥感目标）。摘要未说明任何遥感相关场景。

Model architecture: RG-Flow Transformer：在标准Transformer的自注意力机制基础上，增加了一个具有可学习反常维度γ的尺度感知流（scale-aware stream）、块自旋粗粒化（block-spin coarse-graining）和熵门控同步桥（entropy-gated synchronization bridge），显式引入重正化群（RG）归纳偏置。对比基线为参数匹配的vanilla Transformer和仅层级结构的消融模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：PhysioNet Sleep-EDF corpus（EEG睡眠数据集）；评价指标：5类睡眠分期准确率（accuracy）、β恢复R²；实验设置：留一被试交叉验证（leave-one-subject-out cross-validation），5名被试、5个随机种子，严格按被试划分防止数据泄露。

Experiments: 主要结果：RG-Flow与vanilla Transformer在5类睡眠分期上统计无显著差异（77.3% vs 77.0%准确率，配对p=0.294）；在数据量受限的各预算下vanilla Transformer数值上均更优；RG-Flow的优势在于可解释性——可恢复连续频谱指数β（β恢复R²=0.416），这是vanilla架构不具备的能力。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 未提供任何轻量化或部署相关信息；3. 在分类任务上性能并未超越vanilla Transformer，仅增加了可解释性；4. 仅在EEG小样本场景验证，未在视觉/遥感图像上测试。

Ideas for my research: 1. 该论文与本研究方向基本无关，不建议作为主要参考；2. 如果硬要提取可借鉴思路：RG归纳偏置的思想（多尺度粗粒化+可学习尺度指数）在遥感图像多尺度特征融合中可能有理论上的启发，但需要大量改造才能适配视觉检测任务；3. 论文中"数据稀缺时归纳偏置的优势"这一假设的验证方法论（按被试严格划分、数据预算扫描）值得在遥感小样本场景中借鉴实验设计思路。

Abstract: Brain field potentials are scale-free: their power spectra follow a $1/f^β$ law whose aperiodic exponent $β$ tracks cortical state, and sleep depth in particular is a shift in $β$. We ask whether a transformer endowed with an explicit renormalization-group (RG) inductive bias -- the RG-Flow Transformer, which couples ordinary self-attention to a scale-aware stream with a learnable anomalous dimension $γ$, block-spin coarse-graining, and an entropy-gated synchronization bridge -- has an advantage over a parameter-matched vanilla transformer on \emph{real, scarce} EEG. Using the PhysioNet Sleep-EDF corpus with a strict leakage-free by-subject hold-out, we (i) benchmark RG-Flow against a param-matched vanilla transformer and a hierarchy-only ablation on 5-class AASM sleep staging, (ii) sweep the per-subject data budget to look for the inductive-bias crossover predicted when data are scarce, and (iii) test whether RG-Flow's learned $γ$ tracks the measured spectral exponent $β$ out-of-sample -- a quantity the vanilla model does not possess. Across $5$ subjects and $5$ seeds under leave-one-subject-out cross-validation, RG-Flow and the vanilla transformer are statistically indistinguishable on 5-class staging (77.3\% vs 77.0\% accuracy; paired $p=0.294$), and the predicted scarce-data crossover does not appear: vanilla is numerically ahead at every data-limited budget. What does separate the models is interpretability -- RG-Flow recovers the continuous spectral exponent out-of-sample ($β$-recovery $R^2 = 0.416$), a capability the vanilla architecture has no analogue for.

</details>


### [166] [What Does Goodness Measure? A Likelihood-Ratio Account of Forward-Forward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12501)
*Paolo Giannitrapani*

Main category: cs.LG

Model architecture: Forward-Forward (FF)算法，逐层局部训练，使用平方激活和作为goodness度量。论文从生成模型角度重新解释该架构的设计选择。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或任何部署相关信息。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。FF算法的逐层局部训练特性理论上可能对分布式或资源受限场景有启发，但本文未涉及任何遥感或轻量化内容，借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: The Forward-Forward (FF) algorithm trains each layer locally, so that a scalar goodness - the sum of squared activations - is high on real inputs and low on contrastive ones, with activations normalized between layers. Both choices are usually treated as heuristics. Under an explicit generative model they are not: the squared goodness is the sufficient statistic of a likelihood-ratio test between two zero-mean populations differing in scale, and the FF threshold is its boundary. It generalizes: anisotropic populations yield a Mahalanobis goodness, the plain square being its isotropic case; heavy-tailed populations yield a saturating statistic whose slope is a posterior precision - divisive normalization - with bounded evidence and an advantage only under aggregation. The same lens characterizes the inter-layer normalization: it must remove the length while preserving per-coordinate energy, explaining a depth collapse we observe under unit-norm normalization; and the pairwise objective admits a scale-inflation shortcut that a whitened goodness removes.

</details>


### [167] [Scalable Optimal Transport Algorithm for Network Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11952)
*Elaheh Hassani,Durga Mandarapu,Qi Yu,Hanghang Tong,Ariful Azad*

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关；2. 仅报告加速比，未报告绝对延迟、吞吐量、功耗等部署关键指标；3. 未涉及模型参数量或FLOPs的压缩；4. 方法高度针对网络对齐的OT计算，通用性有限。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴价值有限。可考虑的点：稀疏感知计算和自定义核融合的思路在理论上可迁移到遥感检测模型的推理加速中（如对稀疏特征图或稀疏注意力进行定制化算子优化），但论文本身未提供可直接复用于遥感目标检测的具体技术细节。

Abstract: Network alignment identifies node correspondences across different networks and is a fundamental primitive in many data science applications, including social network analysis, fraud detection, and knowledge graph integration. However, state-of-the-art network alignment methods often achieve high accuracy by repeatedly constructing and updating dense matrices, sacrificing scalability in the process. To address this scalability limitation without compromising alignment accuracy, we present FastAlign, a scalable, sparsity-aware framework for optimal transport-based network alignment. Rather than introducing a new alignment model, FastAlign preserves the original OT formulation and reinterprets its computation as a set of recurring mixed sparse-dense operations. FastAlign combines sparsity-aware graph computation with domain-specific kernel fusion, including a custom SpMM kernel. Our results show that FastAlign achieves alignment quality comparable to state-of-the-art OT-based methods while substantially reducing end-to-end runtime up to 3.89x-9.45x on CPU and 2.24x-32.54x on GPU.

</details>


### [168] [When Does Reward Teach State? A Hidden-Automaton Instrument and the Group-Language Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11953)
*Jim Allchin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种白盒框架，通过将任务建模为隐藏确定性有限自动机（DFA），来分离强化学习智能体的"高奖励"与"潜在状态学习"，证明高奖励并不等同于任务理解。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是强化学习中的表示学习与奖励机制问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：强化学习中的表示学习与状态表征分析。应用场景：合成DFA任务。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明任何与星上/边缘部署相关的信息。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性指标。

Datasets and metrics: 使用153个容量受控的合成DFA作为测试集。评价指标包括奖励值、状态探测准确率、精度（0.86）等。

Experiments: 实验表明：弱策略梯度RL下智能体获得高奖励但状态探测为随机水平；PPO+GAE可部分恢复状态但种子方差大；任务结构（置换/群语言）可提前预测感知缺口。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。该论文属于强化学习基础理论研究，不涉及任何视觉感知或部署优化问题。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文研究的是RL智能体的表示学习理论，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在问题域、方法论和应用场景上均无交集。

Abstract: Does a reinforcement-learning agent that earns high reward represent its task's latent state, or only a reward-correlated shortcut? The question is usually unanswerable: the "true state" is undefined. We make it exactly answerable with a white-box instrument: express the task as a hidden deterministic finite automaton (DFA), let the agent observe a symbol stream and intermittently choose the next symbol under partial control, and grant one sparse terminal reward for acceptance. Knowing the automaton gives two things for free: the optimal return (so reward becomes an interpretable normalized score) and the exact latent state at every step (so we can probe the agent's representation without ever showing it). Reward success and latent-state learning thus become separately measured quantities whose coupling is governed by three controllable axes. Optimizer strength: under weak on-policy RL the agent earns reward with the state probe at chance for every architecture, tempting the conclusion that sparse RL cannot install latent state; a pre-registered control overturns it -- PPO+GAE recovers the state, but only partially and with high seed variance. Task structure: permutation (group-language) structure is a warning sign computable from the transition function before any training, and held out on 153 capacity-controlled fresh automata it flags perception gaps at precision 0.86 (89 of 103), in one direction only. Observation informativeness: a label-free auxiliary is vacuous when observations carry no state and recovers it in proportion to how much they reveal. The payoff is a distinction reward-only evaluation cannot make: a perception gap (latent state not linearly recoverable, though representable) versus a planning gap (state recoverable but unused). High reward is thus not evidence of task understanding; whether an agent recovers latent state is predictable in advance.

</details>


### [169] [Graph-Constrained Policy Learning for Extreme Clinical Code Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11954)
*Amritpal Singh,Sebastian Torres,Khawar Shakeel,Syed Ahmad Chan Bukhari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出图约束遍历策略，将ICD临床代码预测转化为层次化决策过程，利用单语言模型逐层遍历剪枝后的代码层级图，在极端多标签分类任务上优于平面基线方法。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文面向临床文本（discharge summaries）的ICD-10-CM代码预测，属于自然语言处理/医学信息学领域，完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测或模型轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：临床代码预测（ICD-10-CM leaf code prediction）；场景：医院出院小结文本编码；传感器类型：不适用（纯文本数据）；目标类别：ICD-10-CM医学代码（15,761个叶子节点）。

Model architecture: 基于单语言模型（single language model）的图约束遍历策略，将ICD预测建模为有限时域决策过程，模型逐层选择有效子节点直至到达可计费叶子代码。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。文中"pruned code hierarchy"指对代码层级进行剪枝，而非模型剪枝。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。任务场景为临床文本编码，与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：MIMIC-IV discharge summaries；评价指标：micro-F1, macro-F1；实验设置：50-code curated subset和full 15,761-code space两种规模。

Experiments: 最佳监督策略SFT-1+在50-code子集上micro-F1为0.709，在完整15,761代码空间上micro-F1为0.527，优于CAML、LAAT、PLM-ICD等平面基线。完整设置下比最强平面基线提升0.044 micro-F1和0.157 macro-F1。消融实验表明增加监督轨迹数据是唯一持续提升性能的干预手段，GRPO强化学习无额外收益。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关；纯文本NLP任务，不涉及视觉感知、空间特征、实时推理等核心问题；无任何轻量化或部署相关信息。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但层次化决策/图约束遍历的思想在理论上可类比遥感旋转框检测中的层次化标签结构（如DOTA数据集中的类别层级），不过实际借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Clinical code prediction maps unstructured discharge summaries to ICD-10-CM leaf codes in a large, sparse, and deeply hierarchical label space. Most systems treat the task as flat multi-label classification, scoring codes independently and providing limited training signal for rare labels. We propose a graph-constrained traversal policy that formulates ICD prediction as a finite-horizon decision process over a pruned code hierarchy. A single language model descends the graph level by level, selecting valid child nodes until billable leaf codes are reached. This converts extreme multi-label prediction into sparse, hierarchy-aware subset decisions while guaranteeing structurally valid outputs.
  On MIMIC-IV discharge summaries, our best supervised policy, SFT-1+, achieves 0.709 micro-F1 on a curated 50-code subset and 0.527 micro-F1 on the full 15,761-code space, outperforming flat baselines including CAML, LAAT, and PLM-ICD. In the full setting, SFT-1+ improves over the strongest flat baseline by 0.044 micro-F1 and 0.157 macro-F1, suggesting that graph-constrained decomposition mitigates the rare-code bottleneck. A controlled factorial study evaluates architecture, training algorithm, and data budget. Across both scales, one shared policy matches a three-specialist cascade while avoiding its context-window overflow on 28-32% of full-space test notes. Increasing supervised trajectory data is the only intervention that consistently improves performance, while GRPO reinforcement learning provides no benefit over supervised continuation with matched data. These results show that simple graph-constrained policy learning can outperform more complex flat, cascaded, and reinforcement-learning alternatives for extreme clinical code prediction.

</details>


### [170] [Exact and Certified Data Shapley for Weighted k-Nearest-Neighbor Regression and Soft-Label Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11956)
*Zongye Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出加权KNN回归Data Shapley的首个伪多项式精确算法及FPTAS，解决了该场景下因归一化分母破坏可加性结构而长期悬而未决的计算难题。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文核心是Data Shapley值在加权KNN回归场景下的精确计算算法，属于可解释机器学习/数据估值领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：Data Shapley值计算（数据估值）；应用场景：数据点价值评估、误标注检测；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 加权KNN回归模型，非深度学习模型，无神经网络结构设计。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文讨论的是Shapley值计算的算法效率（伪多项式算法、FPTAS），而非模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等指标。仅提及开源CPU-only库。

Datasets and metrics: 12,716个对抗性实例用于验证精确算法零误差；86,400次检查验证FPTAS误差界；下游误标注检测使用TOST统计检验（n=8, p<10^-4）。

Experiments: 精确算法与穷举枚举零误差匹配；FPTAS误差界从未被违反；与Monte-Carlo方法在误标注检测任务上统计等价，但Monte-Carlo无法复现精确top-10%排名（最高3,000次排列，约128万次效用评估）。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。算法针对的是数据估值而非模型推理加速，应用场景和问题设定均不匹配。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于数据估值/可解释性方向，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无交叉。

Abstract: Data Shapley is the standard principled answer to which training points are worth what, and its k-nearest-neighbor (KNN) specialization is the version deployed in practice: the exact estimator shipped by toolkits such as pyDVL and OpenDataVal. Exact algorithms are known for unweighted KNN and for weighted KNN classification, but weighted KNN regression and soft-label prediction have resisted: the only exact method is an O(N^K) brute force, exponential in neighborhood size K. The obstruction: the weighted regression prediction is a ratio of two coalition-dependent sums, whose normalization denominator breaks the additive, threshold, and duplication structures the prior polynomial algorithms rely on. We close this gap. We give (i) the first pseudo-polynomial-time exact algorithm (polynomial in N and K at fixed lattice precision) for weighted KNN-regression Data Shapley, a counting dynamic program over the joint integer state (sum of w, sum of w*y), verified against exhaustive enumeration with zero mismatch on 12,716 adversarial instances; (ii) a certified FPTAS for continuous weights and targets, with a machine-checkable per-value error certificate never violated across 86,400 checks; (iii) a complexity landscape, including an unconditional Omega(D_w) output-size lower bound and access-model hardness results; and (iv) a weighted soft-label multi-class extension. We release an open-source, CPU-only library and the first exact weighted-regression Data Shapley ground truth. On downstream mislabel detection our exact values are statistically equivalent to Monte-Carlo Data Shapley (dataset-level TOST, n=8, p<10^-4), the pre-registered outcome; the value of exactness is instead determinism, a certified error bound, and an exact reference for auditing estimators: Monte-Carlo did not reproduce the exact top-10% ranking at any budget tested, up to 3,000 permutations (~1.28e6 utility evaluations).

</details>


### [171] [Constructed Reality, Contested Priors: Decoupling and the Architecture of Cognitive Relapse Under the Free Energy Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11958)
*MD Ibrahim Hossain Ridoy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从自由能原理出发，研究预测系统（卷积VAE+循环潜在预测器）的"本体论反转"现象，即系统默认生成行为从基线域转向目标域又部分回退的"认知复发"问题，属于认知科学/计算神经科学领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文讨论的是自由能原理下的预测编码与本体论反转（ontological inversion）问题，属于计算神经科学/认知科学范畴。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测、遥感场景理解；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT。

Reading priority: 1分。完全不相关，不建议阅读。该论文属于计算神经科学/认知科学领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向无任何关联。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：研究预测系统的本体论反转与认知复发现象。应用场景：认知科学/计算神经科学。传感器类型：合成视觉域（baseline domain和target domain），非遥感传感器。目标类别：无。

Model architecture: 卷积变分自编码器（convolutional variational autoencoder）+ 循环潜在预测器（recurrent latent predictor）。优化目标为证据下界（ELBO），与变分自由能数学等价（符号相反）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法。无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等内容。

Onboard deployability: 完全不涉及星上/边缘部署。未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 使用合成视觉域（baseline domain和target domain），非公开遥感数据集。评价指标：表征精度（representational accuracy，0.97-0.998）和默认行为（default behavior）。共90次实验（full sweep of 90 runs）。

Experiments: 主要发现：表征精度始终保持在0.97-0.998的高水平，与rehearsal ratio r无关；而默认行为完全由r决定，两者出现显著解耦（decoupling of learning from acceptance）。在中等r值时，系统默认输出先向目标域上升，然后部分回退到基线域，这种现象被称为"认知复发"（cognitive relapse）。

Limitations: 摘要明确说明"nothing further"（仅作为计算存在性证明）。与遥感目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的模型结构、压缩方法或部署策略。局限性在于其研究问题本身属于认知科学，不涉及工程应用。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文研究的是认知科学中的本体论反转问题，使用的模型（卷积VAE+循环预测器）和任务（预测编码下的域转移行为）与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化没有任何交集。不建议投入时间阅读。

Abstract: Under the free energy principle, a predictive system does not observe reality directly; it maintains a generative model of the world and experiences that model's best current hypothesis. Can a synthetic environment be made consistent enough that a predictive system's own inference machinery adopts it as this default hypothesis, permanently displacing the environment that first shaped it? We call this state ontological inversion. Because inducing and monitoring such a transition in a nervous system is neither ethical nor technically feasible, we study the underlying computational problem through a controlled proxy: a convolutional variational autoencoder paired with a recurrent latent predictor, whose evidence lower bound objective is mathematically identical, up to sign, to variational free energy itself. The network is trained first on a baseline visual domain, then on a mixed stream in which a swept rehearsal ratio r controls how much baseline content persists during transition to a target domain. Representational capacity, what the latent space can discriminate, is tracked separately from default behavior, what the system generates when left unconstrained. Across a full sweep of 90 runs, the two diverge sharply: representational accuracy stays near ceiling, 0.97 to 0.998, regardless of r, while default behavior spans nearly the system's entire range depending on r alone, a decoupling of learning from acceptance. More strikingly, at intermediate r the system's default output rises toward the target domain, then partially reverts toward the baseline while training continues unchanged, a structural failure we term cognitive relapse. Resistance to reality-adoption is not reducible to learning speed; it is a structural property with its own distinct failure modes, established here as a computational existence proof and nothing further.

</details>


### [172] [Evaluating Reliability in Machine Learning Models for Early Chronic Kidney Disease Prediction: A Systematic Review of Data Leakage and Predictor Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11963)
*Mashrul Hossain,Nafesa Kibria,Fahim Shahriar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文是一篇关于慢性肾病（CKD）早期预测的机器学习系统性文献综述，重点分析了数据泄露对性能评估的影响，并提出了泄露评分框架。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文主题是医疗健康领域的慢性肾病预测，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一个核心要素。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：慢性肾病（CKD）早期预测的文献综述与可靠性评估。应用场景：医疗健康。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型结构。论文是对已有CKD预测研究的系统性综述，而非提出新模型。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：通过系统检索学术数据库筛选出19篇相关研究。评价指标：主要关注准确率（Accuracy），高泄露研究平均95.48%，无泄露研究平均80.2%。

Experiments: 论文提出了信息泄露分类体系和定量泄露评分框架，分析了泄露与性能膨胀之间的关系，并进行了跨研究特征稳定性分析，发现超过80%的预测因子缺乏可靠性。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。论文聚焦医疗领域的数据泄露方法论问题，不涉及视觉检测或模型压缩。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。但论文中关于"数据泄露导致性能虚高"的分析思路，在遥感目标检测领域的数据集构建和评估中也有警示意义——例如在遥感视频流或时序数据中，应避免训练集和测试集之间的时空相关性导致的信息泄露。

Abstract: The early detection of Chronic Kidney Disease using machine learning has attracted significant interest in healthcare-related computer science. Despite rapid advancements in this field, many reported studies remain inconsistent and potentially misleading. A significant drawback is the lack of organized evaluation regarding methodological concerns. Key issues include data leakage, limited access to temporal patient records and inconsistency in reported clinical indicators. This research offers a systematic literature review of existing CKD prediction studies using interpretable machine learning techniques, where nineteen relevant studies were selected via systematic searches across major academic databases. To assess methodological reliability, this study introduces a structured taxonomy of information leakage and a quantitative leakage scoring framework to systematically evaluate reliability across CKD prediction studies. The analysis reveals a strong relationship between leakage and inflated performance. Here, High leakage-studies report an average accuracy of 95.48%, compared to 80.2% for leakage-free studies, reflecting an increase of approximately 15.28%. Furthermore, a cross-study feature stability analysis shows that only a small subset of predictors is consistently reproducible, with over 80% lacking reliability. Overall, the findings suggest that many reported performance improvements stem from methodological limitations rather than true predictive capability.

</details>


### [173] [LIDAR-AD: A Decoder-Free Latent-Interaction Dreamer with Action-Residual Chains for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11964)
*Yongzhi Liu,Yang Xiao,Zhong Cao,Zeng Kang,Sunan Zhang,Zhaozhi Dong,Guojun Yu,Weichao Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种用于自动驾驶的无解码器潜交互世界模型LIDAR-AD，通过冗余缩减的潜对齐和残差动作链实现长时域闭环决策。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦自动驾驶（autonomous driving）场景下的世界模型与决策，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关领域；不涉及目标检测任务；不涉及模型轻量化、剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、边缘/星上部署等任何轻量化或部署技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：自动驾驶闭环决策（closed-loop decision making）；应用场景：动态交通环境（dynamic traffic environments）；传感器类型：多源观测（multi-source observations），具体传感器类型摘要未说明；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。

Model architecture: LIDAR-AD（Latent-Interaction Dreamer with Action-Residual Chains），无解码器（decoder-free）潜空间世界模型，包含冗余缩减的潜对齐（redundancy-reduced latent alignment）、残差动作链（residual-action chains）、残差动作序列对比学习（residual-action sequence contrastive learning）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：nuPlan衍生的日志重建场景（nuPlan-derived log-reconstructed scenarios）以及多种模拟驾驶场景；评价指标：reward（奖励值）、success rate（成功率）。

Experiments: 在多种模拟驾驶场景中，LIDAR-AD持续优于世界模型基线方法，取得最高奖励和最佳成功率；在nuPlan真实交通布局场景中验证了迁移性。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关；2. 仅针对自动驾驶决策任务，不涉及任何目标检测或视觉感知任务；3. 未讨论模型效率、参数量、推理速度等部署相关指标。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无直接可借鉴点。若强行关联，其"冗余缩减的潜对齐"思想（去除控制无关冗余）在概念上可类比于特征压缩/特征蒸馏的思路，但方法本身与遥感目标检测场景差异极大，不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires long-horizon closedloop decision making in dynamic traffic environments. Latent world models offer an effective framework for this problem by enabling imagination-based decision making in compact latent spaces. However, multi-source observations contain controlirrelevant redundancy, whereas reliable driving decisions rely on risk-relevant relations, future dynamics, and continuous action adjustments. This mismatch makes observation reconstruction and absolute action modeling suboptimal for learning decisionrelevant latent dynamics. We propose LIDAR-AD, a decoderfree Latent-Interaction Dreamer with Action-Residual Chains for autonomous driving. LIDAR-AD replaces observation reconstruction with redundancy-reduced latent alignment, encouraging compact representations of risk-relevant relations in multi-source driving inputs. It further models vehicle control as residual action updates and uses residual-action sequence contrastive learning to align multi-step residual-driven rollouts with future latent states. A deterministic analysis shows that the latent-tanh residual parameterization preserves interior action reachability while representing smooth long-horizon control as compact local updates. Together, these designs improve risk-aware state abstraction, continuous-control modeling, and long-horizon dynamics prediction. Extensive experiments across diverse simulated driving scenarios demonstrate that LIDAR-AD consistently outperforms world-model baselines, achieving the highest reward and the best success rate among learning-based methods. Evaluations on nuPlan-derived log-reconstructed scenarios further demonstrate the transferability of LIDAR-AD under real-world traffic layouts.

</details>


### [174] [Beyond Coordinate Gauge: An Audited Protocol for Detecting Donor-Specific Functional Fingerprints after Neural Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11967)
*Truong Xuan Khanh,Phan Thanh Duc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究独立训练神经网络间的功能指纹可检测性，利用Neural Collapse现象和仿射对齐方法在MNIST上验证不同网络间的功能差异是否可区分。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化，属于纯机器学习/神经网络可解释性研究。

Reading priority: 1分，与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：跨网络功能指纹检测（detectability）；应用场景：纯机器学习理论研究；传感器类型：无；目标类别：MNIST手写数字。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体网络结构，仅提及独立训练的神经网络在MNIST上重建Neural Collapse。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 完全不涉及星上/边缘部署，无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：MNIST；评价指标：有序配对识别准确率（20个有序供体-受体对全部正确识别），精确置换检验p=0.0083。

Experiments: 使用5个独立训练的神经网络，通过仿射校正对齐将供体头部映射到受体坐标，验证供体特异性功能指纹在受体基线校正后仍可区分，并通过泄漏审计验证鲁棒性。

Limitations: 仅验证了detectability（可检测性），未验证transplantability（可移植性）和causal persistence（因果持久性）；仅在MNIST上验证，泛化性未知。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，几乎无可借鉴点。该论文研究的是跨网络功能指纹对齐与检测，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化无直接关联。唯一可能的间接启发是：如果未来研究涉及多颗卫星上部署不同版本的轻量化检测模型，可能需要考虑模型间特征空间的对齐问题，但非常间接。

Abstract: Independently trained neural networks have no shared neuron-index reference frame, so comparing them requires accounting for coordinate freedom. Neural Collapse sharpens this problem: networks converge toward a shared, low-dimensional geometry, raising the question of whether trajectory-specific functional variation remains distinguishable after convergence. We distinguish three claims - detectability, transplantability, and causal persistence - and address the first. Using five independently trained networks reconstructing Neural Collapse on MNIST, we apply a verified affine-correct alignment mapping donor heads into recipient coordinates. Donor-specific functional fingerprints remain distinguishable after recipient-level baseline correction: all 20 ordered donor-recipient pairs are correctly identified, with an exact permutation p=0.0083, robust to a leakage audit. These findings establish detectability under the test used here, but not transplantability or causal persistence. The study shows how alignment, ambiguity diagnostics, and leakage control combine to test cross-network variation in a controlled setting; whether this generalizes beyond it is open.

</details>


### [175] [Self-Evolving In-Context Learning for Direct Pilot-to-Beamformer Design in MU-MISO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11970)
*Yubo Zhang,Xiaodong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种增强型上下文学习（ICL）框架，用于多用户MISO系统中的导频波束赋形，通过ICL-Transformer骨干网络和上下文数据集实现无需重训练的多信道模型适应。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于无线通信领域的波束赋形（beamforming）问题，属于物理层通信信号处理范畴，完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星观测、目标检测或模型轻量化部署等核心内容。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文属于无线通信物理层领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：多用户MISO系统中的导频波束赋形（pilot-based beamforming）；场景：无线通信；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: ICL-Transformer骨干网络 + 导频编码器-解码器网络（pilot EDN）+ 波束赋形器EDN（beamformer EDN）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何模型轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 仿真环境（simulation results over diverse communication environments），未说明具体数据集名称；评价指标为sum-rate（和速率）等通信系统指标。

Experiments: 消融实验验证了上下文学习架构和增强训练策略的有效性；与WMMSE基准和现有Transformer方法对比，所提方案在导频设置下持续优于已有方案。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化内容。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与本人研究方向无关，无可借鉴点。

Abstract: We develop an enhanced in-context learning (ICL) framework to improve the performance of pilot-based beamforming in multi-user multiple-input single-output (MU-MISO) systems. The proposed scheme integrates the ICL-Transformer backbone with the pilot encoder-decoder network (EDN) and the beamformer EDN. A crucial feature of our ICL network is that it can handle multiple channel models without retraining, enabled by the construction of model-specific context datasets.
