<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：结构化地图学习（拓扑图恢复）、环境认知建模。应用场景：网格世界、MNIST图像序列。传感器类型：抽象符号观测、手写数字图像。目标类别：无目标检测任务。

Model architecture: gradCSCG（可微CSCG模块）+ VQ-VAE（向量量化变分自编码器）感知前端。CSCG是规范海马体模型，gradCSCG将其改造为全可微模块，通过软发射前向传播使地图学习目标能反向传播到感知模块。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未涉及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。该工作为概念验证性质，未考虑边缘或星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：符号网格世界（aliased observations）、MNIST图像序列（每个位置对应一个数字，每次访问采样新图像）。评价指标：边精度（edge precision）、边召回率（edge recall），用于评估恢复的邻接图质量。

Experiments: 实验1：在符号网格世界上，梯度训练再现了CSCG的结果，从严重混叠的观测中恢复房间拓扑。实验2：在MNIST图像序列上，端到端流水线成功从视觉输入中恢复底层邻接图，在四个严重混叠环境中取得高边精度和召回率。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及任何轻量化或部署考虑；3. 仅在小型网格世界和MNIST上验证，未在真实遥感场景测试；4. 未报告计算开销、推理速度等工程指标；5. 概念验证性质，离实际应用较远。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：VQ-VAE作为感知前端将连续图像映射为离散编码的思路，在遥感图像压缩或星上特征提取中可能有参考价值；但整体方向（拓扑地图学习）与在轨目标检测轻量化差异较大。

Abstract: How can an agent build a structured map of its world from nothing but an ongoing sequence of raw sensory input and its own movements, especially when natural variation means exact sensory patterns rarely repeat? The Clone-Structured Causal Graph algorithm (CSCG), a normative hippocampus model, shows how an interpretable map can be learned from aliased observations. However, CSCG requires a predefined discrete alphabet, and its expectation-maximization formulation is not easily combined with existing neural network modules, preventing the end-to-end processing of raw image sequences. We remove this barrier by reformulating CSCG as a single, fully differentiable module, gradCSCG, and coupling it to a learned vector-quantized variational autoencoder (VQ-VAE) perceptual front-end. A soft emission forward pass allows the map-learning objective to flow back into perception, while a set of loss-balancing mechanisms mitigates module collapse during joint training. We demonstrate, first, that gradient training reproduces CSCG's results on original symbolic grid worlds by recovering room topology from heavily aliased observations. Second, we show that map recovery remains robust on MNIST image sequences, where each visit to a location yields a newly sampled image of its assigned digit. Across four heavily aliased environments, the end-to-end pipeline successfully uncovers the underlying adjacency graph with high edge precision and recall, directly from visual input. This work provides a proof of principle that CSCG can serve as a composable building block in a deep learning architecture.

</details>


### [204] [ReDiTT: Retrieval Augmented Conditional Diffusion Transformers for Asynchronous Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12391)
*Saiyue Lyu,Zhitian Zhang,Ruizhi Deng,Thibaut Durand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ReDiTT，一种基于检索增强的条件扩散Transformer，用于异步时间序列预测（事件类型和间隔时间预测）。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文研究的是异步时间序列预测（event prediction），不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、轻量化模型或星上部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：异步时间序列预测（预测下一事件间隔时间和事件类型）。场景：通用时间序列预测，非遥感场景。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：事件类型，非视觉目标。

Model architecture: ReDiTT：检索增强的条件扩散Transformer（conditional diffusion transformer），在潜在空间（latent space）中运行，包含记忆库（memory bank）和交叉注意力（cross attention）机制。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 7个真实世界数据集。评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标名称，但提及next event prediction和long horizon forecasting的性能。

Experiments: 在7个真实数据集上达到state-of-the-art性能，包括下一事件预测和长程预测任务。

Limitations: 与遥感目标检测和轻量化完全无关，无法提供任何相关局限性分析。摘要未说明计算开销、推理速度等关键部署信息。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。扩散模型（diffusion model）和检索增强（retrieval augmented）的思想在遥感目标检测中可能有潜在借鉴价值（例如用于数据增强或特征增强），但本论文本身不涉及遥感或检测任务，借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: We present a diffusion based model for asynchronous time series prediction, where the goal is to predict the next inter event time and event type. To address the inherent uncertainty of future events, we introduce ReDiTT, a retrieval augmented conditional diffusion transformer that operates in latent space. ReDiTT retrieves structurally similar latent sequences from a memory bank during both training and inference and incorporates them as reference conditions through cross attention. This retrieval based conditioning allows the model to attend to relevant temporal dynamics and provides global structural guidance for generation. As a result, ReDiTT stabilizes long horizon forecasting and improves sample diversity. Experiments on seven real world datasets demonstrate state of the art performance on next event prediction and long horizon forecasting. Our code is available at https://github.com/BorealisAI/ReDiTT.

</details>


### [205] [Mechanical Analysis of Parachute Suspension Line Deployment with Binding Tapes Using PINN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12409)
*Xiang Zhao,Ronghui Quan,Yaqi Xiao,Junlin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种物理信息神经网络(PINN)用于降落伞伞绳拉直阶段的张力预测，相比传统数值积分方法在计算效率和精度上有提升。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是降落伞伞绳拉直过程中的张力预测问题，属于航空航天中的降落伞动力学领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一个核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。论文研究降落伞动力学问题，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：降落伞伞绳拉直阶段的张力预测；场景：航空/航天/救生任务中的降落伞部署；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 物理信息神经网络(PINN, Physics-Informed Neural Network)，具体网络结构摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到PINN相比传统数值积分方法在"计算效率"上有提升，但未给出量化指标。

Datasets and metrics: 验证方式：与飞行试验数据和传统数值结果进行对比验证；具体数据集名称和评价指标摘要未说明。

Experiments: 进行了与飞行试验数据和传统数值方法的对比验证，验证了PINN框架的可靠性和有效性；还研究了捆绑带参数对线动态张力的调节规律。具体数值结果摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关；2. 未涉及任何目标检测任务；3. 未涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法；4. 未提供计算效率提升的量化指标。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无直接可借鉴点。唯一可能的间接启发是：PINN方法将物理约束引入神经网络训练，这种"物理信息引导"的思路或许可以类比到遥感目标检测中——例如利用遥感图像的几何先验（地物尺度分布、空间关系等）来引导轻量化模型训练，但关联性非常弱，不推荐深入阅读。

Abstract: Parachutes are widely utilized in aviation, aerospace and lifesaving missions. As the initial stage of parachute deployment, suspension line extraction and straightening directly determines the smooth implementation of subsequent inflation procedures. This ultra-short process involves intricate dynamic load variations. Most existing studies adopt numerical integration of ordinary differential equations to calculate line tension, yet this method fails to rapidly acquire tension values at arbitrary positions along suspension lines. This paper develops a physics-informed neural network (PINN) algorithm for tension prediction during line extraction and straightening, which outperforms traditional integration methods in both computational efficiency and numerical accuracy. Furthermore, the regulatory law of binding tape parameters on line dynamic tension is investigated. Comparative validations against flight test data and conventional numerical results verify the reliability and effectiveness of the proposed PINN framework.

</details>


### [206] [The Computational Basis of Confidence in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12447)
*Dharshan Kumaran,Viorica Patraucean,Maks Ovsanikov,Petar Veličković,Nathaniel Daw*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多模态语言模型中置信度信号的计算机制，提出统计决策置信度（SDC）框架，分析答案logit差异是否反映潜在决策变量。

Research relevance: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦多模态语言模型的置信度校准与计算神经科学解释，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多模态语言模型的置信度估计与校准；应用场景：感知判别任务、记忆决策任务、视觉推理任务；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。涉及三类多模态非推理模型和一个推理模型，但未给出模型名称或结构细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩、加速或轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：三个感知判别任务和一个基于记忆的决策任务；评价指标：正确/错误折叠X模式（folded-X pattern）、正确性预测能力、校准度。

Experiments: 在三个感知判别任务和一个记忆决策任务上验证了LD满足SDC预测的定性特征（包括折叠X模式）；在复杂视觉推理任务中LD仍能预测正确性但完整几何特征缺失。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。论文本身局限：SDC框架在复杂视觉推理中完整几何特征缺失，且仅适用于有显式规范过程模型的场景。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。论文研究的是语言模型置信度，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化课题无交集。

Abstract: Reliable confidence -- the probability that a model's own answer is correct -- is essential for the trustworthy deployment of language models. Existing work has largely evaluated confidence by how well it predicts correctness and whether it is calibrated, leaving open a more fundamental question: what does the confidence signal itself represent? Answer logits may reflect a latent decision variable sufficient to compute normative confidence, or instead a heuristic preference signal that combines the available evidence in a non-Bayesian manner. We address this using statistical decision confidence (SDC), a normative framework from computational neuroscience. Treating the answer-logit difference (LD) as a candidate readout of the latent decision variable, we test the qualitative signatures predicted by SDC. Across three perceptual discrimination tasks and a memory-based decision task, spanning three multimodal non-reasoning models and one reasoning model, LD satisfied these signatures -- including the diagnostic correct/error folded-X pattern -- showing that, in these settings, answer logits behave as monotonic readouts of a latent decision variable rather than heuristic preference scores. In complex visual reasoning, LD continued to predict correctness beyond objective task difficulty, but the full geometric signatures of SDC were absent, illustrating the current boundary of the framework when explicit normative process models are unavailable. These results provide a computational account of confidence in multimodal language models, delineate when answer logits behave as readouts of a latent decision variable, and establish SDC as a unifying framework for studying confidence across biological and artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [207] [Exploring Zero-Shot Foundation Models for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12454)
*Martin Uray,Saverio Messineo,Roland Kwitt,Stefan Huber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 探究将单变量时序预测基础模型TimesFM零样本应用于多变量时序异常检测（MTSAD），发现其效果不佳，但可用于变化点检测。

Research relevance: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦工业时序异常检测（SWaT水处理数据集），不涉及遥感图像、卫星、目标检测、轻量化或边缘部署等任何相关要素。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：多变量时序异常检测（MTSAD）与变化点检测。场景：工业过程监控（水处理系统）。传感器类型：时序传感器（非遥感图像）。目标类别：异常/正常状态。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: TimesFM（时序基础模型，单变量预测FM）。两种策略：(1) 作为逐特征预测器 + 阈值化预测误差；(2) 作为嵌入器 + 标准异常检测器。摘要未说明具体网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩、剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或星上/边缘部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Secure Water Treatment (SWaT) benchmark。评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标（如F1、precision/recall等），仅提及"not competitive with established baselines"和"partial separation"。实验设置：零样本（zero-shot），无任务特定训练。

Experiments: 两种零样本策略均不优于已有基线方法。嵌入表示仅能部分区分正常与异常片段。FM对时序动态捕捉过强，在完全异常窗口内预测误差仍很低，导致持续异常无法与正常行为区分。但误差在异常边界处出现峰值，表明FM可检测分布变化（变化点检测）。

Limitations: (1) 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；(2) 零样本FM在MTSAD任务上效果差；(3) 未讨论模型效率或部署问题；(4) 仅使用单一数据集（SWaT），泛化性存疑。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的间接启发：基础模型在特定下游任务中可能因"过于有效"地捕捉数据分布而导致检测失效——这提示在遥感目标检测中，若使用预训练基础模型进行零样本或微调，需警惕模型对遥感图像中背景纹理/噪声的过度拟合，导致异常目标（如小目标）被"平滑"掉。但整体而言，该论文对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的参考价值很低。

Abstract: Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection (MTSAD) is essential for reliability and safety in domains such as industrial process monitoring and financial risk management, yet conventional approaches rely on application-specific models that are costly to train and hard to scale. Foundation Models (FMs), pre-trained on broad data with strong zero-shot generalization, have recently become available for univariate time series forecasting, raising the question of whether they can address MTSAD without task-specific training. We investigate the zero-shot application of a univariate forecasting FM, TimesFM, to industrial MTSAD on the Secure Water Treatment (SWaT) benchmark, evaluating two strategies: treating the FM as a per-feature forecaster with thresholded prediction errors, and as an embedder whose intermediate representations feed standard outlier detectors. Neither of our proposed setups is competitive with established baselines; embeddings reveal only partial separation between normal and anomalous segments, insufficient for reliable detection. The cause is that the FM is too effective at capturing temporal dynamics, yielding low error even within fully anomalous windows, so persistent anomalies become indistinguishable from normal behavior. However, these observations yield valuable insights: the error peaks at anomaly boundaries, indicating FMs reliably detect distribution changes. We conclude that the proposed naive zero-shot FMs are unsuitable for MTSAD but promising for change-point detection.

</details>


### [208] [Sample Efficient Generative Optimization for Molecular Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12488)
*Sarina Kopf,Cristina Nevado,Philippe Schwaller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SEGO框架，通过贝叶斯优化与生成模型结合，在极少的oracle调用次数下实现高效的分子优化。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于药物发现、材料设计、催化领域的分子优化问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：分子优化（molecular optimization）；应用场景：药物发现、材料设计、催化；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：化学分子。

Model architecture: SEGO框架包含：概率代理模型（probabilistic surrogate model）、生成模型（generative model）、采集函数（acquisition function）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文关注的是样本效率（sample efficiency），即减少oracle调用次数，而非模型本身的轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: PMO benchmark（Practical Molecular Optimization benchmark）；多参数对接任务（multiparameter docking task）；评价指标：oracle调用次数、命中数（hits）。

Experiments: 在PMO benchmark上，SEGO仅用其他方法十分之一的oracle调用次数即达到SOTA性能；在多参数对接任务上，达到10个命中所需的oracle调用次数约为现有方法的一半。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但该工作与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法直接借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。SEGO中的贝叶斯优化+生成模型思想在理论上可类比于在轨场景下用少量标注样本高效搜索最优轻量化架构（类似NAS），但论文本身未涉及任何遥感、检测或轻量化内容，跨领域迁移需要大量额外工作。

Abstract: Molecular optimization in drug discovery, materials design, and catalysis requires searching vast chemical spaces under tight evaluation budgets, since high-fidelity oracles and experimental measurements are costly. The practical impact of an optimization method therefore hinges on its sample efficiency: how few evaluations it needs to find strong candidates. We introduce Sample Efficient Generative Optimization (SEGO), a framework for Bayesian optimization on adaptively generated molecules. In SEGO, a probabilistic surrogate model forms a hypothesis about where hits lie in chemical space, a generative model is steered to propose candidates in that region, the most promising candidate is selected via an acquisition function, and the resulting oracle call is used both to sharpen the surrogate and to anchor the generator in real reward. SEGO attains state-of-the-art performance on the practical molecular optimization (PMO) benchmark using only one tenth of the oracle calls consumed by other methods, and on a multiparameter docking task it reaches ten hits in roughly half the oracle calls of existing approaches. These gains move molecular optimization closer to campaigns driven by direct experimental feedback.

</details>


### [209] [Adversarial Attacks on Online Handwriting using Salience-based Temporal Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12500)
*Yataro Tamura,Brian Kenji Iwana,Jiseok Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于时序显著性引导的在线手写识别对抗攻击方法，通过插入/删除笔迹点而非加噪来生成对抗样本。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是在线手写识别（online handwriting recognition）的对抗攻击，属于时序序列识别任务，与遥感卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化均无任何关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 在线手写识别（online handwriting recognition），传感器为笔迹轨迹时序数据，目标类别为手写字符。与遥感/卫星/目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构，仅提及基于梯度激活映射（gradient-based activation mapping）估计时序显著性。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署，未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: Unipen和CASIA-OLHWDB数据集；攻击成功率、白盒/单次黑盒迁移性能等对抗攻击指标。

Experiments: 在白盒和单次黑盒攻击设置下进行实验，所提时序编辑攻击在单次黑盒迁移性上优于传统图像对抗攻击，且能保持手写笔迹的视觉结构。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文研究的是手写识别对抗攻击，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Deep learning models for online handwriting recognition have been shown effective and are increasingly deployed in practical applications. However, their vulnerability to adversarial attacks is still a challenge. Existing adversarial methods are predominantly designed for image-based inputs and typically rely on additive spatial perturbations. When applied to online handwriting, which is inherently represented as a time series of pen trajectories, such perturbations often introduce high-frequency jitter and visibly unnatural stroke artifacts. In this work, we propose a novel adversarial attack framework for online handwriting recognition based on salience-guided temporal editing. Instead of adding noise, the proposed method generates adversarial examples by inserting and deleting points at time steps selected according to temporal salience, preserving the shape and smoothness of the original handwriting. Temporal salience is estimated using gradient-based activation mapping, which guides edits toward time steps that strongly support the original class prediction. We evaluate the proposed approach on the Unipen and CASIA-OLHWDB datasets under both white-box and one-shot black-box attack settings. Experimental results demonstrate that while conventional image-based attacks achieve strong white-box performance, they exhibit poor transferability across models. In contrast, the proposed temporal editing attack achieves stronger one-shot black-box transferability while preserving the visual structure of the handwriting. These results indicate that temporal editing is a relevant threat model for online handwriting recognition, particularly in one-shot black-box transfer settings.

</details>


### [210] [OOD-RL-Bench: A Benchmark Framework for Out-of-Distribution Detection in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12523)
*Emil Mittag,Richard Dazeley,Peter Vamplew*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一个用于评估强化学习智能体在轨迹中异常检测（OOD检测）的基准框架OOD-RL-Bench，不涉及遥感、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于强化学习中的分布外（OOD）检测基准构建，不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测任务，也不涉及任何模型轻量化或边缘部署技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：强化学习轨迹中的OOD异常检测；应用场景：RL智能体的鲁棒性评估；传感器类型：不涉及遥感传感器；目标类别：不涉及。使用LunarLander-v3仿真环境。

Model architecture: 使用Deep Q-Network (DQN)策略，不涉及目标检测模型架构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：LunarLander-v3仿真环境（非遥感数据集）；评价指标：matched-time AUROC, matched-time AUPRC, matched-time false-positive rate, detection delay, segmented-onset metrics。

Experiments: 在LunarLander-v3环境中使用DQN策略评估多种OOD检测器，发现观测扰动和状态切换可被高精度识别，而观测延迟和动作条件动力学仍具挑战性。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。仅针对RL轨迹OOD检测，不涉及任何视觉目标检测、遥感场景理解或模型压缩技术。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。唯一可能的间接启发是：在轨部署的RL智能体（如卫星自主决策）可能也需要OOD检测来应对传感器故障或环境变化，但本文未涉及遥感场景，且不讨论轻量化部署问题。

Abstract: Reliable reinforcement learning (RL) agents must maintain operational integrity amidst sensor malfunctions, dynamic disturbances, and slow environmental shifts. The detection of out-of-distribution conditions is pivotal to determining when an agent's observations, transitions, or trajectory dynamics deviate from the assumptions underpinning its policy training. Current out-of-distribution (OOD) detection benchmarks typically evaluate image classifiers or static low-dimensional datasets, failing to account for the complex, action-dependent temporal structure inherent in RL trajectories. To address this gap, we present OOD-RL-Bench, a comprehensive and extensible framework designed to evaluate OOD detectors against categories of anomalies injected into RL trajectories. Detectors and anomaly injectors are integrated through shared interfaces and configuration, which allows new scoring methods and perturbation families to be evaluated without modification of the core benchmark loop. We evaluate the utility of the framework using a Deep Q-Network policy within the LunarLander-v3 environment. We assess the performance of each detector across a suite of anomaly types using matched-time AUROC, matched-time AUPRC, matched-time false-positive rate, detection delay, and segmented-onset metrics. Our analysis reveals significant performance variance across anomaly types: observation perturbations and regime switches are identified with high accuracy by several methods, while observation delay and action-conditioned dynamics remain difficult even when post-onset anomaly scores are compared against clean scores from the same timesteps. We make the framework, trained policy checkpoint, and complete results publicly available as a reproducible artefact.

</details>


### [211] [From Preimage Search To Source-Grounded Feature Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12526)
*Kaixiang Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Research relevance: Relevance analysis unavailable

Reading priority: Priority unavailable

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: Task and scene extraction unavailable

Model architecture: Model architecture extraction unavailable

Lightweight method: Lightweight method extraction unavailable

Onboard deployability: Onboard deployability analysis unavailable

Datasets and metrics: Datasets and metrics extraction unavailable

Experiments: Experimental analysis unavailable

Limitations: Limitations analysis unavailable

Ideas for my research: Research ideas unavailable

Abstract: Interpreting a neural network requires understanding what its internal features extract from a particular input. Feature inversion seeks to express a selected feature in the input domain, but canonical iterative methods search for an input whose re-encoded representation matches the target. Because many inputs can satisfy this constraint, target matching alone does not specify the inverse associated with the sample that generated the feature. We formulate source-grounded feature inversion by conditioning the inverse on the source-local network geometry at the target-generating input. At each boundary of the computational DAG, backpropagation provides the correct reverse dependencies but transports an adjoint signal rather than an upstream-state estimate. We locally repair this signal with a closed-form matrix Wiener map from a mean-seed VJP to the upstream state, followed by a second Wiener map for the JVP forward-consistency residual, and compose the repaired states through the same DAG in one finite reverse pass. One calibrated zero-intercept map family supports new inputs, depths, channels, and channel groups across diverse CNN and Transformer architectures, tensor components, and visual distributions without query-specific optimisation. Matched target and source controls verify that each inverse depends on the selected feature and the local operators of the sample being explained, rather than a target-independent image template. Prediction-conditioned feature atlases align these visualisations with independent interventions on the corresponding internal features. Together, source-grounded feature inversion opens the model's hidden feature hierarchy to inspection at the level of individual layers and channels, linking what the network extracts from an input to the internal evidence that shapes its decision.

</details>


### [212] [A JoLT for the KV Cache: Near-Lossless KV Cache Compression via Joint Tucker and JL-Residual Allocation for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12550)
*Rahul Krishnan,Volker Schulz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出JoLT方法，利用部分Tucker分解对Transformer推理中的KV缓存进行张量压缩，实现近无损2-3倍压缩，降低显存占用。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于大语言模型(LLM)推理中的KV缓存压缩，不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测或轻量化部署中的模型压缩（剪枝/量化/蒸馏等），属于纯NLP/大模型推理优化工作。

Reading priority: 1分，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：Transformer推理中的KV缓存压缩；场景：大语言模型推理加速与显存优化；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基于Transformer架构（Mistral-7B-v0.3分组查询注意力，LLaMA-2-13B多头注意力），核心方法为部分Tucker分解压缩KV缓存的token和feature轴，结合Johnson-Lindenstrauss旋转低比特残差恢复。

Lightweight method: 张量分解（部分Tucker分解）+ 低比特残差量化（JL旋转低比特残差），通过拉格朗日对偶分配Tucker秩和残差位宽。属于模型推理阶段的显存压缩方法，非模型权重压缩。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、能耗等部署指标。仅提及压缩比2-3x，以及FlashJoLT变体在压缩阶段加速5-13倍。不涉及星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 评估指标：困惑度(perplexity)、GSM8K准确率、RULER needle-in-a-haystack检索。数据集摘要未具体说明。相对Frobenius误差：K 0.009, V 0.006。

Experiments: 在Mistral-7B-v0.3和LLaMA-2-13B上验证，2-3x压缩下困惑度、GSM8K准确率、RULER检索均接近无损基线。与跨层SVD和4-bit量化对比，误差低约一个数量级。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对LLM的KV缓存压缩，不涉及遥感目标检测模型；2. 未讨论在遥感图像特征上的适用性；3. 未涉及模型权重压缩或推理加速；4. 未报告实际硬件部署延迟和能耗。

Ideas for my research: 1. 张量分解视角（将特征视为三阶张量）可借鉴到遥感检测模型的多头注意力特征压缩；2. Tucker分解+低比特残差的混合压缩策略可尝试用于遥感模型的特征图压缩；3. 拉格朗日对偶自动分配压缩率的思想可用于轻量化中的自动化压缩率搜索。但整体相关性弱，需大幅改造。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache has become the dominant memory cost of transformer inference. It grows with batch size, context length, and depth, and at long context it, rather than the model weights, sets the ceiling on throughput. Two families of methods reduce it. Low-rank methods factor two-dimensional slices of the cache, either per-head matrices or cross-layer feature blocks, and quantization methods lower the bit-width of every entry. Neither family exploits the fact that the cache at a layer is naturally a third-order tensor whose three axes, the heads, the tokens, and the features, carry very different amounts of redundancy. We take this tensor view directly. Our method, JoLT, applies a partial Tucker decomposition that compresses only the token and feature axes while leaving the head and layer axes intact, and then restores the energy that truncation discards with a Johnson-Lindenstrauss (JL) rotated low-bit residual. A single Lagrangian dual allocates the Tucker ranks and the residual bit-widths together, per layer group and separately for keys and values, under one byte budget. The result is a near-lossless 2-3x compression: perplexity, GSM8K accuracy, and RULER needle-in-a-haystack retrieval all stay at or within statistical noise of the uncompressed baseline on both a grouped-query-attention model (Mistral-7B-v0.3) and a multi-head-attention model (LLaMA-2-13B). At 2x, JoLT reconstructs the cache to relative Frobenius error 0.009 (K) and 0.006 (V) on both architectures, roughly an order of magnitude below cross-layer SVD and 4-bit quantization. A randomized-SVD variant, FlashJoLT, delivers a 5-13x compression-time speedup at matched quality.

</details>


### [213] [Lightweight Multi-Scale Anomaly Detection for Resource-Constrained Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12599)
*Raheen Junaid Wani,Smruti R. Sarangi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级多尺度自编码器(LMSAE)用于单变量时间序列异常检测，利用离散小波变换提取多尺度特征，在Jetson Nano上实现低延迟低功耗推理。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于IoT/传感器网络中的**时间序列异常检测**，而非遥感图像/卫星影像中的目标检测任务；虽然涉及轻量化和边缘部署，但应用场景、数据类型（一维时间序列 vs. 遥感图像）、任务目标（异常检测 vs. 目标检测）均与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向不匹配。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，仅边缘部署和轻量设计思路有微弱参考价值，不建议作为重点阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：单变量时间序列异常检测；应用场景：IoT系统、传感器网络、边缘监控；传感器类型：未明确说明（通用时间序列传感器）；目标类别：异常点/异常模式（非遥感目标）。

Model architecture: Lightweight MultiScale AutoEncoder (LMSAE)，基于自编码器架构，引入Discrete Wavelet Transform (DWT)提取多尺度特征，采用多尺度损失函数。

Lightweight method: 轻量网络设计（模型<500KB），未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等经典轻量化方法。通过DWT替代复杂多尺度模块来降低计算量。

Onboard deployability: 在NVIDIA Jetson Nano上测试：推理延迟降低9倍，功耗降低2倍，模型<500KB。但这是针对时间序列任务的边缘部署，非遥感卫星星上部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 基准时间序列异常检测数据集（摘要未说明具体名称）；评价指标：检测性能（未说明具体指标如F1/Precision/Recall）。

Experiments: 在基准数据集上取得有竞争力或更优的检测性能，参数量显著减少，模型<500KB。消融实验和详细对比摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对一维时间序列，无法直接迁移到遥感图像目标检测；2. 未涉及遥感场景中的小目标、旋转框、密集目标等核心问题；3. 轻量化仅体现在模型尺寸和边缘推理，未涉及剪枝/量化/蒸馏等通用轻量化技术；4. 未在星上硬件（如FPGA/NPU）上验证。

Ideas for my research: 1. DWT用于多尺度特征提取的思路可借鉴到遥感目标检测的轻量骨干网络设计中，替代计算量大的多尺度卷积模块；2. 多尺度损失函数的设计思想可用于提升遥感小目标检测的灵敏度；3. 在Jetson系列边缘设备上的部署验证流程可参考，但需替换为遥感目标检测任务和遥感数据集。

Abstract: Time-series anomaly detection is increasingly important in IoT systems, sensor networks, and edge monitoring applications, where models must operate under strict constraints on memory, latency, and power consumption. While recent deep-learning approaches have improved detection accuracy, many remain computationally expensive and often fail to capture subtle anomalies due to limited multi-scale sensitivity. Autoencoders are widely used for anomaly detection because they reconstruct normal patterns well, leading to elevated reconstruction errors for anomalous inputs. Their simplicity and efficiency also make them suitable lightweight backbones for handling multi-scale inputs. To address these challenges, we propose a Lightweight MultiScale AutoEncoder (LMSAE) network for univariate time-series anomaly detection, designed to be compact and computationally efficient. LMSAE leverages the Discrete Wavelet Transform (DWT) to extract multi-scale features and employs a multi-scale loss function to improve sensitivity to subtle or hidden anomalies. Experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate competitive or superior detection performance despite using significantly fewer parameters and a model size of less than 500 KB. LMSAE also achieves low-latency, low-power inference on the NVIDIA Jetson Nano, with 9x reduction in inference latency and 2x reduction in power consumption, making it ideal for edge deployment.

</details>

Abstract: Continuous-time generative frameworks construct probability paths between base and target domains by optimizing time-dependent velocity fields. While theoretical targets favor straight trajectories, empirical networks develop complex path deformations. This paper presents the Finite-Time Spectral Sensitivity (FTSS) g(t), a gradient-free, forward-pass metric that exposes flow geometry by tracking the root-mean-square singular value of the state-transition matrix. Serving as a continuous proxy for stable rank, g(t) reveals a distinct geometric pathology under data scarcity: while generalizing models maintain stable effective dimensions, overfitting causes a spectral collapse. We leverage this structural phenomenon to develop an internal geometric audit based on g(t). Our framework detects generative memorization using purely internal trajectory dynamics, removing the need for external membership queries or baseline data comparison.

</details>

Abstract: We propose an architecture for learning the dynamics of mechanical systems based on discrete forced Euler-Lagrange equations on Lie groups using only position data. By formulating the dynamics directly on manifold-valued configuration spaces, the method naturally respects the geometric structure of the systems and preserves geometric invariants and conservation laws. The reliance on position measurements alone makes the framework applicable in settings where velocity data are unavailable or noisy. The approach extends naturally to multibody systems, accommodates external control inputs, and demonstrates strong performance on both synthetic and real-world datasets.

</details>

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：MIMIC-III, MIMIC-IV（医疗EHR数据集）；评价指标：TailPairSeen, F1 score（用于零样本跨群体适应）。与遥感数据集无关。

Experiments: 在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV上，AdaPCLA相比HALO在TailPairSeen上分别提升114.2%和65.1%；零样本跨群体适应F1比GPT风格生成提升3.5%。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。摘要未讨论遥感场景下的任何问题。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。如果强行关联，模拟退火训练策略作为一种自适应训练方法，理论上可迁移至遥感模型训练中的类别不平衡问题（如小目标、罕见类别），但方向差异过大，不推荐深入。

Abstract: Generative modeling of longitudinal Electronic Health Records is increasingly important for privacy-preserving research, yet standard autoregressive models tend to underrepresent the co-occurrence structure of tail events (i.e., diseases, symptoms), reducing the fidelity and faithfulness of generated data for rare subpopulations. To this end, we propose AdaPCLA framework, which enables generative models to adaptively fit and generate EHR data through a data distribution-aware training strategy; this is achieved by internalizing data knowledge parameters by simulated annealing training. It also supports training-free adaptation to a diverse clinical population for generation through zero-shot distribution control. Moreover, our theoretical analysis characterizes rare-code logit updates through the label-wise empirical NTK and derives a prior-internalization bound for how annealing speed and NTK conditioning affect retained prior signals. Experiments on real-world data show that AdaPCLA achieves consistent gains in tail plausibility, downstream utility, and zero-shot control; in particular, it improves TailPairSeen over HALO by 114.2% on MIMIC-III and 65.1% on MIMIC-IV, outperforms GPT-style generation by 3.5% F1 for zero-shot cross-population adaptation.

</details>


### [217] [Extractable Memorization From First Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12649)
*A. Feder Cooper,Marika Swanberg,Jamie Hayes,Lea Duesterwald,Christopher De Sa,Daniel E. Ho,Mark A. Lemley,Percy Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文讨论大语言模型（LLM）中可提取记忆的验证方法，提出匹配比较框架来区分记忆与可预测性，与遥感、目标检测、轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。论文研究的是LLM的记忆提取验证问题，属于自然语言处理/机器学习基础理论方向，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化、在轨部署等均无任何关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及遥感场景、目标检测任务或任何视觉任务。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文讨论的是LLM（OLMo 2 32B, Llama 3.1 70B）的记忆提取问题，不涉及目标检测模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要提及Wikipedia和books作为实验数据，但这是LLM训练数据，非遥感数据集。评价指标为生成概率、FPR（假阳性率）等，与遥感目标检测的mAP、F1等指标无关。

Experiments: 论文在OLMo 2 32B和Llama 3.1 70B上进行了实验，验证了匹配比较框架的有效性，但实验内容与遥感目标检测无关。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向上的局限性分析。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。论文讨论的是LLM记忆提取的统计验证方法，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交叉或可迁移的技术思路。

Abstract: Recent work on extractable memorization in LLMs suffers from two contrasting validity problems. Some studies overstate extraction, e.g., relying on sequences too short to distinguish memorization from predictability. Others imply that extraction is unreliable evidence of memorization, since models can also reproduce real-world text they weren't explicitly trained on. In different ways, both overlook what makes a valid extraction claim: the model must generate a training sequence with high enough probability to indicate memorization. To determine what's high enough, one has to perform a matched comparison: measuring the generation probabilities of both the training sequences of interest and comparable non-training sequences. Because non-training sequences cannot have been memorized, their probabilities provide a baseline for predictability; a training sequence exceeding this baseline provides evidence of memorization. We formalize matched comparisons in two ways: (1) a conformal test that calibrates a threshold to a chosen FPR when training and non-training sequences are sampled from populations, and (2) a census that calibrates against a matched non-training document when the object is a single document (e.g., a book). We show that matched comparisons enable rigorous, calibrated memorization claims, and reveal where prior setups have validity issues. For instance, on Wikipedia OLMo 2 32B reproduces non-training 10-token suffixes roughly 24% as often as training ones: that share of the training generation rate reflects false positives, not memorization. For Llama 3.1 70B on books, the thresholds we calibrate are as low as 1e-27, supporting memorization claims for sequences that no feasible sampling budget would extract. Based on these results, we refine "extractable memorization" to require a valid memorization claim and near-certain generation within a realistic budget.

</details>


### [218] [Evidence-Grounded Verified Agentic Reasoning: A Path Toward Eliminating LLM Hallucination in Empirical Inference via Tool-Attested Kernel Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12650)
*Junyu Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Lean 4的形式化验证工具调用架构EG-VAR，用于确保LLM推理输出的可溯源性和形式化正确性，属于AI安全与形式化验证领域。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文讨论的是LLM推理的形式化验证与可信性问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一个核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。不涉及遥感场景、目标检测任务或任何传感器类型。

Model architecture: EG-VAR架构，基于Lean 4定理证明器的工具调用架构，包含tool-attestation axioms和declared source lifts机制。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。不涉及任何模型轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及星上/边缘部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: TableBench数值推理子集(n=120)；反事实压力测试(5 domains x 2 models)；评估指标为准确率(120/120)、source-faithful比例(100%)、语义形式化错误率(3.3% Sonnet, 1.7% Opus)。

Experiments: 在TableBench上EG-VAR达到120/120，对比同工具基线95%；反事实测试中EG-VAR保持100% source-faithful，同工具基线降至80-90%，无工具基线50-80%。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术点或可迁移方法。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于AI形式化验证与可信推理方向，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化无任何交集。

Abstract: Tool access alone does not make LLM empirical reasoning governable: accepted outputs need not descend from attested evidence, and accepted deductions need not hold up under formal scrutiny. We present EG-VAR (Evidence-Grounded Verified Agentic Reasoning), a Lean 4-based tool-calling architecture in which the Lean kernel is the sole minter of Verified claims via tool-attestation axioms and declared source lifts. Every verified output structurally descends from an attested tool call (Thm. 3.1) and a kernel-checked chain of valid inference (Thm. 3.2); residual outputs are honest Abstain with a replayable audit trail. On a subcollection of TableBench numerical reasoning (n=120), EG-VAR attains 120/120 versus a 95% same-tool baseline; on counterfactual stress tests (5 domains x 2 models), EG-VAR stays 100% source-faithful while same-tool drops to 80-90% (no-tool 50-80%). With the LLM as deployment-time formalizer, residual semantic-formalization error is 3.3% on Sonnet and 1.7% on Opus. We position EG-VAR as a technical-governance interface for high-stakes empirical claims: a formal sidecar makes the target proposition, source scope, evidence boundary, proof obligation, and abstention condition auditable, eliminating unsupported Verified outputs today while turning formalization errors, lift and source-authority disputes, ambiguities, and abstentions into explicit audit targets. Over time, typed sidecars in datasets, APIs, public records, and AI-generated documents can amortize this formalization burden into reusable infrastructure.

</details>


### [219] [Learning-based Probabilistic Load Forecasting with Post-hoc and In-model Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12730)
*Sarah Al-Shareeda,Gulcihan Ozdemir,Heung Seok Jeon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究智能建筑负荷预测中，当推理输入依赖特征重构时，不确定性应放在模型哪个环节（模块化后验分位数 vs. 模型内部分位数学习），并比较了三种深度学习骨干网络。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是智能建筑负荷预测（smart-building load forecasting），属于建筑能源/时间序列预测领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等任何遥感相关场景，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：一日前概率负荷预测（one-day-ahead probabilistic load forecasting）；场景：智能建筑能源管理；传感器类型：建筑传感器（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：无目标检测任务。

Model architecture: 三种DL骨干：循环网络（recurrent）、混合循环网络（hybrid recurrent）、注意力机制Temporal Fusion Transformer (TFT)。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：未公开命名，使用带标签的测试窗口；评价指标：MAPE（2.2-3.6%）、RMSE（28-83W）、Quantile Score (QS)、区间宽度、Diebold-Mariano检验。

Experiments: 对比模块化后验残差分位数与模型内部分位数学习；TFT上集成分位数学习最优；重构输入使QS增加106%但区间宽度几乎不变；与非DL基线及季节性留出周进行鲁棒性检验。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。摘要本身局限：仅针对建筑负荷预测场景，未讨论模型效率或部署约束。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。唯一可能的间接启发是：如果遥感在轨推理中也涉及缺失特征重构（如云遮挡导致波段缺失），本文关于不确定性放置位置的分析思路可能有参考价值，但非常间接。

Abstract: Smart-building load forecasters are often trained offline on dense, multivariate, high-frequency data, but deployment may provide only hourly, feature-limited inputs. Missing features must then be reconstructed, and their errors can propagate through the model. If this input uncertainty is not reflected, prediction intervals may become miscalibrated, affecting demand-response scheduling. Our work examines where uncertainty should be placed once inference inputs are reconstructed. We develop a unified one-day-ahead probabilistic forecasting framework that aligns temporal resolution, reconstructs the unavailable inputs, and derives causal features, and we compare a modular post-hoc residual-quantile scheme with an integrated in-model quantile-learning scheme. The comparison uses three mid-scale Deep Learning (DL) backbones: recurrent, hybrid recurrent, and attention-based Temporal Fusion Transformer (TFT) models, under identical inputs, forecasting horizon, preprocessing rules, and training budgets. Results show that uncertainty placement is backbone-dependent. Integrated quantile learning is most reliable with the TFT, yielding 2.2-3.6% MAPE and 28-83W RMSE on the labeled test window, while producing intervals about 5x narrower than the modular intervals at the closest-to-nominal coverage level. Diebold-Mariano tests support the TFT ranking and the mixed behavior of the recurrent backbones. A reconstruction-sensitivity test shows that reconstructed inputs increase the Quantile Score (QS) by 106% while interval width remains nearly unchanged, indicating that the model does not automatically absorb reconstruction-induced uncertainty. Robustness checks against non-DL baselines and seasonal hold-out weeks support this ranking. Our results expose the limits of post-hoc residual quantiles when inference depends on reconstructed inputs.

</details>


### [220] [What Makes a Representational Prior Work? Feature Families, Label-Free Invariances, and Critical Windows in Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12735)
*Gunner Levi Howe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究神经网络"grokking延迟"的因果机制，发现对比先验（contrastive prior）可通过注入任务结构化表示来加速泛化，并系统分析了先验的内容、监督方式、时机和设置四个维度的影响。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等任何遥感相关场景；不涉及目标检测任务；不涉及模型轻量化、剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署等任何部署相关技术。论文属于深度学习的理论机制研究（grokking现象），与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。除非对grokking理论机制本身有额外兴趣，否则对遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题帮助极小。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：神经网络泛化机制的理论研究（grokking delay分析）。应用场景：合成任务（modular multiplication）。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。涉及深度网络、归一化变体（normalization variants），但未给出具体网络名称。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文讨论的是对比先验（contrastive prior）对grokking延迟的影响，与模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）无关。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：合成任务（modular multiplication），非遥感数据集。评价指标：grokking成功率、中位加速比（median speedup）、p值统计显著性。实验设置：188次新实验。

Experiments: 主要结果：1) 来自错误特征族（magnitude bands）的先验会阻碍泛化（1/15 vs 0/20 grok, p=0.43）；2) 无标签不变性先验（仅使用交换对）在15/15次运行中泛化，中位加速2.7倍；3) 结合权重范数钳制（weight-norm clamp）得到最强方法（中位17倍，5/5）；4) 先验仅在早期（前2000 epoch，占预算4%）应用即可在10/10次运行中达到2.7倍加速，优于持续应用（8/10, 1.25倍）。

Limitations: 1) 完全不涉及遥感场景和目标检测任务；2) 仅在合成任务（modular multiplication）上验证，未在真实视觉任务上测试；3) 不涉及任何模型压缩或加速技术；4) 不涉及任何部署约束（算力、内存、功耗等）。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但以下思路可间接参考：1) "先验仅在训练早期有效"的发现提示在遥感模型轻量化训练中，知识蒸馏或结构化先验可能也只需在训练早期施加即可获得大部分收益，从而减少训练开销；2) 对比先验（contrastive prior）的思想可迁移到遥感目标检测的特征表示学习中，帮助模型更快形成任务相关表示。但整体而言，该论文对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接可借鉴点很少。

Abstract: Companion work showed the grokking delay is causally the time to form task-structured representations, injectable via a contrastive prior. Here we characterize what makes such a prior work, across four axes, in 188 new runs. Content: a coherent, learnable prior built from the wrong feature family (magnitude bands) blocks generalization like a random partition (1/15 vs 0/20 grok; $p=0.43$ between them), confirming the companion's prediction that priors act at the level of the circuit's features. Supervision: a fully label-free invariance prior -- positives are commuted pairs $(a,b)\sim(b,a)$ only -- generalizes in 15/15 runs at a median $2.7\times$ speedup, more reliably than the label-supervised prior itself ($p=0.038$), and combined with a weight-norm clamp yields the strongest method we test (median $17\times$, 5/5) -- strongest meaning reliably fast: plain cross-entropy with a clamp matches this speed only at the exact critical norm, while the prior keeps it fast across the entire clamp range. Timing: the prior is only needed early -- applied solely during the first 2000 epochs (4% of budget) it generalizes 10/10 at $2.7\times$, beating continuous application (8/10, $1.25\times$) and a duration-matched later window ($2.1\times$). Setting: the dissociation replicates on modular multiplication and across depths and normalization variants, and a clamp sweep quantifies the companion's central claim: structure injection flattens the weight-norm delay-law exponent about 17-fold (plain cross-entropy slows $31\times$ per +10 norm units, a lower bound as higher cells are censored, versus $1.22\times$ with the prior). Honest boundary: tasks that generalize before memorizing have no delay to control. Feature-family alignment decides whether a prior permits generalization; invariance content suffices for acceleration without labels; a brief early window captures nearly all of the benefit.

</details>


### [221] [Constraint-Aware Aggregation for Federated Reinforcement Learning in Microgrid Energy Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12763)
*Usman Haider,Karl Mason*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于惩罚项的约束感知聚合规则，用于联邦强化学习中的分布式能源协调，在不修改本地训练的情况下提升安全性与奖励的权衡。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文研究的是联邦强化学习在分布式能源协调中的应用，涉及的是电力系统、电池储能、电网容量约束等场景，与遥感成像、卫星观测、目标检测、模型压缩/加速等核心主题无任何关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：联邦强化学习（Federated Reinforcement Learning, FedRL）中的约束感知聚合；应用场景：分布式能源协调（电池储能调度）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体的强化学习网络架构（如Q网络、策略网络等）。核心贡献在服务器端聚合规则的设计，而非模型结构。

Lightweight method: 文中提到"lightweight aggregation strategies"，这里的"轻量"指的是聚合规则计算简单（仅需奖励和约束违反估计的线性组合），不需要对偶优化或修改本地训练。但这与模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）是完全不同的概念，不涉及模型压缩或加速。

Onboard deployability: 不适用。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署，不报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：DairyGridEnv（合成基准）、芬兰真实负荷数据、德国FIELD数据集；评价指标：奖励（Reward）、约束违反（Constraint Violation）。

Experiments: 在DairyGridEnv及真实负荷数据上，基于惩罚的聚合规则相比FedAvg在减少约束违反的同时提升了奖励。联合奖励-违反方案通过λ参数可调节权衡，但稳定性较差。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的模型结构、轻量化方法或部署信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于分布式能源领域的联邦强化学习研究，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无交叉。

Abstract: Federated Reinforcement Learning (FedRL) enables coordination of distributed energy resources without sharing raw local data, but standard aggregation methods such as FedAvg do not account for system-level constraints, often leading to unsafe global behavior. In this work, we study constraint-aware aggregation for federated reinforcement learning in distributed energy coordination. We propose aggregation rules that incorporate both local performance and estimated constraint violation into the server-side update. Among these, a simple penalty-based rule, $w_i \propto R_i - αV_i$, consistently provides the most reliable trade-off between reward and safety, without requiring dual optimization or modifications to local training. \textcolor{black}{We evaluate our approach on DairyGridEnv, a benchmark modeling multiple farms coordinating battery storage under stochastic demand and a shared grid capacity constraint, and further assess robustness using real load-driven demand profiles from Finland and the German FIELD dataset. Across multiple seeds, penalty-based aggregation substantially reduces violations while improving reward relative to FedAvg in both synthetic and real load-driven settings.} A combined reward-violation scheme exposes a tunable trade-off via $λ$, but is less stable. These results demonstrate that lightweight aggregation strategies can substantially improve empirical safety in federated reinforcement learning while preserving standard communication protocols.

</details>


### [222] [Learning Mechanistic Reasoning for Chemical Reactions with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12771)
*Xingyu Dang,Haocheng Tang,Junmei Wang,Yanjun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文构建了化学反应机理推理数据集和FukuyamaBench基准，通过微调Qwen3-30B-A3B大语言模型提升化学推理能力，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。论文主题为有机化学反应机理推理与LLM化学智能增强，属于计算化学/化学信息学领域。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等任何相关技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：化学反应机理推理（reaction mechanism inference）；应用场景：有机化学、药物发现；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: Qwen3-30B-A3B（基于Qwen3架构的大语言模型，30B总参数量，3B激活参数），以及FlowER（专用小规模生成模型）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。虽然模型采用了MoE架构（30B总参/3B激活参数），但论文目的不是轻量化，而是提升化学推理能力。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。30B总参量的模型显然不适合星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：自建大规模反应机理推理数据集（large-scale reasoning dataset of reaction mechanisms）；基准：FukuyamaBench（基于Fukuyama's Advanced Organic Reaction Mechanism book构建）；评价指标：exact pathway match（精确路径匹配率）。

Experiments: 在FukuyamaBench Set A上，Qwen3-30B-A3B达到8.3% exact pathway match，超过FlowER的5.1%。结论：机理感知训练显著增强LLM的化学推理能力。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感/检测/轻量化内容。论文本身局限：FukuyamaBench准确率仍很低（8.3%），模型泛化能力有限。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无直接可借鉴点。唯一可能间接相关的思路是：论文使用MoE架构（30B总参/3B激活）实现了"大模型但激活参数少"的设计理念，这种稀疏激活思想在遥感轻量化中可类比为条件计算（conditional computation），但论文本身未涉及遥感或检测任务，借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: Reaction mechanisms consist of the step-by-step sequences of elementary reactions that explain chemical transformations. Learning the mechanism logic is therefore essential for enhancing the fundamental chemical intelligence of large language models (LLMs). The stepwise deduction of reaction mechanism aligns naturally with the reasoning paradigms of reasoning LLMs. However, current chemical LLMs primarily emphasize coarse-grained name reactions for product prediction and retrosynthesis, often leading to physical inconsistencies and hallucinations. In contrast, specialized small-scale generative models for mechanism inference typically suffer from restricted generalization capacity across diverse chemical spaces. To overcome these limitations, we built a novel, large-scale reasoning dataset of reaction mechanisms. Furthermore, we established the FukuyamaBench, a difficult benchmark derived from Fukuyama's Advanced Organic Reaction Mechanism book, to rigorously evaluate model performance on hierarchical mechanism reasoning. Our fine-tuned Qwen3-30B-A3B achieves 8.3% exact pathway match on FukuyamaBench Set~A, surpassing the specialized FlowER model (5.1%), demonstrating that mechanism-aware training substantially enhances chemical reasoning in language models.

</details>


### [223] [AVQ-Attention: Adaptive Vector-Quantized Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12789)
*Winfried van den dool,Patrick Forré,Amir Habibian,Yuki M. Asano,Max Welling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应向量量化注意力机制(AVQ Attention)，通过根据注意力重要性动态分配码本容量，在保持O(MN)复杂度下提升Transformer注意力机制的精度-效率权衡。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于通用Transformer注意力机制的量化加速方法，不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测或星上部署等任何遥感相关场景。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，属于通用Transformer注意力机制的效率优化工作，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文未指定任何应用场景、传感器类型或目标类别，属于通用视觉/语言Transformer的注意力机制改进。

Model architecture: 基于Vector-Quantized (VQ) Attention的改进，提出Adaptive Vector-Quantized (AVQ) Attention。核心创新：从少量码字开始，在前向传播中识别重要码字并用预训练的子码字进行细化，实现注意力重要区域的细粒度量化和非重要区域的粗粒度量化。实现基于自定义Triton kernel，兼容Flash Attention的tiled计算范式。

Lightweight method: 涉及向量量化(Vector Quantization)方法，属于模型量化范畴。将注意力中key的表示从N个token压缩为M个码字(M < N)，复杂度从O(N²)降至O(MN)。进一步提出自适应码本分配策略，在重要区域分配更多码字容量。但这是针对注意力计算本身的量化，而非模型权重量化或剪枝等传统轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等具体部署指标。仅提到保持了O(MN)复杂度，相比固定码本的VQ attention有更好的精度-效率权衡。未讨论任何边缘/星上部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明使用的数据集和评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体实验结果、对比方法和消融实验。仅声称相比固定码本VQ attention实现了更好的accuracy-efficiency trade-offs。

Limitations: 1. 摘要未提供任何定量实验结果，缺乏说服力；2. 未讨论该方法在遥感等特定领域任务上的适用性；3. 自适应码本分配带来的额外计算开销（重要性评分、子码字插入等）在摘要中仅称"minimal overhead"，但缺乏量化分析；4. 依赖预训练的子码字，增加了训练复杂度。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但以下思路可间接借鉴：1. 注意力机制中的向量量化思想可用于遥感大模型（如ViT-based检测器）的推理加速；2. 自适应码本分配策略可启发在遥感目标检测中根据目标区域重要性动态分配计算资源；3. Triton kernel + Flash Attention的tiled实现方式对星上NPU/FPGA部署的算子优化有参考价值。但整体而言，该论文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化的直接关联度很低。

Abstract: The $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ complexity of attention over $N$ tokens remains a computational bottleneck in transformer models. Vector-Quantized (VQ) attention reduces this to $\mathcal{O}(MN)$ by representing keys with $M$ codewords, but applies uniform codebook capacity regardless of where attention mass concentrates: high-attention regions of key space may be coarsely approximated while low-attention regions waste representational capacity. We propose Adaptive Vector-Quantized (AVQ) Attention, which adaptively allocates codebook capacity based on attention importance. Starting from a small set of codewords, our method identifies the most important codes during the forward pass and refines them with pre-learned child codewords, achieving fine-grained quantization where it matters most while maintaining coarse quantization elsewhere. We develop an implementation using custom Triton kernels that enables the full adaptive refinement process, including importance scoring, child codeword insertion, and parent contribution replacement, to be carried out within the tiled computation paradigm of Flash Attention with minimal overhead. Our approach maintains $\mathcal{O}(MN)$ complexity while achieving improved accuracy-efficiency trade-offs compared to fixed-codebook VQ-attention.

</details>


### [224] [Accelerating Masked Diffusion Large Language Models: A Survey of Efficient Inference Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12829)
*Daehoon Gwak,Minhyung Lee,Junwoo Park,Jaegul Choo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 一篇关于扩散大语言模型(dLLMs)推理加速技术的综述，提出了延迟分解框架，从算法、架构和系统三个维度分类加速方法。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文讨论的是扩散大语言模型(dLLMs)的推理加速，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化模型压缩等核心主题。虽然涉及"部署"和"加速"，但场景和对象完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非对扩散模型的推理加速框架设计思路有跨领域借鉴需求，否则优先级很低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：扩散大语言模型推理加速综述；应用场景：大语言模型部署；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 扩散大语言模型(dLLMs)，区别于标准自回归模型，支持并行生成。

Lightweight method: 论文讨论的是推理加速而非模型轻量化。提及的方法包括：diffusion-aware caching and reuse（扩散感知缓存与重用）、算法级加速、架构级优化、系统级优化、推理时缩放(inference-time scaling)。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等经典轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等具体部署指标。仅提及"端到端延迟(end-to-end latency)"作为分析对象，但未给出具体数值。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明使用的数据集和评价指标。作为综述论文，可能涉及多个基准测试，但摘要未具体列出。

Experiments: 作为综述论文，摘要未展示具体实验结果、对比方法或消融实验。

Limitations: 摘要指出当前存在的主要问题：端到端延迟源于算法、架构和系统层面因素之间复杂的权衡，这些因素在现有基准测试中常常被混淆，导致严格比较困难。此外，并行生成本身并不能保证实际加速，需要专门的推理机制。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但以下思路可间接参考：(1) 论文提出的"延迟分解框架"思路——在遥感在轨目标检测中，也可将推理延迟分解为模型计算延迟、数据传输延迟、NPU/FPGA调度延迟等，分别优化；(2) 论文强调"算法-架构-系统"三轴协同优化，类似地，在轨轻量化也需考虑模型压缩算法、星上硬件架构和系统调度三者的联合优化；(3) caching and reuse思想在遥感视频流或连续帧目标检测中可能有借鉴意义。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) offer a theoretical advantage in parallel generation over standard autoregressive models. However, parallel generation alone does not guarantee practical speedups. Realizing this efficiency requires specialized inference mechanisms, such as diffusion-aware caching and reuse. Consequently, as inference efficiency becomes a prerequisite for practical deployment, recent research has actively explored acceleration techniques across algorithms, architectures, and systems. However, rigorous comparisons remain difficult, as end-to-end latency stems from intricate trade-offs between algorithmic, architectural, and system-level factors that are often conflated in existing benchmarks. In this survey, we introduce a unified latency decomposition framework for dLLMs to disentangle these factors and analyze their impact on inference speed in real deployments. Guided by this framework, we categorize acceleration techniques along three axes covering algorithmic innovations, architectural and system optimizations, and inference-time scaling. Finally, we provide guidelines for reproducible benchmarking and highlight open challenges for realizing the full potential of parallel generation.

</details>


### [225] [Verifier-Based Reinforcement Fine-Tuning of Reasoning Models for Thermal Energy Storage Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12856)
*Takumi Shioda,Kohei Terashima,Tatsuo Nagai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）微调开源推理模型，实现建筑热储能系统的调度优化，使碳排放接近动态规划最优解。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦建筑热储能（TES）调度控制，属于建筑能源管理领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化模型部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：建筑热储能调度（upper-level scheduler输出hourly heat-pump setpoints）；应用场景：建筑能源管理；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于开源推理模型（open-weight reasoning model），通过RLVR（Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards）进行reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT)。具体模型名称摘要未说明（仅提及GPT-5和GPT-4o作为对比）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。本文未使用剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等任何模型轻量化或压缩方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。本文讨论建筑级控制器调度，与星上/边缘部署无关。未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：一个简化的办公建筑TES基准测试（deliberately simple office-building TES benchmark），其中精确DP可解且最优解已知。评价指标：碳排放量（kg-CO2），对比DP最优解（60.8 kg-CO2）。

Experiments: RFT将开源模型的碳排放从70.5降至61.2 kg-CO2，接近DP最优的60.8 kg-CO2。GPT-5几乎匹配DP和MPC（无需任务特定训练），GPT-4o（非推理LLM）产生比无储能基线更高的排放。鲁棒性测试表明强化后的规划模式在预测误差和未见过的TES条件下保持，并迁移到电池任务。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 实验仅在简化的单建筑基准上进行，未涉及真实建筑或城市级规模；3. 未讨论模型推理效率、参数量、计算资源需求等部署关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但以下思路可间接参考：1）RLVR（基于可验证奖励的强化学习）方法可作为一种将领域知识（如DP最优解）转化为奖励信号来微调模型的范式，若未来在遥感领域有类似"已知最优解"的轻量化搜索问题可借鉴；2）推理模型（reasoning model）的推理时计算（inference-time reasoning）对任务性能的影响值得关注，但与本课题的轻量化目标（减少计算量）方向相反。

Abstract: Buildings are expected to shift cooling loads in response to grid conditions. Thermal energy storage (TES) enables this shift, but scheduling it well requires planning hours ahead under storage constraints. Model predictive control (MPC) and reinforcement learning are difficult to scale across buildings. This study instead adapts an open-weight reasoning model through reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). We convert exact offline dynamic-programming (DP) action values into dense rewards for every candidate action. Using only 30 training prompts, reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT) trains the model as an upper-level scheduler that outputs hourly heat-pump setpoints from text-based states and forecasts. Evaluation uses a deliberately simple office-building TES benchmark where exact DP is tractable and the optimum is known. RFT reduces the open-weight model's emissions from 70.5 to 61.2 kg-CO2, close to the DP optimum of 60.8 kg-CO2. GPT-5 nearly matches DP and MPC without task-specific training, while GPT-4o, a non-reasoning LLM, produces higher emissions than the no-storage baseline, so inference-time reasoning appears important. Trace analysis shows that RFT mainly stabilizes observable planning patterns (candidate comparison, look-ahead, and feasibility checking) rather than creating a new strategy. Robustness and generalization tests clarify what transfers: the reinforced planning patterns persist under forecast errors and an unseen TES condition and carry over to a battery task, but its different structure limits the gains. DP-based verifiable rewards offer a practical way to adapt open-weight reasoning models to building storage scheduling. These results motivate higher-fidelity tests of whole-building control and scalable verifiers for city-scale energy management.

</details>


### [226] [Energy-Based Physics-Informed Form Finding for Clustered Tensegrity Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12888)
*Jing Qin,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于能量的学习框架，用于张拉整体结构找形与物理属性预测，通过将总势能最小化和本构关系嵌入训练目标，同时预测平衡节点构型与内力分布。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是结构力学中的张拉整体结构找形问题，属于固体力学/结构工程领域，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化均无任何关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：张拉整体结构找形与物理属性预测（tensegrity form-finding and physical property prediction）；场景：结构力学/土木工程；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基于能量的学习框架（energy-based learning framework），将总势能最小化和本构关系作为训练目标的一部分。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明任何与星上/边缘部署相关的信息（参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等均未提及）。

Datasets and metrics: 数值实验采用张拉整体结构（prism and lander systems），未使用任何遥感或计算机视觉标准数据集。

Experiments: 在prism和lander系统上进行了数值实验，展示了方法的可扩展性和预测准确性，但未与遥感目标检测方法进行对比。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的检测或轻量化技术信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于结构力学领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型压缩或边缘部署中的任何关键技术。

Abstract: Tensegrity form-finding and physical property prediction are fundamental inverse problems in structural mechanics, which aim to determine equilibrium configurations and internal force distributions. These problems are challenging due to strong nonlinearity arising from the coupling between geometry and forces, the need to ensure structural stability, and the enforcement of constraints such as boundary conditions and symmetry. Moreover, traditional methods often lack robustness to noise and outliers. This paper proposes an energy-based learning framework for clustered tensegrity form finding and physical property prediction. The proposed approach incorporates total potential energy minimization and constitutive relations into the training objective, enabling the simultaneous prediction of equilibrium nodal configurations and associated physical quantities, including member forces and force densities. By incorporating energy-based physical losses directly into the learning process, the framework improves physical consistency, robustness, and data efficiency. Numerical experiments on tensegrity structures, including prism and lander systems, show the great potential of the proposed approach and demonstrate its capability for scalable form finding and accurate prediction of structural properties.

</details>


### [227] [Contrastive-Collapsed Loss for Flexible and Geometrically Optimal Embeddings and Faster Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12916)
*Blanca Cano-Camarero,Ángela Fernández-Pascual,José R. Dorronsoro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种名为CoCo的损失函数，通过鼓励类内坍缩和类间对比来学习归一化且结构良好的表征，在OpenML-CC18表格数据集上取得有竞争力的分类性能。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于通用分类任务的损失函数设计，完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测或模型轻量化。实验仅在OpenML-CC18表格数据集上进行，没有任何遥感/卫星/航拍图像相关实验。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，不建议阅读全文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：通用分类任务（tabular classification）；应用场景：非遥感场景；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法。无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等内容。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：OpenML-CC18 benchmark（表格数据集）；评价指标：分类性能（competitive performance），具体指标摘要未详细说明。

Experiments: 与kernel SVM、Random Forest、dot regression、cross-entropy-based neural networks等方法对比，CoCo损失函数取得了有竞争力的性能，并表现出更紧致的类聚类和更快的收敛速度。

Limitations: 1. 仅在表格数据上验证，未在图像/遥感图像上测试；2. 未讨论损失函数对模型大小、推理速度的影响；3. 未涉及任何遥感或目标检测场景。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。CoCo损失函数中"类内坍缩+类间对比"的思想理论上可迁移到遥感目标检测的特征学习，但摘要未提供任何与遥感/轻量化相关的信息，且实验场景差异巨大。不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce CoCo, a loss function aimed at learning normalized and well-structured representations. The proposed loss encourages intra-class collapse and inter-class contrast while preserving sufficient flexibility for neural networks to approximate geometrically optimal embeddings with large angular separation between classes. We provide a theoretical analysis positioning CoCo with respect to related objectives such as dot regression and cross-entropy, showing that the new proposed loss benefits from closer initialization to the optimal configuration, more informative gradients, and stronger incentives for class-wise representation collapse. Extensive experiments on diverse tabular datasets from the OpenML-CC18 benchmark show that CoCo achieves competitive performance with state-of-the-art methods, including kernel SVM, Random Forest, dot regression, and cross-entropy-based neural networks. In addition, both theoretical arguments and empirical analyses demonstrate that the proposal promotes tighter class clustering and faster convergence. These results highlight CoCo loss as an effective objective for learning discriminative representations while maintaining competitive predictive performance.

</details>


### [228] [Efficient Sequential Calibration with $O(T^{2/3-ε})$ Error Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12928)
*Zihan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线二元序列校准问题，提出一种随机预测器，将SPR-Calibration过程与Blackwell校正层结合，实现了比经典T^{2/3}界更优的期望校准误差O(T^{2/3-ε})。

Research relevance: 1分：与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是在线学习中的序列校准（sequential calibration）理论问题，属于机器学习理论/在线学习范畴，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文任务为在线二元序列校准（online binary sequential calibration），属于理论机器学习问题，不涉及遥感场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: SPR-Calibration过程 + Blackwell校正层（outer Blackwell-style correction layer）。但这是理论预测器框架，非深度学习模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文为纯理论工作，未提及任何实验数据集。评价指标为期望校准误差（expected calibration error），但这是在线学习理论中的误差界，非目标检测评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。论文未提及实验对比或消融实验。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向上的局限性分析。论文本身是纯理论工作，未讨论实际部署问题。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于在线学习理论，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无交叉。不建议投入时间阅读。

Abstract: We study the online binary sequential calibration problem. A recent breakthrough by \citet{dagan2024breaking} overcomes the classical \(T^{2/3}\) barrier for calibration error. Building on this result, we present an efficient randomized forecaster that achieves an expected calibration error \(O(T^{2/3-\varepsilon})\) for some constant \(\varepsilon>0\).
  Our forecaster combines the \textsc{SPR-Calibration} procedure \citep{dagan2024breaking} with an outer Blackwell-style correction layer. The \textsc{SPR-Calibration} procedure controls calibration with respect to a surrogate sequence of conditional-mean estimates, while the correction layer controls the additional error incurred when these surrogates are used to approximate the true outcomes. The analysis decomposes the total calibration error into the surrogate calibration error and the residual discrepancy between the surrogate sequence and the true outcomes. The former is bounded by the \textsc{SPR-Calibration} guarantee in \citet{dagan2024breaking}, and the latter is controlled using a quadratic potential argument together with the sparsity of the \textsc{SPR-Calibration} forecaster.

</details>


### [229] [The Spectrum Is Not Enough: When Context Helps Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13006)
*Mert Onur Cakiroglu,Mehmet Dalkilic,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出频谱预测指标不能等价于上下文/检索/预训练模型的价值，并给出一种基于覆盖亏缺的诊断方法，用于区分频谱内与频谱外的预测增益。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化完全无关。论文聚焦于时间序列预测中的频谱分析与上下文价值诊断，属于时间序列分析/信号处理领域，不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测或模型压缩部署内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：时间序列预测的可预测性诊断；应用场景：通用时间序列预测（ECL等基准）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。论文不提出新预测器，而是提出诊断框架（coverage deficit），涉及线性预测、模拟预测、窗口键检索、基础模型等。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：7个时间序列基准（含ECL等）；评价指标：预测增益百分比（如ECL中位数+33%→-35%）、p值、覆盖亏缺（coverage deficit）等；实验设置：留一数据集交叉验证、替代对（surrogate pairs）控制实验。

Experiments: 主要结果：窗口键检索的价值在替代对上崩溃（ECL中位数+33%→-35%，p<10^{-40}），而频谱指标保持不变；基础模型的价值可分解为幸存二阶部分和崩溃的超线性部分；更长线性窗口的价值幸存。结构项（覆盖亏缺）能预测超频谱价值的符号。

Limitations: 论文本身是时间序列预测领域的理论性/诊断性工作，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无直接可借鉴的技术方法。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文讨论的是时间序列预测中频谱指标与上下文价值的关系，与遥感图像目标检测及模型轻量化无任何交集。

Abstract: A growing family of indices scores how predictable a series is from its spectrum. Practitioners increasingly read these scores as answering a different question: whether \emph{adding context}, a longer lookback, a retrieval plug-in, or a pretrained model, will help. These are not the same question. The value of context is a property of the operating point, not of the series. Any index built from the power spectrum is invariant under phase randomization, whereas the beyond-second-order value that retrieval and foundation models supply is not, because a phase-randomized series is asymptotically Gaussian. We state this as an impossibility result and isolate it with surrogate pairs that fix the spectrum and the marginal by construction. We then give a label-free, configuration-level diagnostic, the coverage deficit, whose principal term measures beyond-spectrum structure as the gain of analog over linear prediction. On seven benchmarks the prediction holds: window-keyed retrieval's value collapses across surrogate pairs (ECL median $+33\%\!\to\!-35\%$, $p{<}10^{-40}$) while every spectral index stays frozen; a foundation model's value splits into a surviving second-order part and a small beyond-linear margin that collapses; a longer linear window's value survives. Leave-one-dataset-out, the structure term predicts the sign of beyond-spectrum value where the spectral indices trail it, and the reverse holds for the second-order mechanism. We introduce no new forecaster; the contribution is the distinction, a controlled comparison, and a diagnostic for the deployment decision. Code: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SINE.

</details>


### [230] [The Seriality Gap in Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13031)
*Jorge Diaz Chao,Konpat Preechakul,Yuxi Liu,Yutong Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现标准双向视频扩散模型在多球硬球动力学因果链预测中存在"串行性差距"——去噪步数无法提供可扩展的串行计算，并提出自回归/分块生成和增加网络深度可改善性能。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是视频扩散模型在物理动力学因果推理中的串行计算能力问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文研究视频扩散模型的物理因果推理能力，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：视频预测/物理动力学因果推理；应用场景：多球硬球碰撞动力学；传感器类型：合成视频数据（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：球体碰撞事件。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: 标准双向视频扩散模型（bidirectional video diffusion），自回归/分块生成（autoregressive/blockwise generation），增加网络深度（architectural depth）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：多球硬球动力学合成数据（multi-ball hard-sphere dynamics），含长度匹配的单球控制实验；评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标名称。

Experiments: 主要发现：标准双向视频扩散在因果链变长时性能下降；单球控制实验（无球-球交互）中性能下降消失，说明是依赖事件结构而非视频长度导致；增加串行计算（自回归生成、网络深度）可改善性能。

Limitations: 1. 完全基于合成物理数据，未在真实遥感图像/视频上验证；2. 未涉及目标检测任务；3. 未涉及任何模型轻量化或部署优化；4. 与遥感在轨智能场景无任何关联。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无直接可借鉴点。唯一可能的间接启发是：如果未来研究遥感视频中的时序目标检测（如卫星视频中的运动目标跟踪），可关注串行计算与并行去噪之间的权衡，但与本课题核心方向（轻量化检测模型）关联极弱。

Abstract: When one ball strikes another, then another, video models should predict the consequences of each bounce. In controlled experiments on multi-ball hard-sphere dynamics, we find that the performance of standard bidirectional video diffusion degrades as the causal chain lengthens, even when provided more denoising steps. In a length-matched single-ball control, where ball-ball interactions are absent, the degradation largely disappears, isolating dependent-event structure rather than video length as the cause. Across intervention studies, methods that increase effective serial computation improve performance disproportionately, including autoregressive/blockwise generation and architectural depth. We identify this pattern as the seriality gap: a mismatch between tasks requiring growing serial computation and video diffusion models whose denoising loop does not provide scalable serial compute. We then prove that, for deterministic video prediction, denoising steps do not add serial computation beyond the backbone, indicating a structural obstacle for video diffusion on serial reasoning and simulation tasks.

</details>

### [231] [Did We Actually Fix It? An Independent Adversarial Stress-Test of Post-Point-Adjustment Evaluation Metrics for Time-Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11969)
*Zongye Lyu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文对时间序列异常检测中的多种评分协议（metrics）进行了对抗性鲁棒性审计，发现ROC-based指标和affiliation-F1仍可被无技能检测器轻易攻破，而PR-based指标和PA%K更具鲁棒性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。本文聚焦于时间序列异常检测(time-series anomaly detection)的评分协议评估，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。唯一可能的间接联系是"评估指标鲁棒性"这一方法论思路，但场景和任务完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：时间序列异常检测(TSAD)评分协议的鲁棒性审计；场景：通用时间序列异常检测（UCR Anomaly Archive, SMD, SMAP, MSL, NAB, PSM）；传感器类型：不涉及遥感传感器；目标类别：时间序列中的异常点/段。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。本文评估的是评分协议/metrics而非检测模型本身。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署，未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：UCR Anomaly Archive（250个时间序列，主基准）、SMD、SMAP、MSL、NAB、PSM（5个额外基准）；评价指标：PA%K, range-based precision/recall, affiliation precision/recall, VUS-ROC, VUS-PR等12种评分协议。

Experiments: 对12种评分协议进行对抗性测试，使用无技能评分生成器（trivial and adversarial no-skill score generators），采用预注册的SOTA-relative gameability标准（无技能生成器达到最佳真实检测器得分的90%即视为被攻破）。结果：affiliation-F1在99%的序列上被攻破，ROC-based指标约62-64%被攻破，PR-based指标和PA%K仅14-18%被攻破。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 仅关注时间序列异常检测场景；3. 未讨论任何图像/视觉任务中的指标问题；4. 未涉及模型轻量化或部署。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极为有限。唯一可能的启发是：在遥感目标检测模型评估中，应谨慎选择评价指标，避免使用容易被"攻破"的指标（如ROC-AUC变体），优先考虑PR-based指标。但这一启示非常泛化，且遥感目标检测领域已有成熟的mAP、F1等指标体系。

Abstract: Point-adjustment (PA), long the default scoring protocol in time-series anomaly detection (TSAD), was shown by Kim et al. (2022) to award near-perfect F1 to random scores. The field migrated to replacement metrics: PA%K, range-based precision/recall, affiliation precision/recall, and Volume-Under-the-Surface (VUS) ROC/PR. But did the fix work? Every robustness check to date is proposer-run or theoretical; no independent, adversarial, SOTA-relative audit on real benchmarks exists. We provide one. We stress-test 12 adopted metrics against trivial and adversarial no-skill score generators on the 250-series UCR Anomaly Archive (primary) plus five further benchmarks (SMD, SMAP, MSL, NAB, PSM), under a pre-registered SOTA-relative gameability criterion (a metric is gamed on a series if a no-skill generator reaches >=90% of the best real detector's score). The fix is only partial: affiliation-F1 (gamed on 99% of series) and every ROC-based metric tested (all roughly 62-64%) are gamed by no-skill detectors, genuinely so (real detectors score 0.97-1.00 on the very series they game), while the PR-based metrics and PA%K resist (14-18%). A decisive, one-directional McNemar split: VUS-ROC is gamed on 119 series where its sibling VUS-PR is not, and the reverse on none. The ROC-over-PR direction replicates across all five further benchmarks and never reverses, but the absolute gameability rate is benchmark-dependent and no single metric stays safe on all six (even VUS-PR is gamed on NAB). We release a pip-installable stress-test harness with all metric implementations vendored at frozen commits, plus a metric-selection decision protocol: prefer a PR-based metric or PA%K, treat affiliation-F1 and the ROC-AUC variants as gameable, and verify per benchmark rather than assuming robustness.

</details>


### [232] [Learning the Graphical Nature of Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12026)
*Rashid Barket,Enrico Grimaldi,Yacoub Hendi,Edward Hirst,Adam Onus,Harmeet Singh*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个包含131,406个Cayley图的群论数据集，用于研究有限群代数性质与图结构之间的关系，并比较了传统模型、MLP和GNN在预测群性质上的表现。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文属于纯数学（群论）与图神经网络的交叉研究，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：从Cayley图数据预测有限群的代数性质（群分类、群属性预测）。场景：纯数学/图论。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：有限群（群论对象），非遥感目标。

Model architecture: 比较了MLP、GIN（Graph Isomorphism Network）、GCN（Graph Convolutional Network）等图神经网络架构。摘要未说明具体网络层数、通道数等细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等）。

Onboard deployability: 完全不涉及星上/边缘部署。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：131,406个Cayley图，覆盖阶数≤767（除512阶外）的所有有限群。指标：摘要未明确说明具体评价指标，提及了"predicting algebraic group properties"的准确率或性能比较。

Experiments: 比较了传统工程化图统计特征模型、MLP、GIN和GCN在预测群性质上的表现。结果表明工程化图统计特征信息量丰富，GNN（尤其是GIN和特定固定阶设置下的GCN）能从图结构中恢复结构信号。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。该论文聚焦于纯数学群论问题，应用场景、数据模态、任务目标均不匹配。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文的研究对象（Cayley图与群论）、任务（代数性质预测）和方法（图分类）与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化方向无交叉。

Abstract: Finite groups are rigid algebraic objects, whose Cayley graphs expose a rich network geometry through which group-theoretic structure can be measured, compared, and learned. In this paper, a dataset of $131{,}406$ Cayley graphs is constructed, covering all groups of order at most $767$ except order $512$, recording exact algebraic labels for group properties together with a broad collection of graph, cycle, distance, and spectral statistics. This census aims to provide novel benchmarks for studying how finite-group properties are reflected in Cayley graph observables. It also yields new enumerative contributions: alongside recovering known OEIS sequences for standard group classes, new sequences for monolithic groups and for groups generated by at most three, four, and five elements are contributed to the OEIS. The accompanying network analysis identifies several empirical regularities and formulates testable conjectures, including relationships involving square clustering, Cayley graph diameter, average graph disorder, and spectral eigengaps of nilpotent groups. Finally, a comparison between classical models, an MLP, and graph neural network architectures is performed for predicting algebraic group properties directly from Cayley graph data. The results show that engineered graph statistics are highly informative, while GNNs, especially GIN and in some fixed-order settings GCN, can recover substantial structural signal directly from the graph. Such that graph-aware architectures show phases of optimality on these group-theoretic graph representations.

</details>


### [233] [Dynamic Online Processor-Native Inference for State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12095)
*Orestis Kaparounakis*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种基于处理器原生不确定性追踪的贝叶斯滤波技术，通过确定性分层重要性重构实现非线性状态空间模型的高效近似滤波，在匹配结果质量下比直接蒙特卡洛方法平均加速805倍。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于贝叶斯滤波/状态估计的加速推理，属于信号处理与概率推断领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、深度学习模型压缩或星上部署等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。除非对贝叶斯滤波在遥感状态估计（如目标跟踪）中的加速有特殊兴趣，否则优先级极低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：贝叶斯滤波/状态估计；应用场景：传感器数据驱动的动态系统推断；传感器类型：通用噪声传感器（非遥感专用）；目标类别：无。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。提出一种基于处理器原生不确定性追踪的确定性分层重要性重构技术，用于替代粒子滤波和蒙特卡洛似然估计器。
