Abstract: Sensor-rich data-driven applications increasingly use Bayesian approaches to infer latent states of dynamic systems from noisy sensor measurements and physical models. Yet the computation of the likelihood remains an essential bottleneck for accurate posteriors and performant inference. This paper presents a Bayesian filtering technique that uses processor-native uncertainty tracking for both uncertainty propagation and inference. The technique implements deterministic hierarchical importance restructuring through a native operation, giving deterministic latency and bounded memory use for arbitrary models written as program code. Benchmarks across three nonlinear state-space systems compare the approach against particle filters and Monte-Carlo-based likelihood estimators. The technique enables deterministic approximate filtering with as high as 805$\times$ average speedup against direct Monte Carlo work at matched result quality for model evaluation, and Pareto-dominant accuracy-latency trade-offs for posterior inference while remaining competitive in RMSE with baseline particle filters.

</details>

Abstract: True causal relationships are rarely known, and inferring causal graphs from data is hard. A fundamental challenge is how to assess whether a given causal graph is good in the absence of a ground truth. We propose falsifying candidate causal graphs based on whether they can explain the propagation of an outlier event. Our approach leverages a key principle: weak outliers rarely cause strong ones. While this principle has previously been used in root cause analysis to identify root causes without prior knowledge of the graph, we turn it on its head and use it to falsify candidate causal graphs whose implied outlier propagation is inconsistent with the data. To this end, we present the first statistical tests for the hypothesis that a candidate graph is the true causal graph, and show they have false positive control, power guarantees against incorrect causal graphs, and can operate with a single outlier sample.

</details>

Abstract: We consider Bayesian bandit models and prove that Thompson sampling makes at most twice the expected number of mistakes (selections of a suboptimal arm) as any other policy. Our analysis applies as long as the latent arm processes are independent and each arm evolves only when played. For stochastic bandits with best arm defined via mean reward, this confirms a conjecture of Guha and Munagala from 2014, where the factor $2$ is already best possible. The result holds under any nonincreasing sequence of round weights, including fixed horizon and geometric discounting.

</details>

Abstract: We study the squared $2$-Wasserstein distance to the standard Gaussian as a non-Gaussianity criterion and use it for linear Independent Component Analysis (ICA) and causal inference in Linear Non-Gaussian Acyclic Models (LiNGAM). The analysis relies on a strict inequality between the Wasserstein non-Gaussianity of independent standardized sources and that of their linear combinations. When at most one source is Gaussian, any unit-norm linear combination involving at least two sources has strictly smaller squared Wasserstein distance than the corresponding weighted sum of source distances. At the population level, this yields exact identification of the ICA unmixing matrix, up to signed permutation, and gives an analogous characterization of causal orders through least-squares residuals. We then define empirical plug-in estimators and prove distribution-free uniform convergence bounds under finite-moment assumptions, before detailing three practical solvers: a Picard-style orthogonal optimizer for ICA, an exhaustive dynamic program for causal-order search, and a greedy order-search variant. Empirically, we demonstrate competitive performance for both tasks and provide open-source implementations for source separation and causal inference.

</details>

Abstract: Circular data, representing angles or directions, are frequently encountered in computer vision, biology, geology, and meteorology. Traditional regression targets the conditional mean, which is often geometrically misleading for circular responses under multimodal, skewed, or asymmetric data structures. To address these limitations, a lightweight deep generative framework, namely ANGLE, is introduced for non-parametric distributional regression on the circle. The full conditional distribution of an angular response, given Euclidean and circular covariates, is learned through a generative map optimized via a generalized circular energy score (GCES) loss. Desirable theoretical properties, including the strict propriety of the loss and the rotational equivariance of the estimators, are established. Furthermore, both pre- and post-additive noise models are accommodated. A unified toolbox is provided for advancing previously underexplored challenges in circular statistics: extrapolation, sufficient dimension reduction, and conditional distribution equality testing. The framework's efficacy is demonstrated through extensive simulations and real-world applications. Specifically, the proposal is utilized for object pose estimation from imagery and wind direction prediction, which are integral to surveillance, autonomous vehicles, and energy systems, respectively. Superior predictive performance and robust uncertainty quantification of the proposed method in these tasks are revealed.

</details>


### [238] [Accelerated Mixing Time of Randomized Hamiltonian Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12902)

Abstract: We show the Randomized Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (RHMC) algorithm has accelerated mixing time guarantees for sampling from log-concave probability distributions. RHMC proceeds by repeatedly simulating the continuous-time Hamiltonian dynamics for some random integration times, and resetting the velocity to be an independent Gaussian random variable between each simulation. We show that when the target distribution is log-concave and satisfies an $α$-Talagrand inequality (for example, if the target distribution is $α$-strongly log-concave), if we use a random integration time from either the triangular or the exponential distribution with mean $Θ(α^{-1/2})$, then RHMC converges exponentially fast in KL divergence, and the total integration time to reach error $\varepsilon$ in KL divergence scales as $O(α^{-1/2} \log(\varepsilon^{-1}))$. We also show that when the target distribution is log-concave, if we use a sequence of random integration times from the triangular distribution with exponentially increasing means, then the total integration time to reach error $\varepsilon$ in KL divergence scales as $O(\varepsilon^{-1/2})$. Our analysis relies on a bound on the average KL divergence along Hamiltonian dynamics, which is inspired by an analogous result on accelerated optimization methods based on Hamiltonian dynamics.

</details>


### [239] [LatentFlow: A General Framework for Conditioning Stochastic Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12922)
*Louis Sharrock,Lachlan Astfalck,Henry Moss*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出LatentFlow，一个无需训练、无需神经近似的随机过程条件化统一框架，通过潜在空间概率流实现精确条件采样。

Abstract: Stochastic-process models are, as a rule, far easier to simulate than to condition. Non-linear observations, non-Gaussian likelihoods, black-box information, and global constraints all induce intractable conditional laws, requiring bespoke, model-specific constructions. We introduce LatentFlow, a single framework for conditioning stochastic processes, with no learned neural approximations and no training. Our starting point is to write the stochastic process as the deterministic image of a tractable latent innovation, $f_0 = T_{\vartheta}(ξ_0)$, with $ξ_0$ sampled from a simple reference distribution. This reduces process-level conditioning to latent-space inference: pull the likelihood back through $T_{\vartheta}$, sample the resulting latent law with a tractable guided probability flow, and push the samples forward. This construction is provably exact at the level of the target law; in practice, approximation enters only through finite terminal noising, Monte Carlo guidance, and time discretisation of the continuous-time dynamics, each of which is explicit and systematically reducible. As LatentFlow is training-free, conditioning reduces to solving a single reverse-time SDE. This enables conditional sampling in seconds on a single desktop CPU across model classes that have never shared a scalable method: classical spatial priors, nonlinear stochastic dynamics, mechanistic models from the physical and life sciences, stochastic PDEs, heavy-tails and extremes, point and discrete-state processes, and neural or simulator-defined processes.

</details>


### [240] [Ensemble Controlled-Flow Filtering for Implicit Data Assimilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12975)
*Zhuoyuan Li,Yue Zhao,Ming Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出隐式数据同化框架及Ensemble Controlled-flow Filter (EnCF)，用于处理非高斯、多对一、隐式观测模型下的状态估计问题。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文属于数据同化（data assimilation）与状态估计领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、轻量化或星上部署等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：数据同化（data assimilation）、状态估计（state estimation）。应用场景：动力系统状态估计。传感器类型：摘要未说明。目标类别：不涉及目标检测。

Model architecture: EnCF（Ensemble Controlled-flow Filter），基于随机受控流（stochastic controlled flow）实现分析更新，通过伴随匹配（adjoint matching）学习观测依赖的控制项。EnCF-LF变体通过代理条件能量（surrogate conditional energy）处理模拟器定义的观测。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。理论分析包括理想精确性（ideal exactness）、一步误差分解（one-step error decomposition）和局部误差非累积性（non-accumulation of local errors）。数值实验对比了Kalman型滤波器。

Experiments: 数值结果表明：Kalman型滤波器在平滑加性高斯观测下仍更优；所提方法更适合非高斯、多对一、多模态和隐式观测模型。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。摘要未说明计算效率、实时性、嵌入式部署等关键信息。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于数据同化领域，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题的研究目标、技术路线和应用场景均不匹配。

Abstract: Data assimilation estimates the state of a dynamical system from model forecasts and incoming observations. Many observation mechanisms, however, are many-to-one, implicit, non-smooth, or accessible only through simulation, and need not provide the residual structures or likelihood guidance required by existing ensemble filters. We introduce implicit data assimilation, in which the analysis law is defined as an energy tilt of the forecast distribution. We then propose the Ensemble Controlled-flow Filter (EnCF), which realizes this update through a stochastic controlled flow and learns the observation-dependent control by adjoint matching from terminal energy gradients. For simulator-defined observations, EnCF-LF learns a surrogate conditional energy from samples and applies the same controlled-flow solver. We prove ideal exactness, derive a one-step error decomposition, and establish non-accumulation of local errors under filter stability. Numerical results show that Kalman-type filters remain preferable for smooth additive-Gaussian observations, while the proposed methods are better suited to non-Gaussian, many-to-one, multimodal, and implicit observation models.

</details>

### [241] [BAT-RM: A Boundary-Aware Transformer with Region-Aware Multi-Directional Mamba for Clinically Deployed Cervical Cancer Radiotherapy Auto-Contouring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11949)
*Istiak Ahmed,Kazi Shahriar Sanjid,Galib Ahmed,Md. Tanzim Hossain,Md. Anwarul Islam,Shahrukh Khan,Md. Ashrif Rahman Arian,Md. Nishan Khan,Md. Misbah Khan,S M Hasibul Hoque,Rahnuma Shahrin Rista,Md. Jobairul Islam,Sheikh Anisul Haque,Md Arifur Rahman,Syed Md. Akram Hussain,Syeda Nashra,Sayeed Shafayet Chowdhury,Md. Mostafa Kamal Sarker,M. Monir Uddin*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出BAT-RM混合架构（Transformer+Mamba），用于宫颈癌放疗自动勾画系统，已在临床部署，显著提升勾画精度和效率。

Research relevance: 1分，基本无关。该论文面向医学影像（宫颈癌放疗CT勾画），与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景完全无关；不涉及目标检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、蒸馏等模型压缩方法。唯一可借鉴的是其Mamba模块用于长程上下文建模的线性复杂度思路，但应用场景和任务目标完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。仅当对Mamba架构在视觉任务中的应用感兴趣时，可作为背景参考。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：医学图像分割（auto-contouring for cervical cancer radiotherapy）；场景：临床放疗规划；传感器类型：CT（医学影像）；目标类别：GTV、CTV、直肠、膀胱等7个解剖类别。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: BAT-RM：Boundary-Aware Transformer with Region-Aware Mamba，混合架构，包含Sobel-gated boundary attention、线性时间多方向Mamba模块、boundary-skeleton-guided fusion gate。Mamba模块用于长程上下文建模，复杂度为线性O(n)，避免全空间自注意力的二次复杂度。

Lightweight method: 摘要未明确提及轻量化方法。Mamba模块本身具有线性时间复杂度，可视为一种高效架构设计，但未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等典型轻量化技术。未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟等部署指标。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署指标。系统已临床部署于合作医院，基于Web界面兼容Varian、RayStation、Monaco等放疗系统，但这是临床软件部署，与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：多机构数据收集，独立外部验证集；评价指标：IoU（交并比），13名放射肿瘤科医生的前瞻性多中心读者研究；未提及遥感领域常用指标如mAP、F1、Recall等。

Experiments: 与4个基线方法对比，在7个解剖类别上取得更优性能；AI辅助将初级医生IoU从0.899提升至0.965，接近高级医生水平；勾画时间减少80%以上；减少专家会诊率，提高读者间一致性；患者等待时间从数天缩短至数小时。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未提供模型参数量、FLOPs、推理速度等轻量化关键指标；3. 未讨论模型在资源受限设备上的部署可行性；4. Mamba模块虽为线性复杂度，但未与遥感场景中的轻量化需求结合。

Ideas for my research: 1. Mamba架构的线性复杂度特性值得关注——若将其引入遥感目标检测的backbone或neck设计，可能替代Transformer的自注意力机制，在保持长程依赖建模能力的同时降低计算开销，这对星上部署有潜在价值；2. Sobel-gated boundary attention的边界感知机制可借鉴用于遥感小目标的边缘增强检测；3. 但需注意：本文是医学分割任务，需重新设计适配遥感目标检测的检测头和损失函数。

Abstract: We present a clinically deployed end-to-end auto-contouring system for cervical cancer radiotherapy planning, anchored by the Boundary-Aware Transformer with Region-Aware Mamba (BAT-RM), a hybrid architecture that integrates Sobel-gated boundary attention, a linear-time, multi-directional Mamba module for long-range context, and a boundary-skeleton-guided fusion gate. This design achieves linear-time complexity for long-range context modeling, avoiding the quadratic cost of full spatial self-attention. The full pipeline spans multi-institutional data collection, rigorous inter-rater quality assurance, external validation in an independent cohort, and a web-based clinical interface natively compatible with Varian, RayStation, and Monaco. Against four baselines, BAT-RM achieves superior performance across seven anatomical classes, with statistically significant improvements in target volumes, including GTV and CTV, and in organs at risk such as the rectum and bladder. A prospective multi-center reader study involving 13 radiation oncologists demonstrated that AI assistance elevates junior oncologists' IoU from 0.899 to 0.965, approaching senior-level accuracy, while reducing contouring time by more than 80%. The system also reduced expert consultation rates and improved inter-reader consistency, reflecting gains in both efficiency and quality assurance. Following clinical deployment at a partner hospital, the system reduced patient wait times from days to hours without additional staffing, enabling same-day or next-day initiation of treatment for routine cases. BAT-RM demonstrates that a rigorous research pipeline, from data curation to clinical deployment, can translate directly into measurable patient benefit in resource-constrained settings where the demand for radiotherapy far exceeds specialist capacity.

</details>


### [242] [HPC-Enabled Video-based Coastal Wave Parameter Estimation Using V-JEPA and Deep Spatiotemporal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11998)
*Abubakar Hamisu Kamagata,Dharm Singh Jat,Attlee Munyaradzi Gamundani,Saravanakumar Paramasivam,Babangida Sani,Aliyu Zakariyya*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于视频和HPC的深度学习框架，利用V-JEPA自监督ViT骨干和SlowFast时序编码器，从单目海岸视频中联合估计五个波浪参数（波高、周期、方向），不涉及遥感卫星目标检测或轻量化。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。论文研究的是海岸波浪参数估计（wave parameter estimation），使用地面单目视频（monocular coastal video），不涉及遥感卫星、卫星图像、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然使用了ViT和自监督学习，但应用场景、任务类型和优化目标与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：波浪参数回归（wave parameter regression），包括有效波高、最大波高、峰值周期、零上跨周期、波方向。场景：海岸带视频监测。传感器：单目可见光摄像机（非卫星传感器）。目标类别：无目标检测，为回归任务。

Model architecture: V-JEPA（自监督）ViT Small骨干 + 双流SlowFast时序编码器 + Farneback光流分支 + 多任务回归层（含Airy波色散约束）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。模型在NVIDIA DGX A100集群上训练，未提及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。模型在DGX A100集群上训练，未提及星上或边缘部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：6个标注训练场景（data-limited regime），含地理分布不同的测试站点。评价指标：Pearson correlation coefficient (PCC)，R²值。PCC范围0.451~0.832，R²最大0.246。

Experiments: 模型在epoch 31早停。PCC: Hs=0.451, Hmax=0.578, Tp=0.643, Tz=0.680, wave direction=0.832。R²最大0.246。验证了在数据有限条件下的可行性。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 无任何轻量化或部署优化；3. R²值较低（最大0.246），方差捕获能力有限；4. 仅6个训练场景，数据量极小；5. 未与任何基线方法对比。

Ideas for my research: 可借鉴点极少。唯一可能相关的是V-JEPA自监督ViT骨干在数据有限场景下的特征提取能力，但该思路在遥感目标检测中已有类似工作（如MAE预训练）。整体而言，本文对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的参考价值很低。

Abstract: High deployment cost, poor spatial coverage and susceptibility to storm conditions are all challenges faced by traditional in-situ methods. This paper presents a video-based and high performance computing (HPC) enabled deep learning framework for joint sensor free estimation of five coastal wave parameters, namely significant wave height (Hs), maximum wave height (Hmax), peak period (Tp), zero upcrossing period (Tz) and wave direction (theta) from monocular coastal video. The proposed architecture comprises of a V-JEPA (self supervised) ViT Small backbone for robust spatiotemporal feature extraction in visually challenging scenarios, a dual-stream SlowFast temporal encoder for broad bandwidth representation of wave motion in both hydrodynamic breaking and swell regimes, an optical flow stream based on Farneback optical flow algorithm for adding saliency information to the structure with emphasis on hydrodynamically active wavelength bands of waves, and a multi-task regression layer with dispersion constraints (Airy wave dispersion lambda_p = 0.1). The model was trained on an NVIDIA DGX A100 cluster and was early stopped at epoch 31 and achieved Pearson correlation coefficients of 0.451, 0.578, 0.643, 0.680 and 0.832 for Hs, Hmax, Tp, Tz and wave direction respectively, with generalization ability to geographically diverse held out test data sites. While operating in a data-limited regime (6 annotated training scenes), the framework demonstrates statistically significant temporal correlations (PCC of 0.451 to 0.832), confirming proof of concept feasibility; R2 values (max 0.246) indicate that variance capture will improve with larger annotated datasets.

</details>


### [243] [Analyzing Image Encoder Choices and Graph Homophily in GCN Frameworks for Breast Ultrasound Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12054)
*Sabahattin Mert Daloglu,Ceren Coskun,Harvey Castro,Soner Hacihaliloglu,Ilker Hacihaliloglu*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 系统评估五种图像编码器（CNN和Transformer）对基于GCN的乳腺超声分类中图构建和分类性能的影响，发现高容量编码器能提升图同质性（homophily）和分类准确率。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦乳腺超声医学图像分类，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化方法。

Reading priority: 1分，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：乳腺超声图像分类（benign vs malignant）；场景：医学超声筛查；传感器：超声成像；目标类别：良性/恶性病变。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 五种图像编码器（CNN和Transformer架构）+ 单层GCN + 线性分类头；图构建方式为cosine相似度k近邻图。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 乳腺超声数据集（具体名称摘要未说明）；评价指标：accuracy, AUC, sensitivity, specificity, F1-score；三折患者级交叉验证。

Experiments: 对比五种编码器（CNN和Transformer），高容量编码器在图同质性（homophily）和分类性能上均更优；发现测试集图同质性与分类准确率呈强线性正相关。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对乳腺超声医学图像，与遥感场景无关；2. 未涉及目标检测任务；3. 未涉及任何模型轻量化或边缘部署；4. 未讨论推理效率。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。唯一可参考的是"图同质性（homophily）"作为评估表示质量的指标这一思路，但需在遥感目标检测场景下重新验证，且GCN在遥感目标检测中并非主流范式。

Abstract: Breast ultrasound is widely used for screening, yet automated analysis remains challenging due to speckle noise, acquisition variability, and weak separation of benign and malignant cases in standard ultrasound imaging. Graph convolutional networks (GCNs) have recently emerged as a promising approach by leveraging relationships among similar patient samples. However, it remains unclear how the choice of image encoder influences graph construction and downstream classification performance. In this work, we systematically evaluate five image encoders spanning convolutional and transformer-based architectures for GCN-based breast ultrasound classification. Image embeddings are used to construct cosine similarity k-nearest-neighbor graphs, which are classified using a single-layer GCN with a linear classification head. Across three patientwise cross-validation folds, higher-capacity encoders consistently improve graph homophily and downstream classification performance, yielding gains in accuracy, AUC, sensitivity, specificity, and F1-score. Moreover, test-set graph homophily exhibits a strong linear correlation with classification accuracy, with higher-capacity encoders consistently occupying the high-homophily, high-accuracy region suggesting that encoder-driven improvements in graph structure are a key mechanism underlying the observed performance gains. These findings establish encoder selection as a critical factor in graph-based breast ultrasound classification and identify graph homophily as a key indicator linking representation quality to downstream classification performance.

</details>


### [244] [Calibrated Selective Prediction Using Deep Ensembles for ROI-Based Thyroid Nodule Ultrasound Classification Under Dataset Shift: A Retrospective Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12075)
*Md. Sadibul Hasan Sadib,Md. Mohayminul Mukit,Rahmatul Kabir Rasel Sarker,Tahmid Alam Tamim,Md. Monir Hossain Shimul*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于深度集成(Deep Ensemble)的甲状腺结节超声图像分类与选择性分诊框架，使用ConvNeXt-Tiny骨干网络、集成平均概率和互信息(MI)作为不确定性度量，实现校准概率输出和选择性转诊。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文任务为甲状腺结节超声图像分类与临床分诊，属于医学影像分析领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等均无任何关联。不涉及目标检测任务，也不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署等方向。

Reading priority: 1分。论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化均无直接关系，属于医学影像分析领域，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：甲状腺结节超声图像分类（ROI-based分类）与选择性图像分诊（triage）；应用场景：临床超声诊断；传感器类型：超声（ultrasound）；目标类别：甲状腺结节良恶性分类。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: ConvNeXt-Tiny作为骨干网络，加入squeeze-and-excitation attention；五成员深度集成（five-member deep ensemble）；集成平均恶性概率（ensemble-mean malignancy probability）；互信息（Mutual Information, MI）作为集成不一致性分数（ensemble-disagreement score）；三级分诊策略（No-FNA suggestion / FNA recommendation / radiologist review）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法。虽然使用了ConvNeXt-Tiny（相对轻量的骨干），但论文核心贡献在于校准（vector-scaling calibration）、不确定性估计和选择性预测，而非模型压缩或加速。未提及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。未涉及星上/边缘部署场景。虽然使用了ConvNeXt-Tiny，但未给出任何效率指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：TN5000（模型开发，五折交叉验证）、TN3K（独立外部数据集，用于评估数据集漂移下的泛化性）；评价指标：AUC-ROC、AP（Average Precision）、ECE（Expected Calibration Error）、Brier score、NPV（Negative Predictive Value）、PPV（Positive Predictive Value）、恶性捕获率（malignancy capture）。

Experiments: 内部验证（TN5000 pooled out-of-fold）：AUC-ROC 0.9395, AP 0.9715, ECE 0.0088, Brier score 0.0813；在50%名义MI保留率下，7.2% No-FNA建议（NPV 98.3%），39.9% FNA推荐，52.9%放射科医生审查（恶性捕获率99.83%）。外部验证（TN3K）：AUC-ROC降至0.7870, AP降至0.7254, ECE增至0.1899, Brier score增至0.2281；阈值不可迁移性明显，83.7%被分配到审查路径。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 外部数据集漂移下校准性能显著下降，阈值不可迁移；3. 未提供模型效率指标（参数量、FLOPs、推理速度等）；4. 仅针对超声图像二分类任务，非目标检测任务。

Ideas for my research: 尽管论文与遥感在轨目标检测无关，但以下思路可能有间接参考价值：1. 深度集成（Deep Ensemble）结合不确定性估计（如互信息MI）可用于遥感在轨目标检测中的模型可靠性评估，帮助识别OOD（分布外）样本；2. 选择性预测（Selective prediction）策略可借鉴到星上场景——当模型对检测结果置信度低时，可选择将原始数据下传而非在轨处理，避免误检；3. Vector-scaling校准方法可用于提升遥感检测模型的概率校准质量。但以上均为间接启发，非核心贡献。

Abstract: Background: Deep learning models can classify thyroid nodules on ultrasound, but reliable clinical decision support also requires calibrated probabilities, uncertainty estimation, and selective referral, particularly under dataset shift.
  Methods: We developed a calibrated deterministic five-member deep ensemble for ROI-based thyroid nodule classification and selective image-based triage. TN5000 was used for model development, five-fold cross-validation, member-wise vector-scaling calibration, and fold-specific threshold selection. TN3K served as an independent external dataset-shift evaluation. The framework used ConvNeXt-Tiny with squeeze-and-excitation attention, ensemble-mean malignancy probability, and mutual information (MI) as an ensemble-disagreement score. A three-tier policy assigned images to No-FNA suggestion, FNA recommendation, or radiologist review.
  Results: On pooled out-of-fold TN5000 predictions, the ensemble achieved AUC-ROC 0.9395, AP 0.9715, ECE 0.0088, and Brier score 0.0813. At 50% nominal MI retention, 7.2% of cases received a No-FNA suggestion, 39.9% an FNA recommendation, and 52.9% radiologist review, with 98.3% No-FNA NPV and 99.83% malignancy capture. On TN3K, AUC-ROC decreased to 0.7870, AP to 0.7254, ECE increased to 0.1899, and Brier score to 0.2281. The frozen TN5000 policy assigned 83.7% to review, 1.0% to No-FNA, and 15.3% to FNA recommendation. No malignant image entered the No-FNA pathway, but FNA-recommendation PPV fell to 76.6%.
  Conclusion: The framework showed strong internal discrimination and calibration, but limited external threshold transportability. Selective prediction may help identify images unsuitable for automated triage, but local recalibration, threshold validation, and prospective clinical evaluation are required before deployment.

</details>


### [245] [Uncertainty-Aware Multi-Source Retinal Fluid Segmentation in OCT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12212)
*Animesh Kumar*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种注意力引导的TransUNet，用于OCT视网膜积液分割，结合域自适应归一化和不确定性估计，在四个独立OCT源上达到0.78的平均Dice，不确定性在专家标注分歧区域高出1.34倍。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文研究的是医学图像（OCT视网膜积液分割），属于医学影像分析领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关场景，也不涉及目标检测任务（而是语义分割），更不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、边缘部署等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。除非对域自适应或不确定性估计在遥感中的迁移应用有特别兴趣，可作为泛读参考。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：医学图像语义分割（视网膜积液分割）；应用场景：眼科临床诊断（黄斑病变治疗决策）；传感器类型：OCT（光学相干断层扫描）；目标类别：三种视网膜积液类型。与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: 注意力引导的TransUNet（Attention-guided TransUNet），结合Transformer和U-Net架构。摘要未说明具体网络深度、通道数等细节。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法。没有剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计、实时推理或边缘部署相关内容。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。该模型面向临床工作站而非星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：四个独立OCT源（不同扫描仪）；评价指标：平均流体Dice（0.78）、不确定性指标（专家分歧区域不确定性高出1.34倍，p<10^-4）。

Experiments: 主要结果：平均流体Dice=0.78；不确定性在专家标注分歧区域高出1.34倍（p<10^-4），可作为临床分诊信号。摘要未提供与其他方法的对比结果或消融实验。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但明显的问题包括：仅针对医学OCT图像，与遥感场景无关；未涉及任何轻量化或部署优化；域自适应方法仅针对不同OCT扫描仪间的域偏移，而非遥感中常见的传感器/分辨率/光照/天气等域差异。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但以下两点可抽象参考：(1) 域自适应归一化（domain-adaptive normalisation）的思想可迁移到遥感多传感器（如不同分辨率、不同光谱配置的卫星图像）间的域适应问题；(2) 不确定性估计（uncertainty estimate）用于标记不可靠像素的思路，可借鉴到遥感目标检测中标注质量不一致或遮挡严重场景下的置信度校准。但这两点均非本文核心创新，且需大幅改造才能适配遥感目标检测任务。

Abstract: Measuring retinal fluid from optical coherence tomography (OCT) drives treatment decisions in macular disease, but manual annotation is slow and segmentation models trained on one scanner degrade on another. We present an attention-guided TransUNet that segments three fluid types across four independent OCT sources, combining a domain-adaptive normalisation scheme with an uncertainty estimate that flags unreliable pixels. The model reaches a mean fluid Dice of 0.78, and -- most usefully for clinicians -- its uncertainty is 1.34x higher exactly where expert graders disagree (p<10^-4), turning a raw segmentation map into an actionable clinical triage signal.

</details>


### [246] [Medical Image Segmentation based on Deep Active Contour and Mean Curvature Loss Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12586)
*Xiao-qiang Zhai,Zhi-feng Pang,Peng Zheng,Ze-wen Li,Yan-zhe Hou*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种结合平均曲率的深度主动轮廓损失函数（DACMC），用于医学图像分割，通过卷积核近似平均曲率来引入几何先验约束。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于医学图像分割（liver and spleen），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：医学图像分割（medical image segmentation）；场景：临床分析（clinical analysis）；传感器类型：医学影像（CT/MRI等，摘要未明确）；目标类别：肝脏和脾脏（liver and spleen）。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。核心贡献是DACMC损失函数，利用卷积核近似平均曲率（mean curvature）作为几何约束，集成到深度主动轮廓（Deep Active Contour）框架中。

Lightweight method: 不涉及轻量化方法。虽然提到"用卷积核近似平均曲率以节省计算成本（save computational cost）"，但这属于损失函数层面的计算优化，并非模型压缩或加速方法（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：肝脏和脾脏数据集（liver and spleen dataset），以及多个分割数据集（several segmentation datasets）。评价指标：摘要未明确列出具体指标名称（如Dice, IoU等）。

Experiments: 在肝脏和脾脏数据集上验证，声称达到新的state-of-the-art性能。摘要未提供与其他方法的详细对比结果或消融实验数据。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对医学图像分割，与遥感场景无关；2. 未涉及目标检测任务；3. 未涉及任何模型轻量化或部署优化；4. 摘要未说明在更大规模或多样化数据集上的泛化能力。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可参考的点是：用卷积核近似几何约束（平均曲率）来降低计算开销的思路，如果未来在遥感分割或检测中引入几何先验约束时，可考虑类似的计算优化策略。但整体上与本课题（遥感在轨目标检测+轻量化）方向差异较大。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation is a crucial task in the field of clinical analysis and applications. Though deep learning techniques recently play a crucial role in several scenarios, the training at the individual pixel level leads to a lack of geometric prior information. Scholars proposed to integrate the Chan-Vese model into the loss function for training which can take into account the region and length of the region inside and outside the segmentation process and then improve the performance in medical image segmentation. However, these methods still lack an effective characterization of the segmented region. To overcome this problem, we introduce the mean curvature as a geometric natural constraint and propose a Deep Active Contour and Mean Curvature (DACMC) loss function where the convolution kernel is used to approximate the mean curvature to save computational cost. We have validated the performance of our method on the liver and spleen dataset. Our proposed method demonstrates new state-of-the-art performance on several segmentation datasets.

</details>


### [247] [Improving Autonomous Nano-drones Performance via Automated End-to-End Optimization and Deployment of DNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12593)
*Vlad Niculescu,Lorenzo Lamberti,Francesco Conti,Luca Benini,Daniele Palossi*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套自动化部署流程，将视觉CNN导航模型（PULP-Dronet）部署到超低功耗多核SoC上，在Crazyflie 2.1纳米无人机上实现了内存减半、推理加速1.6倍，且功耗仅占无人机功率预算的1.6%。

Research relevance: 2分（弱相关）。论文涉及轻量化部署（模型压缩、加速、低功耗推理）和无人机视觉导航，但场景是纳米无人机（nano-UAV），而非遥感卫星。不涉及遥感图像、卫星、Earth observation、SAR/高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心要素。轻量化方法（自动化部署流程、内存优化、推理加速）对星上部署有间接借鉴意义，但非直接相关。

Reading priority: 2分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向弱相关，主要价值在于低功耗部署的自动化流程思路，但场景和任务差异大，不建议优先精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：视觉CNN导航（自主避障、自由飞行、车道跟随）；场景：纳米无人机（nano-UAV），Crazyflie 2.1平台；传感器类型：摘要未明确说明（应为机载摄像头）；目标类别：无目标检测，属于导航/控制任务。

Model architecture: PULP-Dronet（基于CNN的自主导航模型），部署在ULP多核SoC（超低功耗多核片上系统）上。摘要未说明具体网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 自动化部署流程（automated deployment flow），包括模型压缩（memory footprint减少2x）、推理加速（inference speedup 1.6x）。具体技术（剪枝/量化/蒸馏等）摘要未明确说明，但属于模型优化和部署工具链层面的轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 部署平台：ULP多核SoC（超低功耗多核片上系统），Crazyflie 2.1纳米无人机；计算资源消耗：<1.6%无人机功率预算；内存占用：减少2x；推理速度：加速1.6x；参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗具体数值摘要未说明。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：摘要未说明训练数据集；评价指标：峰值制动速度（peak braking-speed 1.65 m/s）、速度/制动空间比（speed/braking-space ratio）、自由飞行速度（1.96 m/s vs baseline 0.5 m/s）、90度转弯车道跟随能力；预测精度：与原始手工CNN相同。

Experiments: 对比对象：原始手工CNN（hand-crafted CNN）；实验结果：内存占用减少2x，推理速度提升1.6x，预测精度不变；实际飞行表现：避障峰值制动速度1.65 m/s，自由飞行速度提升至1.96 m/s（baseline 0.5 m/s），实现90度转弯车道跟随；代码已开源（Crazyflie 2.1兼容）。

Limitations: 1. 场景为纳米无人机，非遥感卫星，平台约束（计算/内存/功耗）与卫星差异大；2. 不涉及遥感图像处理、目标检测任务；3. 轻量化方法的具体技术细节（是否使用剪枝/量化/蒸馏等）摘要未说明；4. 未报告参数量、FLOPs等标准轻量化指标；5. 未涉及星上部署特有的约束（如辐射环境、实时性要求、SAR/多光谱数据特性）。

Ideas for my research: 1. 自动化部署流程（automated deployment flow）的思路可借鉴到遥感卫星在轨模型部署中，减少人工调优成本；2. 在极低功耗（<1.6%功率预算）下实现实时推理的思路对星上功耗受限场景有参考价值；3. 从训练到闭环评估的端到端部署方法论可迁移；4. 但需注意：纳米无人机与卫星的计算架构（ULP多核SoC vs FPGA/NPU/TensorRT）不同，轻量化方法需重新适配遥感目标检测任务。

Abstract: The evolution of energy-efficient ultra-low-power (ULP) parallel processors and the diffusion of convolutional neural networks (CNNs) are fueling the advent of autonomous driving nano-sized unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs). These sub-10 cm robotic platforms are envisioned as next-generation ubiquitous smart-sensors and unobtrusive robotic-helpers. However, the limited computational/memory resources available aboard nano-UAVs introduce the challenge of minimizing and optimizing vision-based CNNs -- which to date require error-prone, labor-intensive iterative development flows. This work explores methodologies and software tools to streamline and automate all the deployment of vision-based CNN navigation on a ULP multicore system-on-chip acting as a mission computer on a Crazyflie 2.1 nano-UAV. We focus on the deployment of PULP-Dronet, a state-of-the-art CNN for autonomous navigation of nano-UAVs, from the initial training to the final closed-loop evaluation. Compared to the original hand-crafted CNN, our results show a 2x reduction of memory footprint and a speedup of 1.6x in inference time while guaranteeing the same prediction accuracy and significantly improving the behavior in the field, achieving: i) obstacle avoidance with a peak braking-speed of 1.65 m/s and improving the speed/braking-space ratio of the baseline, ii) free flight in a familiar environment up to 1.96 m/s (0.5 m/s for the baseline), and iii) lane following on a path featuring a 90 deg turn -- all while using for computation less than 1.6% of the drone's power budget. To foster new applications and future research, we open-source all the software design in a ready-to-run project compatible with the Crazyflie 2.1

</details>


### [248] [Spatially-Aligned Chroma from Luma Prediction for Lossless JPEG XS Raw Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12636)
*Taizo Suzuki,Soma Yokota,Masaki Onuki*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于CfL增强的Star-Tetrix变换(CfL-STT)，用于改进JPEG XS标准下的原始图像压缩，通过亮度到色度的预测和空间对齐提升无损压缩效率。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于原始图像压缩编码（JPEG XS标准下的CfL-STT变换），不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一个核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：原始图像无损压缩（lossless raw image compression）；场景：JPEG XS编码标准下的CFA采样图像处理；传感器类型：通用相机原始图像传感器（CFA采样）；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。

Model architecture: CfL-STT变换：将Chroma from Luma (CfL)预测集成到Star-Tetrix transform (STT)中，通过水平/垂直方向的线性插值实现亮度样本的空间对齐，构建Y-Delta-Du-Dv色彩空间。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未涉及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：原始图像数据集（raw image datasets，具体名称未说明）；评价指标：编码效率（coding efficiency）、无损压缩性能（lossless compression）、可逆性（exact reversibility）。

Experiments: 实验在JPEG XS参考软件中实现；结果表明：直接应用CfL预测性能依赖图像且可能降低编码效率，而所提CfL-STT在无损原始图像压缩中一致提升编码效率并保持完全可逆性。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 仅针对图像压缩编码，不涉及任何检测或轻量化任务；3. 未提供计算复杂度、推理速度等部署相关指标。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与课题方向基本无关，无直接可借鉴点。如果强行关联，CfL预测中利用亮度信息辅助色度重建的思想，在遥感图像预处理（如多光谱/高光谱波段间相关性建模）中可能有间接启发，但非常薄弱。

Abstract: This study proposes a Chroma from Luma (CfL)-enhanced Star-Tetrix transform (STT), referred to as CfL-STT, for improving raw image compression in JPEG XS. The proposed CfL-STT integrates CfL prediction into the STT to predict chroma components from the luma component in CFA-sampled raw images. Unlike conventional CfL prediction designed for full-color images, the proposed method employs spatially aligned luma samples obtained via linear interpolation along the horizontal and vertical directions to match the chroma sampling grid. This spatial alignment suppresses high-frequency sensor noise while preserving cross-channel correlation, resulting in a more decorrelated Y-Delta-Du-Dv color space. The proposed method was implemented in the JPEG XS reference software and evaluated on raw image datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that a direct application of CfL prediction yields image-dependent performance and may degrade coding efficiency due to the lack of spatial alignment, whereas the proposed CfL-STT consistently improves coding efficiency in lossless raw image compression while preserving exact reversibility.

</details>


### [249] [Exact and Calibrated Diffusion Reconstruction for Digital Breast Tomosynthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12937)
*Imade Bouftini*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于精确数据一致性投影的扩散先验重建方法，用于有限角度数字乳腺断层合成（DBT）成像，解决数据不一致、幻觉定位不准和不确定性未校准三个临床问题。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文研究的是医学影像（数字乳腺断层合成DBT）重建问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、边缘/星上部署等模型压缩或加速方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。除非对扩散模型在医学重建中的精确数据一致性方法有跨领域兴趣，否则优先级最低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：有限角度CT重建（limited-angle DBT reconstruction）；应用场景：医学影像（乳腺癌筛查）；传感器类型：X射线断层合成；目标类别：乳腺组织/病变。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 条件扩散先验（conditional diffusion prior）+ 精确欧几里得投影（exact Euclidean projection）替代近端更新（proximal update），通过m维对偶系统和一次性Gram矩阵AA^T分解实现数据一致性投影。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。虽然投影步骤速度快（4.5 ms/step，248×加速），但这是针对医学重建算法的加速，与遥感在轨部署无关。未报告参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：患者来源的乳腺体模（patient-derived breast phantoms）；评价指标：数据残差（data residual）、期望校准误差（ECE 0.029→0.008）、标准化误差（4.7→0.96）、保真度（fidelity）、深度分辨率（depth-resolution）。与遥感目标检测常用数据集（DOTA, HRSC2016, DIOR, NWPU VHR-10等）和指标（mAP, FPS等）无关。

Experiments: 在患者来源乳腺体模上验证，精确投影将数据残差降至双精度下限（2.4×10^{-13}），保真度提升且不损失深度分辨率；等渗校准将ECE从0.029降至0.008，标准化误差从4.7降至0.96；修复了部署投影仪中20.3%的伴随失配问题。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向的局限性分析。论文本身针对医学DBT重建，未涉及目标检测、遥感场景理解或模型轻量化。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但以下思路可能有间接启发：（1）精确投影替代近似更新的思想，在遥感目标检测的模型压缩中，是否可以用精确的量化/剪枝策略替代近似策略？（2）不确定性校准方法（等渗校准）可迁移到遥感在轨检测的不确定性估计中；（3）对部署算子中伴随失配的修复思路，对遥感模型在FPGA/NPU等硬件部署时的精度校准有参考意义。但整体而言，该论文对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接可借鉴点很少。

Abstract: Limited-angle digital breast tomosynthesis (DBT) reconstructs a volume from a few low-dose projections over a narrow arc. At a representative nine-view, $25^{\circ}$ protocol more than 98% of image space is unmeasured, so a learned prior must supply structure in the missing wedge. Conditional diffusion priors achieve strong perceptual quality here but leave three clinical obstacles: inexact data consistency, unlocalized hallucination, and uncalibrated uncertainty. We enforce measurements exactly by replacing the per-step proximal update of a conditional diffusion sampler with exact Euclidean projection onto the data-consistent set, computed via an $m$-dimensional dual system with a one-time Gram matrix $AA^{\top}$ factorization. This projection costs 4.5 ms per step (a $248\times$ speedup) and drives the data residual to the double-precision floor ($2.4\times10^{-13}$). We prove it is the $ρ\to0$ limit of the proximal step, provide a no-harm theorem, and show that exactly consistent sample ensembles have variance supported on null($A$). Thus, the mean's entire error lies in the unmeasured subspace covered by the uncertainty map. On patient-derived breast phantoms, this improves fidelity at no depth-resolution cost. Conversely, a proximal step applied post-update degrades quality, isolating the consistency step's placement as decisive. Isotonic recalibration brings the ensemble spread to a calibrated error scale (expected calibration error $0.029\to0.008$; standardized error $4.7\to0.96$), ranking errors better than the pure prior. We also repair a 20.3% adjoint mismatch in a deployed projector via a materialized operator of record. This is the first data-consistent, uncertainty-calibrated learned reconstruction for limited-angle DBT. The solver naturally relaxes to discrepancy-ball and maximum-a-posteriori modes for noisy measurements.

</details>

### [250] [Fuse-then-Detect for Passive UAV Localization Using Multi-UE 5G Uplink Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11955)
*Wenyu Huang,Nuria González-Prelcic,Vishnu Ratnam,Murat Bayraktar,Charlie Jianzhong Zhang*

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：仿真城市场景（FR1频段，4个行人UE，100 MHz 5G NR波形）；指标：同步精度（亚纳秒级）、3D定位误差中位数（4.84 m）。

Limitations: 1. 仅基于仿真场景验证，缺乏真实硬件实验；2. 未讨论多无人机场景下的干扰与检测性能；3. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；4. 无任何轻量化或部署相关分析。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的启发是：论文中"跨UE联合检测"的思路或许可类比为遥感中多视角/多时相融合检测，但方向差异过大。

Abstract: Low-altitude uncrewed aerial vehicles (UAVs) can pose growing risks to airspace safety, security, and privacy. Cellular infrastructure can passively sense them without dedicated radar hardware by exploiting integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) technology. Most prior work exploits monostatic sensing or bistatic/multistatic configurations based on downlink measurements. To the best of our knowledge, this paper presents the first uplink framework, where multiple user equipments (UEs) transmit sounding reference signal (SRS) pilots and the base station (BS) receives the UAV-scattered echoes. Sensing from uplink SRS, however, introduces new challenges. Each UE has its own oscillator and timing loop, so the channel estimate at the BS carries residual timing, frequency, and amplitude impairments that corrupt the UAV delay and Doppler. Moreover, the UAV echo is weaker than both the line-of-sight (LOS) path and urban clutter, so detection from a single UE transmission is not reliable. We address these challenges by designing a LOS-referenced synchronization scheme and a joint detector. The synchronization reuses the existing timing advance (TA) command and an adjacent-occasion conjugate product to remove the residuals without additional signaling. Then the detector searches a shared 3D state space and accumulates evidence across UEs. It leverages a normalized contrast that exploits the bistatic geometry. We evaluate the framework in a cluttered urban scene at frequency range 1 (FR1) with four pedestrian UEs and a 100 MHz 5G New Radio (NR) waveform. The proposed pipeline achieves sub-nanosecond synchronization and a 4.84 m median 3D position error.

</details>


### [251] [HAPS-Complemented Terrestrial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12063)
*Animesh Yadav,Halim Yanikomeroglu*

Abstract: We consider a downlink multicell multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) system in an urban region, with a focus on improving the capacity of cell-edge user equipments (UEs). These UEs typically experience lower rates than near UEs because of shadowing, path loss, and inter-cell interference (ICI). To address this issue, we integrate a high-altitude platform station (HAPS) with the terrestrial network as a relay for edge-UE transmissions. We assume that the HAPS operates in full-duplex (FD) mode and exploits its large physical size to enhance passive self-interference (SI) suppression by separating its transmit and receive antennas. In the proposed scheme, each terrestrial base station (BS) forwards edge-UE data to the FD-HAPS, which then relays the data to the intended edge UEs. To design beams at both BSs and HAPS, we formulate a sum-rate maximization problem for under total transmit-power and minimum quality-of-service (QoS) constraints. To solve the resulting non-convex problem, we develop a centralized algorithm based on successive convex approximation (SCA) and alternating optimization (AO) for fast convergence. Simulation results show that relaying information via FD-HAPS significantly improves the capacity of cell-edge UEs compared with a terrestrial-only network.

</details>

Abstract: This paper jointly optimizes the base-station (BS) continuous aperture array (CAPA) dimensions and beamforming functions to maximize energy efficiency (EE) of the downlink multiuser multi-CAPA system, where both the BS and the users are equipped with CAPAs. Since the beamforming functions are continuous current distribution over the BS CAPA, the resulting EE maximization problem is a nontrivial functional optimization problem that couples aperture sizing and beamforming design. To address this challenge, we propose a cascaded network architecture consisting of a graph neural network (GNN) and a functional-gradient based implicit neural representation (FGB-INR) to learn the BS CAPA dimensions and beamforming functions, respectively. Both networks exploit the permutation equivariance of the optimal optimization policy, and the update equations of FGB-INR are designed according to the functional-gradient structure of the EE objective. Simulation results show that the proposed method approaches the EE of the numerical method while substantially reducing inference latency. They also demonstrates that the functional-gradient structure in FGB-INR improves EE while reducing sample complexity and training time.

</details>


### [253] [Local Maxima of the Entrywise $\ell_4$ Norm on the Orthogonal Group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12431)

Abstract: We classify the local maximizers of the entrywise fourth-power objective \[ Q\longmapsto \lVert Q\rVert_4^4 =\sum_{i,j=1}^r q_{ij}^4 \] over the real orthogonal group $\mathcal O(r)$. We prove that the signed permutation matrices are the only local maximizers, and hence the only global maximizers, in every dimension. More strongly, every other stationary point has an explicit rank-two tangent direction with strictly positive second variation. The proof is based on a maximum-entry pivot for the orthostochastic matrix $Q^{\circ2}$: the associated full Riemannian Hessian can be evaluated exactly and is positive at a largest nonunit squared entry. The argument is self-contained and handles zeros, repeated magnitudes, reducible support, and Hadamard-type stationary points.

</details>

Abstract: The direction-of-arrival (DOA) estimation problem using one-bit quantized magnitude-only measurements is studied, where magnitude-only measurements offer robustness against phase errors, thereby avoiding the need for array calibration, while one-bit quantization significantly reduces hardware cost and system complexity. As their direct combination results in meaningless constant measurements, we formulate a sign-consistency optimization problem using a smooth logistic surrogate with ell_2,1-norm regularization to promote joint sparsity. To solve this problem, a proximal-gradient algorithm is developed with guaranteed convergence to a critical point. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves accuracy comparable to coherent one-bit baselines under ideal conditions, while maintaining robust performance under severe phase errors that substantially impair coherent methods.

</details>

Model architecture: AFDM-FTN波形结构、BEM信道估计模型、MLMP多层消息传递算法、BEM-MLMP-JCED联合信道估计与数据检测方案。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及深度学习模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等）。文中"low-complexity"指通信算法层面的计算复杂度降低，非模型压缩。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署信息。仅提及计算复杂度分析，但未给出具体数值。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：未使用任何遥感/视觉数据集；评价指标：BER（误码率）、MSE（均方误差）、频谱效率（SE）。

Experiments: 仿真结果表明所提AFDM-FTN系统在BER性能上与常规AFDM相当，同时提升了频谱效率并降低了接收机复杂度。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。摘要未说明实际硬件部署验证、实时性测试等。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于通信物理层研究，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题的研究范畴（遥感图像处理、深度学习模型压缩、边缘部署）无交集。

Abstract: This paper proposes an affine frequency division multiplexing (AFDM)-aided faster-than-Nyquist (FTN) waveform, termed AFDM-FTN, to enhance spectral efficiency (SE) in high-mobility communication scenarios. We first derive the AFDM-FTN input-output relationship and analyze the FTN-induced interference pattern in AFDM-FTN. To address the channel estimation challenges, a low-complexity channel estimator based on the basis expansion model (BEM) is developed. By exploiting the intrinsic characteristics of the AFDM channel matrix and the FTN coefficient matrix, a multi-layer message passing (MLMP) algorithm is proposed that leverages the sparsity of the time-domain (TD) channel and the FTN coefficient matrix, where belief messages are iteratively propagated across the TD channel, FTN, and transform layers. Building upon the BEM-assisted channel estimation and MLMP, a low-complexity joint channel estimation and data detection scheme (BEM-MLMP-JCED) is further developed to iteratively refine channel estimation with the aid of transmitted data. Finally, the channel estimation lower bound, the mean square error (MSE) performance of the BEM-MLMP-JCED, and the computational complexity are analyzed. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed AFDM-FTN system with BEM-MLMP-JCED achieves comparable BER to conventional AFDM while providing enhanced SE and reduced complexity compared to benchmark receivers.

</details>


### [256] [An Adaptive Transmission Protocol Enabled by The State Switching Strategy of Beyond-Diagonal RIS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12514)
*Xiyao Wang,Hongyu Li*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种BD-RIS（超对角可重构智能表面）的自适应传输方案，通过定义ON/OFF工作状态和波束训练协议，根据信道条件智能切换RIS状态，避免始终开启带来的不必要复杂度。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是无线通信领域的可重构智能表面（RIS）技术，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测、模型压缩或星上部署内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 无线通信中的自适应传输方案，场景为RIS辅助的无线通信系统，不涉及遥感、目标检测或任何视觉任务。

Model architecture: 不涉及深度学习模型。提出的是BD-RIS的ON/OFF状态切换机制和波束训练协议。

Lightweight method: 不涉及模型轻量化。论文中的"复杂度降低"是指通过关闭RIS来减少信号处理的操纵复杂度，而非神经网络模型的剪枝、量化、蒸馏等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及星上部署、边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 使用仿真（Simulation）进行验证，未使用任何遥感或视觉数据集。评价指标为传输成功率（successful transmission）。

Experiments: 仿真结果表明，所提协议在不始终开启BD-RIS的情况下，无论是否存在强直射链路，都能保证成功传输。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的局限性信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于无线通信物理层领域，与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化研究方向无任何交集。

Abstract: Thanks to inter-element interconnections and flexible element arrangements, the beyond diagonal reconfigurable intelligent surface (BD-RIS) breaks through the limitation of traditional RIS to achieve enhanced performance and enlarged coverage. However, existing BD-RIS research assumes that BD-RIS is always turned ON to assist transmission, while, in some scenarios where the transmitter-receiver direct link exists and remains strong, the performance gains of BD-RIS may not justify its manipulation complexity. In order to smartly use BD-RIS, a novel adaptive transmission scheme is proposed in this paper. The proposed scheme first defines two working states of the BD-RIS, namely ON and OFF. Based on these two states, we design a new beam training protocol that enables BD-RIS to intelligently switch its working state according to real-time channel conditions. Furthermore, we construct a decision threshold as the decision basis for state switching to ensure the efficiency and reliability of protocol execution. Simulation results show that, without always turning ON the BD-RIS and performing sophisticated wave manipulation, the proposed protocol can guarantee successful transmission regardless of whether there is a strong transmitter-receiver link or not.

</details>


### [257] [Cellular Signal Constructed Convolutional Vision Transformer for High Accuracy Positioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12518)
*Junshi Chen,Xuhong Li,Russ Whiton,Fredrik Tufvesson*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种混合卷积视觉Transformer(ConViT)架构，用于蜂窝网络中的高精度定位，结合CNN局部去噪和Transformer全局注意力，在商用LTE信号上实现3.46米距离RMSE和2.54度偏航RMSE。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是蜂窝通信系统中的定位问题，使用商用LTE信号和大规模天线阵列，完全不涉及遥感/卫星图像、目标检测或模型轻量化部署。虽然提到了参数数量和计算复杂度降低，但这是通信定位场景下的附带优势，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：蜂窝网络定位（positioning）；场景：城市环境中的非视距信号和强小区间干扰；传感器：大规模天线阵列接收的商用LTE信号；目标类别：不涉及目标检测，仅定位用户设备位置。

Model architecture: ConViT（混合卷积视觉Transformer）：CNN局部感受野用于抑制局部噪声 + Transformer用于捕获不同多径分量之间的全局注意力。还评估了多分布式基站信号融合策略，并应用扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）进行传感器融合以缓解长尾波动。

Lightweight method: 摘要未明确说明轻量化方法。仅提到ConViT相比基准模型具有更低的参数量和更低的计算复杂度，但未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等具体轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等具体部署指标。仅定性提到"lower parameter count and reduced computational complexity"，无定量数据。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：商用LTE信号（城市环境，非视距信号，强小区间干扰）；评价指标：距离RMSE（3.46米）、偏航RMSE（2.54度）。

Experiments: ConViT在距离RMSE（3.46米）和偏航RMSE（2.54度）上显著优于基准模型，同时保持更低的参数量和计算复杂度。还进行了延迟-角度功率分布与Transformer注意力权重的对应分析，证明了模型的可解释性。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关；2. 仅针对蜂窝通信定位场景，不涉及图像或遥感数据；3. 轻量化方面仅有定性描述，无定量指标；4. 未说明模型参数量、FLOPs等具体数值。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。唯一可能参考的点是：ConViT中CNN+Transformer的混合架构思路（CNN局部去噪+Transformer全局建模）在遥感目标检测中也有类似工作（如去除遥感图像噪声、捕获全局上下文），但这已是遥感领域的常见做法，本论文并未提供超越已有遥感方法的创新点。

Abstract: Modern cellular systems employ wide bandwidths and large antenna arrays to meet high data rate requirements. The high spatial and temporal resolution for communication also enables high-accuracy positioning as an ancillary benefit. Standard convolutional neural networks (CNNs) and vision Transformers have demonstrated excellent performance in positioning by leveraging delay-angle domain channel representations. However, they still face practical challenges in complicated cellular environments with low signal-to-noise ratios and severe inter-cell interference. This paper proposes a hybrid convolutional vision Transformer (ConViT) architecture that integrates the local receptive fields of CNNs to suppress local noise and employs Transformers to capture global attention among different multipath components. Various fusion strategies for combining signals from multiple distributed base stations are also evaluated. An extended Kalman filter with sensor fusion is applied to further mitigate long tail fluctuations of model estimates. Comprehensive validation is conducted with commercial long-term-evolution signals received by a large antenna array in urban environments with non line-of-sight signals and strong inter-cell interference. ConViT achieves a distance root mean square error (RMSE) of 3.46 meters and a yaw RMSE of 2.54 degrees, significantly outperforming benchmark models, while maintaining a lower parameter count and reduced computational complexity. Finally, a correspondence analysis between delay-angle power distributions and Transformer attention weights demonstrates the interpretability of the model.

</details>


### [258] [Comparison of Dimension Reduction Methods for EEG Seizure Detection Using Autonomous AI-Driven Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12546)
*Annika Stiehl,Vishal Kagade,Nicolas Weeger,Nicole Ille,Stefan Geißelsöder,Christian Uhl*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 比较四种EEG信号降维方法(PCA/DyCA/DMD/AVD)对深度学习癫痫检测性能的影响，发现方差类方法优于动力学类方法。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于生物医学信号处理(EEG癫痫检测)，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：EEG癫痫发作检测；场景：生物医学信号处理；传感器：多通道脑电图(EEG)；目标类别：癫痫发作/非发作。与遥感场景完全无关。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体深度学习分类器架构，仅提及"autonomous AI-driven research framework independently optimizes architecture and hyperparameters"。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文讨论的是信号降维方法(PCA/DyCA/DMD/AVD)对分类性能的影响，而非模型轻量化技术(剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等)。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。且应用场景为医疗诊断，非星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Temple University Hospital Seizure Corpus (TUSZ v2.0.3)；评价指标：ROC-AUC。

Experiments: AVD(88.28%)和PCA(85.98%)优于DMD(74.56%)和DyCA(74.85%)，AVD验证-测试差距最小。不同表示对应的最优分类器架构不同。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化内容。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。唯一可能的间接启发是：不同输入表示(特征提取方式)会影响下游分类器的最优架构选择，这提示在遥感目标检测中，特征提取与检测头的联合优化可能比独立设计更有效。但这一思路非常泛化，且论文本身不涉及遥感或轻量化。

Abstract: Automated epileptic seizure detection from multichannel electroencephalography (EEG) benefits from dimension reduction to obtain compact, discriminative representations. We compare four signal-space dimension reduction methods, Principal Component Analysis (PCA), Dynamical Component Analysis (DyCA), Dynamic Mode Decomposition (DMD), and Average Volatility Dimensioning (AVD), for deep learning-based seizure detection on the Temple University Hospital Seizure Corpus (TUSZ v2.0.3). To enable a comparison of optimal combinations of representation and classifier, an autonomous AI-driven research framework independently optimizes architecture and hyperparameters for each representation. Measured by test ROC-AUC, the variance-based methods AVD (88.28%) and PCA (85.98%) paired with their respective optimal classifiers outperform the dynamics-based methods DMD (74.56%) and DyCA (74.85%) by over 10%, with AVD also showing the smallest validation-to-test gap. The best-performing classifier architecture differs across representations, indicating that representation and classifier should be optimized jointly. Our results highlight the importance of the input representation for EEG seizure detection and indicate the viability of autonomous AI-driven experimentation in biomedical signal processing.

</details>


### [259] [SpeedyGS: Content-Aware 3D Gaussian Splatting Compression via Two-Stage Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12656)
*Junteng Zhang,Tong Chen,Yuxin Zhao,Yibo Shi,Jing Wang,Zhan Ma*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出SpeedyGS，一种面向3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)数据的内容感知压缩器，通过联合优化自适应量化与剪枝、稀疏八叉树编码和局部自回归熵编码，在压缩率、解码延迟和渲染速度间取得平衡。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS)数据的压缩与解码加速，属于3D场景表示与渲染领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心场景，也不涉及目标检测任务。虽然涉及量化、剪枝、压缩等轻量化技术，但应用场景和目标完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。除非对率-失真联合优化框架的理论方法有特殊兴趣，否则可忽略。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：3D场景表示压缩与解码加速；应用场景：交互式3D渲染、实时图形学；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: SpeedyGS包含两个阶段：(1) 结构形成阶段：联合优化自适应量化(adaptive quantization)和剪枝(pruning)，使用轻量级率代理(lightweight rate proxy)估计下一阶段的熵编码成本；(2) 统计编码阶段：将高斯几何转换为稀疏八叉树token(sparse octree tokens)进行多级编码，高斯属性序列化为1D token流，通过复杂度可控的局部自回归模型(complexity-controllable local autoregressive model)进行熵编码。还支持通道级定长编码(channel-wise fixed-length coding)作为简化替代方案。

Lightweight method: 涉及量化(quantization)、剪枝(pruning)、熵编码(entropy coding)、稀疏八叉树编码、局部自回归模型。但这些方法针对的是3DGS数据的压缩与解码加速，而非神经网络模型的轻量化。不涉及知识蒸馏、NAS、TensorRT/FPGA/NPU等部署优化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明星上/边缘部署相关信息。虽然提及解码延迟(decoding latency)接近零、在消费级硬件上加速优化9倍，但参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等指标均未给出，且与遥感卫星在轨部署场景无关。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。提及"common datasets"（常见数据集），评价指标包括：模型尺寸缩减倍数(up to 160×)、解码延迟(decoding latency)、优化速度(9× acceleration)、渲染速度(rendering speed)、质量退化(quality degradation)。

Experiments: 与vanilla 3DGS相比，SpeedyGS在常见数据集上实现高达160倍的模型尺寸缩减，质量退化可忽略。与最先进的压缩方法相比，解码速度显著更快，优化速度在消费级硬件上加速9倍。

Limitations: (1) 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；(2) 摘要未说明在遥感数据上的表现；(3) 未涉及目标检测任务的精度指标（如mAP）；(4) 未说明在边缘/星上硬件上的实际部署性能。

Ideas for my research: 虽然场景不同，但以下思路可能有间接借鉴价值：(1) 联合优化量化与剪枝的统一率-失真框架(unified rate-distortion objective)思路，可迁移到遥感检测模型的压缩中；(2) 用轻量级率代理(lightweight rate proxy)替代真实熵编码成本来指导压缩，类似思路可用于指导剪枝/量化策略；(3) 复杂度可控的编码策略，可根据硬件资源动态调整。但整体借鉴价值有限，因为3DGS与CNN/Transformer检测模型的结构差异巨大。

Abstract: Recent progress in compressing large-scale 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) data has substantially reduced storage footprint, network transmission bandwidth, and memory traffic to GPU caches before rendering. Yet decoding with advanced 3DGS codecs still takes seconds, making them unsuitable for interactive applications. To systematically address this challenge, we propose SpeedyGS, a Content-Aware 3DGS Compressor that separately optimizes the structural formation and statistical coding. First, in structural formation, we jointly optimize adaptive quantization and pruning under a unified rate-distortion objective, where the rate term is replaced by a lightweight rate proxy that estimates entropy coding cost of the next stage, thereby efficiently regulating Gaussian density and precision to yield a compact scene representation. Then, in the statistical coding phase, Gaussian geometry is converted into sparse octree tokens and subsequently undergoes multi-stage coding, while Gaussian attributes are serialized into a 1D token stream for entropy coding via a complexity-controllable local autoregressive model. SpeedyGS achieves a favorable balance among optimization efficiency, compression performance, decoding latency, and rendering speed. Compared to vanilla 3DGS, SpeedyGS achieves up to 160$\times$ model size reduction with negligible quality degradation across common datasets. Compared to state-of-the-art compression methods, it also offers significantly faster decoding and accelerates optimization by 9$\times$ on consumer-grade hardware. To further reduce decoding overhead, the statistical coding stage also supports channel-wise, fixed-length coding for Gaussian as a simpler alternative, enabling SpeedyGS to better adapt to the underlying application and reduce decoding latency to nearly zero.

</details>


### [260] [Positional Attention-based Graph Neural Network for Learning Permutation Non-equivariant Wireless Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12744)
*Baichuan Zhao,Chenyang Yang,Jianyu Zhao,Di Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于位置注意力的图神经网络，用于学习无线通信中非置换等变的信道估计与端到端预编码策略。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究无线通信中的信道估计与预编码问题，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署均无任何关联。属于无线通信领域的图神经网络方法研究。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：信道估计、端到端预编码；场景：无线通信系统；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基于位置注意力的图神经网络（positional attention-based GNN），通过嵌入函数将顶点相对位置引入注意力机制，以捕获非对称关系。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称；评价指标为信道估计与预编码的性能指标（具体名称未给出）；仿真实验。

Experiments: 仿真结果表明所提GNN优于现有信道估计和端到端预编码方法，训练所需样本更少，可泛化到不同天线数和用户数的系统。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可参考的局限性信息。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与本人研究方向无关，无可借鉴点。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) have emerged as a promising approach to learning wireless policies efficiently by leveraging topology prior and incorporating relational inductive biases. However, when the optimal policy is not permutation equivariant (PE), conventional GNNs suffer from mismatched inductive biases, leading to degraded performance or poor generalizability. This issue arises in wireless tasks with expected objectives, such as channel estimation and end-to-end (E2E) precoding, where the PE property of the optimal policy depends on the underlying channel distribution. In this paper, we propose a novel positional attention-based GNN to learn permutation nonequivariant policies efficiently. The core idea is to incorporate relative positions of vertices into the attention mechanism via an embedding function, enabling the GNNs to capture asymmetric relationships. Consequently, the proposed GNN can represent permutation non-equivariant functions, while retaining high learning efficiency and size generalizability through parameter sharing. We consider channel estimation and E2E precoding as case studies, and prove that their policies are PE to users but not to antennas under spatially correlated channels. We employ the proposed GNN to learn the policies, where the embedding function is designed based on the channel covariance matrix. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed GNN outperforms existing channel estimation and E2E precoding methods, requires fewer samples for training, and can be generalized to systems with different numbers of antennas and users.

</details>


### [261] [Scalable Multi-BD Access for OFDM-Based Symbiotic Backscatter Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12762)
*Fikiri Salum Uledi,Muhammad Bilal Janjua,Muhammad Sohaib J. Solaija,Cagri Ozgenc Etemoglu,Hüseyin Arslan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于OFDM的共生反向散射通信中多反向散射设备的无干扰接入方案，采用正交频率码扩频和块状正交码扩频来抑制直连链路干扰和反向散射设备间干扰。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文属于无线通信领域（symbiotic backscatter communication, OFDM），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文任务为多反向散射设备接入方案设计，场景为OFDM共生反向散射通信系统，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测或任何视觉任务。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文提出的是通信接入方案（OFC方案、块状正交码扩频、非相干能量检测器），而非深度学习模型或神经网络架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS或任何模型轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或星上/边缘部署相关内容。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文使用仿真验证（Rayleigh fading信道），评价指标为平均漏检概率（PMD）和和速率（sum-rate），不涉及任何遥感或目标检测数据集。

Experiments: 仿真结果验证了OFC方案相比传统码辅助多BD接入方案具有更低的PMD和更高的频谱效率。无任何目标检测或轻量化相关实验。

Limitations: 该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关，属于无线通信物理层接入技术，无法提供任何直接借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文研究内容与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化完全无关。

Abstract: We propose an interference-free multi-backscatter device (BD) access scheme for orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM)-based symbiotic backscatter communication (SBC). Specifically, we introduce an orthogonal frequency-code spread (OFC) scheme in which the transmitter reserves empty subcarriers for BDs to incorporate their information through frequency shifting, which effectively suppresses direct-link interference (DLI). Since enabling simultaneous access over shared subcarriers introduces inter-backscatter device interference (IBDI), a blockwise orthogonal code-spread is applied to assign each BD to a unique codeword, thereby mitigating IBDI with orthogonal transmissions of all BD signals in the code domain. We develop a non-coherent energy detector whose performance is analyzed in terms of average probability of missed detection (PMD) and sum-rate under Rayleigh fading. The simulation results validate the OFC scheme and demonstrate low PMD values and high spectral efficiency compared to conventional code-assisted multiple-BD access schemes.

</details>

Abstract: We optimise bidirectional OESCL-band coherent transmission under total fibre power constraints using a GPU-accelerated boundary-value Raman solver and launch-power optimisation. While no gain is observed without power constraints, for an 18-dBm limit 3-span transmission reversing the O-band direction increases aggregate capacity by up to 24.5%

</details>

### [263] [EFLUX: Elastic Multi-Robot Formation Navigation and Adaptation with Agentic LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12050)
*Jinyuan Zhang,Yuwei Wu,Guangyao Shi,Jonathan Diller,Gaurav S. Sukhatme,Vijay Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EFLUX框架，利用大语言模型(LLM)实现多机器人编队在受限环境中的弹性变形与重构导航。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文研究的是多机器人编队导航（multi-robot formation navigation），不涉及遥感图像、卫星观测、目标检测或模型轻量化/部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 多机器人编队导航（multi-robot formation navigation），应用场景为受限/杂乱环境（confined/cluttered environments），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等。

Model architecture: EFLUX框架：基于LLM（大语言模型）的智能体框架，包含结构化场景表示提取、LLM推理变形动作（缩放、剪切）和重构动作（分裂、合并）、闭环生成-验证-修正管线。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性、星上/边缘部署等信息。

Datasets and metrics: 仿真实验和硬件实验（simulation and hardware experiments），具体数据集名称和评价指标摘要未说明。

Experiments: 与基线方法相比，EFLUX减少了死锁和导航失败，保持了连贯的多机器人协调。具体数值摘要未说明。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向上的局限性分析。论文本身未提及在遥感场景下的适用性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。论文涉及LLM驱动的多机器人编队控制，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化无直接关联。唯一可能的间接启发是LLM用于结构化场景推理的思路，但与本课题（轻量化检测模型）方向差异过大。

Abstract: Multi-robot teams operating in confined or cluttered environments must adapt both their formation geometry and group topology to navigate through complex obstacles. This adaptation requires two complementary behaviors: deformation, where the team continuously reshapes its geometry while remaining connected, and reconfiguration, where robots split into subgroups or merge back into a single formation. Existing methods often model these behaviors independently, connect them through handcrafted rules, or lack explicit geometric criteria for determining when each behavior should be invoked. However, challenging environments may require online changes in formation shape, connectivity, and effective team composition, making decoupled or rule-based approaches prone to suboptimal trajectories and deadlock. We propose EFLUX, a geometry-grounded LLM agentic framework for automatic and elastic multi-robot formation navigation. EFLUX extracts a structured scene representation and uses an LLM to reason jointly over both deformation actions, such as scaling and shearing, and reconfiguration actions, such as splitting and merging. These strategies are then translated into executable per-robot waypoints through a closed-loop generation, verification, and correction pipeline. Simulation and hardware experiments show that EFLUX enables safe, continuous, and elastic formation navigation in constrained environments, reducing deadlock and navigation failures compared with baselines while maintaining coherent multi-robot coordination.

</details>


### [264] [Enabling 24-hour Agricultural Robotics: Unsupervised Day-to-Night Cross-Modal Image Translation for Nighttime Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12065)
*Robel Mamo,Rajitha de Silva,Grzegorz Cielniak,Taeyeong Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无监督图像翻译框架，利用CLIP模型将白天农田行RGB图像转换为夜间近红外(NIR)图像，实现夜间农业机器人视觉导航，无需像素级配对标注。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦农业机器人夜间视觉导航，涉及图像翻译和语义分割，不涉及遥感卫星、在轨目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然使用了NIR图像，但属于农业近地机器人场景，非遥感卫星对地观测场景。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：无监督图像翻译（day-to-night translation）、语义分割；场景：农业机器人夜间视觉导航；传感器：RGB相机 + 近红外(NIR)相机；目标类别：农田行（plant-row）、作物/土壤区域。不涉及遥感目标检测。

Model architecture: 基于GAN的图像翻译框架，引入预训练CLIP模型保持语义一致性，加入visibility mask处理NIR照明有限有效范围问题。具体网络结构摘要未详细说明。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要提到在物理机器人上进行了实时自主导航实验，但未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等具体部署指标。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：AgriNight（自建，428张白天+549张夜间图像，像素级语义标注）；评价指标：图像质量（未具体说明指标名称）、下游语义分割性能（mIoU等未明确列出）。

Experiments: 与SOTA图像翻译基线对比，在图像质量和下游语义分割任务上表现更优；在真实机器人上进行了夜间自主导航实验。具体数值摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 未涉及任何目标检测任务；3. 未涉及模型压缩或加速方法；4. 农业近地场景，非遥感卫星对地观测场景；5. 未报告模型效率指标。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。唯一可能相关的点是：CLIP模型用于保持语义一致性的思路，在遥感领域跨模态/跨时相图像翻译中可能有参考价值（如光学-SAR转换、白天-夜间遥感图像转换），但与本课题"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"的核心方向关联很弱。

Abstract: While visual navigation has been extensively studied in agricultural robotics, most existing systems assume daytime conditions. In fact, deploying autonomous robots at night offers significant advantages, including 24-hour crop and soil monitoring, fruit harvesting, and nocturnal pest detection. Modern vision-based systems, however, rely heavily on large-scale well-annotated image datasets, which remains challenging to obtain for nighttime operation scenarios. To address this, we propose an unsupervised image translation framework that converts daytime plant-row RGB images into near-infrared (NIR) nighttime counterparts without requiring pixel-to-pixel supervision. This enables the direct reuse of daytime semantic labels for training nighttime perception models. In particular, by incorporating a pre-trained Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP) model, the proposed framework is designed to preserve semantic consistency during day-to-night translation. Additionally, a visibility mask is introduced to account for the limited effective range of NIR illumination in nighttime scenes. We conduct comparative evaluations with state-of-the-art image translation baselines and demonstrate higher image qualities, as supported by improved performance in downstream semantic segmentation for nighttime visual navigation. For evaluation, we utilize AgriNight--a novel dataset comprising 428 daytime and 549 nighttime images collected using night-vision-equipped mobile robots in agricultural fields and manually annotated with pixel-wise semantic labels--and introduce it as the first benchmark for nighttime agricultural visual navigation. We also perform real-time autonomous navigation experiments with a physical robot operating at night. The data and code are available at: https://github.com/mamorobel/AgriNight.

</details>


### [265] [Robust In-Hand Manipulation via Priors in Reinforcement Learning and Mechanical Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12105)
*Yifei Chen,Shihan Lu,Ed Colgate,Kevin Lynch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究多指机械手的在手滚动操作，通过强化学习结合物理先验（抓取质量先验和接触几何先验）提升操作鲁棒性，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。论文研究的是机器人多指手的在手操作（in-hand manipulation），属于机器人操作领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化均无任何关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：多指机械手在手滚动操作（in-hand rolling manipulation）；场景：机器人操作；传感器类型：无外部传感（external sensing-free）；目标类别：三个不同物体（具体物体名称摘要未说明）。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体网络结构。采用强化学习（reinforcement learning）框架，引入两种物理先验（physics priors）作为奖励塑形（reward shaping）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 实验设置：多指机械手在四种手掌朝向（palm orientations）下操作三个不同物体。评价指标：旋转效率（rotation efficiency）、抓取稳定性（grasp stability）、扰动抑制能力（disturbance rejection）、sim-to-real迁移效果。

Experiments: 结果表明引入物理先验后，旋转效率、抓取稳定性和扰动抑制能力均有显著提升，且改善了sim-to-real迁移效果。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何可借鉴信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于机器人操作领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: In-hand manipulation without external sensing is challenging due to uncertainties from finger-object contacts and disturbances by gravity. While reinforcement learning has shown promise in learning complex finger gaiting, existing approaches do not prioritize maintaining well-conditioned grasps for sustained manipulation. We introduce two complementary physics priors for robust in-hand rolling: a global grasp-quality prior derived from classical grasp analysis and a local contact-geometry prior based on fingertip curvature. The grasp-quality prior is used as a dense reward-shaping term that encourages well-distributed contacts with improved worst-case wrench resistance. The contact-geometry prior is expressed in the fingertip geometry that mechanically shapes the contact interface toward task-aligned rolling while reducing off-axis drift. We evaluate the effect of these priors on learning in-hand rolling manipulation for a multifingered robotic hand manipulating three different objects at four palm orientations. Results show significant improvement in rotation efficiency, grasp stability, and disturbance rejection, suggesting that physics priors embedded in both learning and fingertip morphology improve task robustness and sim-to-real transfer. An overview video can be found at https://youtu.be/pdd1wHxQnJM?si=dM-U5kiiPTYsk3Pk.

</details>


### [266] [GaitSpan: Growing Humanoid Locomotion from Walking to Running](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12114)
*Kwan-Yee Lin,Zilin Wang,Janelle J. Liu,Stella X. Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GaitSpan框架，将预训练的行走策略扩展为可连续调速的奔跑策略，用于人形机器人步态生成。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究人形机器人步态生成与技能扩展，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：人形机器人步态生成与速度连续控制；场景：仿真与真实地形行走/奔跑；传感器类型：未说明；目标类别：无。

Model architecture: GaitSpan框架包含三个模块：1) rhythm generation（节奏生成）- 多内部时钟调制冻结的行走策略；2) stride shaping（步幅塑造）- 基于弹簧负载倒立摆动力学的物理奖励；3) residual adaptation（残差适应）- 捕获前两者未覆盖的运动细节。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及zero-shot部署到未见过的仿真和真实地形，但这是迁移能力而非模型轻量化部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。提及与multi-expert或imitation from humans的baseline对比，学习更快且步态性能更强。

Experiments: 摘要未提供详细实验结果。仅声称相比baseline学习更快、步态性能更强，且能zero-shot迁移到未见过的仿真和真实地形。

Limitations: 1) 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2) 未涉及任何模型轻量化或边缘部署技术；3) 未提供量化指标（参数量、速度、能耗等）。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。唯一可能的概念启发是"seed skill"思想——将基础能力作为种子进行扩展，类似于在遥感检测中可将轻量基础检测器扩展为多尺度/多类别检测器，但方法论上差异较大。

Abstract: A humanoid that can walk should not relearn locomotion from scratch to jog or run. Yet current approaches often obtain gait diversity by prescribing gait schedules, imitating motion clips, training experts to switch between or distilling skills into one policy. These strategies can produce impressive behaviors, but offer limited flexibility across continuous speed commands, terrains, and morphologies. We study skill growth with GaitSpan, a framework that expands a pretrained, basic walking policy into faster locomotion. It treats walking as a seed skill: reusable motor structure for balance, support, body coordination, and contact transition that can be regenerated at new rhythms, extended into longer/higher strides, and corrected by residual adaptation. This expansion has three aspects: 1) rhythm generation, which modulates the frozen walking policy with multiple internal clocks and learns command-conditioned combinations of the resulting canonical actions; 2) stride shaping, which rewards dynamic locomotion patterns appropriate for higher commanded speeds using a physically grounded objective inspired by spring-loaded inverted pendulum dynamics; and 3) residual adaptation, which captures motion details not accounted for by rhythm generation or stride shaping. GaitSpan is the first to deliver a single command-conditioned humanoid policy that spans walking, jogging, and running-like regimes covering a continuous speed range, transfers across morphologies, and deploys zero-shot on unseen sim-to-sim, and real-world terrains. Compared with baselines either trained with multi-experts or imitation from humans, it learns faster and achieves stronger gait performance.

</details>

*Christopher A. Tucker*

Experiments: 摘要未说明。论文为语料分析性质，未提供定量实验对比。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术细节。论文讨论的是机器人行为脚本的抽象表示，属于机器人控制/软件工程范畴。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。论文不涉及遥感、目标检测或模型轻量化，对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无参考价值。

Abstract: This paper presents a corpus-level analysis of generated behavior diagrams derived from Sony's R-CODE sample distribution for the ERS-111 AIBO. Rather than reading each script in isolation, the study compares named states across the corpus to identify the recurring control vocabulary that structures the sample set. The resulting aggregate shows that many superficially different routines are built from a compact embodied grammar centered on initialization, sensing, iterative action, synchronization, and recovery. In addition to historical analysis, the paper argues that this form of state-based abstraction is useful as an intermediate representation for constructing new encapsulated behavior routines, especially on constrained native robotic systems where deterministic control, direct hardware access, and modular behavioral composition remain important.

</details>


### [268] [More than a Manipulator: Planning Propellant-Free Attitude Maneuvers for Free-Floating Spacecraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12130)
*Harsh G. Bhundiya,Avi Soval,Keenan Albee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究利用机械臂运动实现无推进剂航天器姿态控制，通过轨迹优化解决关节约束与碰撞避免问题，并与反作用轮进行控制能力对比。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦航天器姿态控制中的机械臂利用问题，完全不涉及遥感成像、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上智能处理等核心内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 航天器姿态控制（attitude control）、机械臂轨迹优化（trajectory optimization）、在轨组装与制造（in-space assembly and manufacturing）。不涉及遥感、目标检测、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文采用轨迹优化方法（trajectory optimization），使用内点求解器（interior point solver）求解非线性规划问题（nonlinear program），不涉及深度学习模型。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上部署、边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时推理等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。评价指标为动量包络（momentum envelopes）和力矩包络（torque envelopes），用于与反作用轮阵列（reaction wheel arrays）进行控制能力对比。

Experiments: 在多种不同运动学复杂度与质量特性的航天器-机械臂系统上进行了复杂机动（slew）和消旋（detumble）轨迹的仿真验证。

Limitations: 与遥感目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或检测方法。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于航天器姿态控制/动力学领域，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无交叉。

Abstract: Spacecraft attitude control is traditionally achieved using momentum exchange devices or propellant-consuming thrusters.
